<a id="the-theory-of-becoming--生成变化之论"></a>
# The Theory of Becoming

Begun 9 October 2026 (Asia/Bangkok). Direction: Yongxu Ren. The organization and wording of this chapter are the AI's (Claude), written from:

- Ren's [priority proposal](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-08-theory-of-becoming-priority.md);
- Ren's clarification of [the whole and the manifold](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-09-becoming-whole-generative-manifold.md);
- Ren's [direction of 生生](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-shengsheng-ethical-direction.md) (shēngshēng, “life giving rise to life”);
- Ren's instruction to [unify everything as one theory](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-09-one-theory-unification.md).

Ren's own words are marked as quotations; everything else is the AI's elaboration.

**This chapter is the canonical entry to the theory.** It does three things:

- it organizes the established arguments as one theory;
- it states every premise;
- it links each conclusion to its evidence.

New formal results are stated on one Lean spine (section 9). Earlier chapters remain the record of their own arguments and evidence at their original scope; this chapter replaces only their role as entry points (section 11).

<a id="一句话--in-one-sentence"></a>
## In one sentence

**The Theory of Becoming explains how relations form, persist, transform, degenerate and dissolve within one relational unfolding, and how life, evolution, knowing and evaluation differentiate within it, without first assuming that becoming is good.**

> **Status.**
> - **No axiom.** The theory asserts no axiom, ethical or substantive. Every source declaration of the Lean core has an empty axiom report (section 9).
> - **Laws under local hypotheses.** Each law holds of every world so represented under the local conditions written into it (finiteness, enclosure, listing, decidedness, and any premise it names). No global premise is assumed (section 0).
> - **One condition, stated and not asserted.** The continuation condition, “for anything, its continuation into the future has significance” (`Unrestricted`; Ren proposed it in these words), enters only as the condition of section 6.15. The theory states what follows from it and does not assert it.
> - **Living significance follows immediately from that condition.** Living significance, that every living claim has significance from every standpoint, is the condition restricted to living claims (`g0_of_unrestricted`, `relevance_least`; sections 6.8, 6.15). The condition gives significance to every claim, living or not; it gives no positive valence. Without the condition, the theory characterizes living significance exactly and shows that the represented dynamics do not entail it (section 5).
> - **Necessity is open.** That the whole's determinations are internally related moments of one unfolding, with no added substantive premise, is the open first target (section 8).

When Ren proposed this scope, he wrote:

> when we add axiom, we can define what is good, which is a part of the whole space generative model projects
>
> but, if we count all space, generative, degenerative, we get The theory of evolution, or the theory of becoming/life, which doesn't even require axiom, i think can be done with 0

and clarified:

> the whole thing, the theory of becoming, is deductively necessary.
>
> generative is a part of its manifold

**Two targets are kept apart.**

1. **The deductive necessity of the whole.** Explain the necessary content of Becoming as a whole with no added substantive axiom. This is the open first priority (section 8).
2. **The ethical orientation within the whole.** Explain, through living significance and disclosed practical premises, how 生生 (life in the whole and its continuing generation) acquires normative significance. Under its declared conditions this target is complete (section 6). Living significance is not an axiom: it follows immediately from the continuation condition of section 6.15 (sections 6.8, 6.15), and the theory does not assert that condition.

Neither target substitutes for the other. An absence of ethical axioms is not an absence of substantive premises, and an empty Lean axiom report is not an absence of premises.

<a id="理论的完整陈述--the-theory-stated-in-full"></a>
## The theory stated in full

This section states the theory once as a whole. It starts with the whole and its laws. It then turns to the parts the premises carve, the unity of coming to be and ceasing, and the conditions of further activity. Last come the places of evolution, knowing and evaluation. Items 1–10 use no axiom; they close with [the theory stated whole](#第一部分的总陈述--part-i-stated-whole). For living significance, the normative half of item 10, the content and standing are also stated with no axiom (sections 5–6). Living significance holds under the continuation condition of section 6.15, which the theory does not assert.

Each item is marked with its standing:

- **representation**: how a world is represented. Steps are discrete; amounts are natural numbers; one shared index identifies conditions with relata (section 0).
- **law**: holds of every world so represented, with no global premise; its local conditions are written into the law; checked.
- **theorem**: holds under disclosed premises; checked.
- **condition**: the continuation condition (`Unrestricted`), which enters only as the condition of section 6.15 and is not asserted.
- **reading**: an interpretation of checked content, stated with its argument. **[AI reading, Dn]** marks a reading the AI adopted, recorded with its reasons and what would change it as decision Dn of the [decision record](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/DELEGATED-CHOICES.md); a better argument revises it, and revising it changes no theorem. **[AI reading, open]** marks a reading not yet adopted. The open readings are listed in section 8.4. Ren took D1, D5, D22, D39 and D43 on 18 October 2026 (section 8.3).
- **open**.

Decidedness, that what is counted is decided, is not a commitment of the representation. It is the condition of the laws that count or name a step, written inside them. Classically it always holds; asked of every world, it is excluded middle (`Becoming.Decidedness`). The counting laws of `Becoming.Counting` quantify over worlds whose activity is globally decidable; restated over counted censuses (`Becoming.CensusCounts`, `Becoming.CensusBinding`), every law of that family, under the premises, each selection, where the selected premises bind, under enclosure and in finite worlds, takes no decision procedure and holds for every property of the counts (section 0). All modules are in the pure core of the Lean package Becoming (section 9). The structure of the whole and its parts is in [space-and-its-parts](../results/records/space-and-its-parts.md). The full declaration catalogue and the selection results for items 1–11 are kept verbatim in [the reference catalogue](THE-THEORY-OF-BECOMING-REFERENCE.md).

**I. The whole**

1. **The space.** The whole is every world: relata of one kind, the flow among them and their web of dependence (`World`). Formations and conditions are two roles of one relatum; which condition is which relatum is supplied by the representation's shared index. `World` is not the starting point. The readings in context of one unfolding always form one world, with no gluing (`Contextual.world`). A context-free description exists exactly where those readings glue everywhere (`Contextual.fitsFree_iff`), and `World` is such a description. Labelled processes and frames are readings of the same unfolding: every history is a run of the law it shows, and the gap between law and history is memory (`Becoming.Unfolding`). With the rest of the whole counted as one relatum, every world balances and is one run of the law of exchange (`World.unity`). That balance holds by construction, since the rest relatum is defined as the residual imbalance. The checked content is that a world's own balance is exactly the rest never being active. Ren confirmed that law and history are one unfolding read two ways, and adopted counting the rest as a relatum (14 October 2026). [representation; theorems; other readings: AI reading, D23]

2. **Laws of the whole.** These laws hold of every world with no premise about the unfolding; each carries its own local condition. They are of four kinds. First, identities by definition: arising and ceasing, a dependence forming and dissolving, and coming to stand and ceasing to stand are one change read three ways (`change_readings`). Second, arithmetic identities: over any stretch, the end with its falls equals the start with its rises (`over_stretch`), and Price's equation holds in counting form. Third, consequences of two-valuedness and finiteness: in a finite world whose activity is decided, arising recurs exactly when ceasing does (`World.arising_iff_ceasing`); asked of every finite world without decidedness, this law is equivalent to excluded middle (`World.law_iff_em`). Fourth, laws of one step (`Becoming.Locality`). Beyond what the representation excludes, they exclude no unfolding. They locate the claim about the whole; they are not dynamical content. [law]

3. **Premises carve parts.** Seven premises are each met by some worlds and failed by others (`World.contingent`): activity needing support, balance, only active relata giving, only active relata taking what they draw on, dissipation, embodiment, and a reserve carrying activity. The map of premises drops the reserve, which follows from activity needing support, and adds a census and bounded transmission. These eight are independent: each is failed by a world meeting the other seven (`World.independent`). In `hatch` all eight hold and each binds, where binding means only that the condition has an instance (`World.hatch_binds`). Under each of the 256 selections, the laws of counting worlds with decided activity are of two kinds: the tally alone, or the tally plus nothing arising at a step at which nothing is active (`World.counting_under`); over counted censuses the same holds with no decided activity and for every property of the counts (`World.counting_under_counted`). This independence measures the freedom of `World`'s separately chosen fields, not contingency in becoming. Read off one exchange with the rest counted, balance, only active giving and only active taking hold by reading (`World.withRest`). [theorem]

**II. Coming to be and ceasing in one unfolding**

4. **Within a step, over the long run, and of the whole.** In a balanced flow, across a step at which a family is dissipated on, a member rising comes with another member falling (`Flow.rise_with_fall`). In a finite world whose activity is decided, arising recurring and ceasing recurring are one part. What this needs is a bound on the count of the active, not finiteness (`World.CountedWithin.arising_iff_ceasing`). For pointwise properties, the tally is the whole law of counting (`counting_laws`). The rest theorem covers a balanced, dissipative world with censuses of what is active and of what stands, whose arising is embodied, and which is enclosed around what stands from some step on. In such a world, that it comes to rest cannot be refuted (`World.comes_to_rest_unrefuted`). Naming, for every finite world meeting the eight premises, a step after which nothing arises would give the limited principle of omniscience (`World.lpo_of_finite_ends`). The eight premises decide neither enclosure nor openness. Dissipation here means losing at least one whole unit with every uptake from a closed family, which is stronger than the statistical second law. [theorem]

**III. Conditions of further activity**

5. **Dependence and reserve.** Dependence forms, holds, breaks and dissolves across steps. Where activity needs support, theorems connect ending, transformation and restoration after a loss, and the step that constitutes the break selects among them (`Becoming.Dependence`). A reserve defers the end without escaping it (`Becoming.Reserve`). [theorem]

6. **Renewal, replenishment and giving.** Renewal and consumption belong to runs, are graded by return, and in the renewable process no finite history decides which a run is (`Becoming.Renewal`, `Becoming.Horizon`). Recurring uptake needs replenishment that never stops for good, and more without end needs replenishment and renewers beyond every bound (`Becoming.Flow`). In a balanced flow every rise is given to, so embodied generation is itself replenishment. Take a finite family, without repetition, enclosed from some step on, under balance and dissipation, from which taking recurs. It cannot be that from some step on nothing beyond it feeds it. Takers each within what is given can together exceed it, and that joint excess ends the renewers and what depends on them at such a need (`Becoming.Continuation`). A part's contribution to what can form next is the comparison with and without it: closing needs a net draw, opening a net give (`Becoming.Admission`). [theorem]

7. **Identity and wholes.** Identity across steps is carried by the pattern of names; labels carry nothing (`Becoming.Identity`). The census laws hold under every continuation in two halves. Without splits, recurring ceasing needs recurring arising; without merges, under a bounded count, recurring arising needs recurring ceasing (`Census.balanceFrom`). Across one step, more forces arising exactly when no formation continues as two (`forces_iff_noSplit`). A whole is active exactly while some part is. Consider a whole active from some step on whose listed parts each end. It cannot be that from some step on none of its parts arises: it persists only through their generation (`Becoming.Wholes`). [theorem; the reading of identity is representational]

**IV. Differentiation**

8. **Evolution.** Descent is defined by taking part, giving and coming to stand. Where only active formations give and activity needs support, only supported formations leave descendants. A transmitted need then exceeds its condition's level at the giving step by at most the bound of transmission; with bound zero it was met there (`Slack`). Activity in between sets later selection (`Becoming.Evolution`). Transmission is a premise. Price's equation is an arithmetic identity, and the selection term proper is not stated. The theory of evolution's own programme keeps its scope (section 2). [theorem]

9. **Knowing.** A formation can test only a claim that differs in what couples into it (`Becoming.Testing`). What an act does is fixed by the record only where the configuration returns, so experiment needs a model of what is irrelevant (`Becoming.Experiment`). A judgment under uncertainty is a one-step inquiry; a family of readings within what it reads cannot hold every reading at once; context-free claims are a commitment that what quantum states show does not meet. Where the number of states is bounded by N, the prefix of 2N − 1 steps fixes the course through the map, and induction becomes deduction (`Becoming.Identification`). This holds for every process exactly where its runs do not part through the map (`Becoming.Parting`). A record shows no weight; counting shows that most courses show the weights, but that the course which happens is typical is not derived (`Becoming.Typicality`). Section 3 gives the account of knowledge. [theorem]

10. **Evaluation.** Structurally, the part in which every event is living (not undermining, in the stepwise reading) crosses arising and ceasing and coincides with neither (`World.living_crosses`). Normatively, living significance says that living and further generation within the whole have basic positive normative significance; at the level of claims, every living claim has significance from every standpoint (`Valuation.Significant`). It is a condition, not an axiom. On a connected whole, it is exactly a seed with two neutralities (`Valuation.significant_iff`). Where every living claim is held, it is exactly self-significance at every standpoint with standpoint neutrality (`Valuation.significant_iff_held`). The represented dynamics contain no significance, so they do not entail living significance either way (`Web.significance_undetermined`). Along dependence, the center is not derived (`Clearing.center_not_derived`). Under the condition of section 6.15, living significance follows immediately. [living significance: a condition, characterized by theorems with no axiom; readings: AI reading, D1, D6, D11]

**V. Standing**

11. **What is complete and what is not.** Every item has a checked statement or a disclosed premise. The core imports nothing outside itself, and every source declaration has an empty axiom report. This is the completeness of a formal statement in the sense of the four checkable criteria of [plan section 9](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-UNIFICATION-PLAN.md#9-走向完整的生成之论--toward-a-complete-theory-of-becoming), not a proof of necessity. The laws hold of worlds so represented; whether every becoming can be so represented is part of the warrant still needed. One deduction remains open: that the whole's determinations are internally related moments of one unfolding, with no added substantive axiom ([foundation section 5](BECOMING-FOUNDATION.md#5-completion-audit-and-next-central-inference)). The readings of 生 and 生生 are AI readings, which Ren took on 18 October 2026 (生生 read as generation generating generation, its direction compared by widening, D1); a better argument still revises them (section 8.3). Whether actual unfoldings meet the premises is empirical. Section 8 sorts what is open into four classes. [open]

<a id="0-前提卡--premise-card"></a>
## 0. Premise card

Every premise is named and visible. In Lean, each is a definition or a hypothesis, never an axiom. Every source declaration of the spine has an empty axiom report. The generated `injEq` lemmas on `propext` in the core closure are not source declarations, and no source declaration uses them (section 9). The card has six blocks. The first two are not premises about the unfolding: they are how it is represented, and the local conditions that individual laws carry. The World premises carve parts of the space. The rest bear on application, ethics and practice.

**A. Commitments of the representation**

| Commitment | Content | Lean | Standing |
|---|---|---|---|
| Proof language | Lean 4.29.1 kernel. The core compiles from Lean's prelude alone: the checker strips `GenerativeCore`'s `import Std` and recompiles the core closure without it. Constructive: no excluded middle, choice, propositional or function extensionality is assumed. A law that needs a classical principle names it as a hypothesis (excluded middle, LPO, WLPO, LLPO, Markov's principle, double-negation elimination, dependent choice). | `check_becoming.py` (prelude-only step) | method commitment |
| Discrete steps, one shared index | Every relatum's activity, level and exchange is indexed by one sequence of natural-number steps. There is thus one shared order of steps; a definite causal order beyond it is not assumed (section 1, “The order of change”). For exchanges acting locally the index is read off the relata's own courses: it orders, beyond them, only exchanges sharing no relatum, which changes no configuration (section 1, “The step index read off the relata's own courses”). | `World`, `Web`, `Flow` | representation |
| Natural-number amounts | Levels, needs, takes and gives are natural numbers. A positive amount is at least one unit. Several laws depend on this, among them dissipation's least loss, the doubling of loops, and novelty needing gain. | `Web.level`, `Flow.take`, `Flow.give` | representation; finer units in `Becoming.Units`, `Becoming.Resolution` |
| One kind of relatum | A world is relata of one kind, with a flow (`present`, `take`, `give`) and a web (`level`, `active`, `requires`, `need`, `draws`). Formations and conditions are two roles of one relatum. | `World`, `World.ofRoles`, `Becoming.Roles` | representation |
| The shared index | Which condition is which relatum is given by the representation, not derived from the dynamics. | `relabel` | representation premise |
| Requirements as thresholds | What a formation requires at a step, and how much, is given in advance for every relatum of the world's type. Support is meeting every requirement: `need ≤ level`. | `Web.requires`, `Web.need`, `Web.Supported` | representation |
| Context-free claims (frames only) | A frame reads what is shown of a claim as independent of what is shown with it. What quantum states show does not meet this. | `Becoming.Context` | commitment of the frame reading (section 3) |
| Identity across steps | Which formation continues which is fixed by the naming. | `Becoming.Identity` | representation (section 1) |
| Pointwise properties | Counting laws are stated for properties of counts that respect their values at each step, since the core does not assume function extensionality. Over counted censuses no such condition is needed: counts taken as relations are unique where they exist (`Counts.unique`, `Becoming.CensusCounts`). | `Pointwise` | condition of the decided counting laws |

**B. Local conditions carried by individual laws**

| Condition | Content | Lean | Where it is used |
|---|---|---|---|
| Finite | Finitely many relata, each listed once | `World.Finite` | finite-world laws; a finite world has a census where its activity is decided, and every step of it is enclosed |
| Enclosed | Across a step, the exchange of the formations taking part lies within some finite family without repetition; around a family or around what stands, the same with that family included | `Flow.Enclosed`, `Flow.Around`, `AroundStanding` | rest, change-bound and drawing-in laws |
| Listed | What holds at each step is listed once; a family is without repetition | `Listed`, `Web.Census`, `Distinct` | census, counting and loop laws (for example “every condition listed”) |
| Decided | What a law counts or names is decided where the law needs it. A count exists exactly where what it counts is decided (`counts_iff_decided`); listing once is decided identity (`listsOnce_iff_decided`). The counting laws of `Becoming.Counting` quantify over worlds with `[∀ t x, Decidable (w.web.active t x)]`, which is global decidability of activity. Over counted censuses (`Becoming.CensusCounts`) the laws under the premises, each selection, enclosure and finite worlds take none: a ceasing or coming count across a step exists exactly where the listed relata's being listed at the other end is weakly decided (`World.ceasings_exist_iff_listed`). Over counted censuses the laws where the premises bind take none either (`Becoming.CensusBinding`). | `Becoming.Decidedness`, `Becoming.Listing` | counting, recurrence and naming-a-step laws |

**C. The World premises**

The eight premises of the map, each a definition about a `World`:

| Premise | Content | Lean |
|---|---|---|
| Activity needing support | A relatum is active only where the levels meet everything it then requires | `World.Dependent` (`Web.Dependent`) |
| Balance | Every change of a level is exactly what is taken from it and given to it | `World.Balanced` (`Flow.Balances`) |
| Only active relata give | Whatever gives across a step is active there | `World.Acting` (`Flow.Acting`) |
| Only active relata take, and only from what they draw on | Whatever takes across a step is active there and draws on what it takes from | `World.Taking` (`Flow.Taking`) |
| Dissipation | On every family without repetition closed across a step, each formation taking part gives it no more than it takes from it, and at least one unit less when it takes | `World.Dissipative` (`Flow.Dissipative`, `Flow.DissipatesOn`) |
| Embodiment | Every relatum stands as a condition exactly while it is active | `World.Embodied` |
| A census | The relata active at each step are listed, each once | `World.Counted` |
| Bounded transmission | Descent transmits every dependence within one bound | `World.Transmitting` |

Two weaker forms are also used: a reserve carrying activity (`World.Carried`) and embodied arising (`World.ArisingEmbodied`).

- *Contingency.* Seven premises are each met by some worlds and not by others (`World.contingent`): the first six above and the reserve.
- *Implications.* Activity needing support gives a reserve, the zero reserve. Embodiment gives embodied arising. A finite world with decided activity has a census. With only active taking and dissipation, only active relata give across an enclosed step (`World.acting_across`).
- *Independence.* The eight are independent: each is failed by a world meeting the other seven (`World.independent`, `World.none_follows`). The independence is relative to the eight; finiteness, enclosure and decidedness are not among them.
- *Binding.* In `hatch` all eight hold and each binds (`World.hatch_binds`). Binding means only that a premise's condition has an instance. No finite world binds the census, since the census binds only where the world is not finite (`World.Finite.not_binds`); this holds by definition.
- *What the independence measures.* It measures the freedom of `World`'s separately chosen fields, not contingency in becoming. With activity and drawing read off one exchange and the rest of the whole counted, balance, only active giving and only active taking hold by reading. For a law of exchange, so does activity needing support (`World.withRest`, `ExchangeLaw.readWorld_glue`). Records: [map-of-premises](../results/records/map-of-premises.md), [premises-read-off-the-exchange](../results/records/premises-read-off-the-exchange.md).

One discipline is stated, interaction: one draws on a condition and the other depends on it at a positive level. It gives connection in the declared class. |
| Correspondence | Readings of `living` | Three are defined: whole-run (active and not consuming in that run), stepwise (active and not undermining), and relation level. All are relative to a run. They read activity compatible with others' dependence, not further generation. | `Conditions.valuation`, `Web.valuation`, `Web.relations` | A disclosed choice of reading (section 6.3). No comparison uses it. 生生 is read as generation generating generation, its direction compared by widening what can form [AI reading, D1]; the `living` readings do not include further generation, so they do not read 生生. |
| Correspondence | Held | Every living claim lies in some standpoint's own region. This always holds on the stepwise reading where each formation's own region is its own events. Lives no one holds are reached through formed relations. That holding is decided is `Valuation.significant_iff_reached`'s own condition, localized. | `Valuation.Held` | The AI's formal reading of “what wants its continuation” in Ren's proposal of 10 October 2026. Self-significance is the reading of “wanting” [AI reading, D11] (section 6.7). |

**E. Ethics**

| Premise | Content | Lean | Standing |
|---|---|---|---|
| **The continuation condition** (section 6.15) | For anything, its continuation at every step has significance from every standpoint. Ren proposed it in these words: “for anything, its continuation into the future has significance”. The boundary of the significance of continuation is neutral. | `Unrestricted` (`∀ p t f, sig p t f`) | **Not asserted.** The theory states it only as the condition of section 6.15. As a condition (section 6.8): living significance, the seed, self-significance, relational neutrality and standpoint neutrality follow from it by restriction. On level paths, C with “better” read as retaining and adding what has significance follows. Every use of living significance is discharged by it, and it is living significance for anything (section 6.12); so living significance needs no axiom of its own. It gives every claim significance, not a positive valence. Without it, route (b) reaches the self and what the self depends on, and reaches the whole only where the whole is one feeding closure (section 6.9). [AI reading, D16] |
| **Living significance** (a condition, not an axiom) | Living and further generation within the whole have basic positive significance; at the level of claims, every living claim has significance from every standpoint | `G0`, `Valuation.Significant` | The theory states it as a condition, with no axiom. Its content is characterized exactly by the next three rows (`Valuation.significant_iff`, `Valuation.significant_iff_held`). The represented dynamics do not entail it (`Web.significance_undetermined`, section 5). Under the continuation condition it follows immediately (sections 6.8, 6.15). Positive relevance is read from significance: it is the significance of realized living content (`Frame.relevance`), and every use of living significance is discharged through it (section 6.12). |
| Self-significance | A standpoint's own living, further-generating content has positive significance for it | `SelfGood` → `Seed` | A normative premise. A reading of Ren's restatement proposal of 10 October 2026: self-significance is wanting one's own continuation, and every point of wanting is a center (section 6.7) [AI reading, D11]. It concerns living claims only; on the stepwise reading it already filters for not harming. Under the continuation condition it holds everywhere, with no wanting needed (section 6.8). Self-significance is not the seed: a seed is some living claim significant from some standpoint, which any position holding self-significance with its own living content supplies. |
| Relational neutrality | Significance passes along formed relations to living content (the patient's side) | `transmits` | A boundary-neutrality premise; follows from the continuation condition (section 6.8) |
| Standpoint neutrality | The significance of living content holds for every standpoint (the standpoint's side) | `shares` | A boundary-neutrality premise: what has significance from any center has significance from every standpoint, and no center is special. Where every living claim is held, living significance is exactly self-significance at every standpoint with standpoint neutrality (section 6.7). It is not the only completion: on a connected whole where every standpoint holds a living claim, self-significance with relational neutrality also gives living significance. Standpoint neutrality is adopted as the reading of the condition in Ren's restatement proposal of 10 October 2026 [AI reading, D11]; section 6.8 reads that condition as unrestricted significance, from which standpoint neutrality follows. |
| A | Access, together with securing positive content from one's own standpoint over one's scope, gives a supporting reason (the addressee's side) | `articulates` | An externalist articulation premise. Under the continuation condition it must be read on living content; read on bare significance it would give a reason for any new content (section 6.12) [AI reading, D16]. |

**F. Practice**

| Premise | Content | Lean | Standing |
|---|---|---|---|
| C | An extension with no loss that realizes more living content is better | `Comparison` | A comparison premise. Courses are compared on possibilities, for the reasons of section 6.12. With the condition, this is the condition's own comparison: it ranks the absence of an overdrawing formation above its presence and withholds no event. On level paths, with “better” read as retaining and adding what has significance, C follows from the continuation condition (section 6.8). With “better” read as widening what can form, C's no-loss extension is exactly widening; that is definitional (`Web.paths_comparison_widens`, section 6.13), and that widening is better is the open warrant. |
| L | Lifting the comparison of outcomes to responses with many outcomes; the conservative default is “every outcome is better than every outcome” | `LiftBetter` | a substantive commitment (section 6.5) |
| The practical commitments (J1–J4) | Complete comparison and retained losses; priority of basic conditions; bounded responsibility; legitimate feasible choice | `PracticalJudgment`, `BetterAt` (periphery; standard Lean axioms) | further commitments proposed by the AI, disclosed as conditions of the practical laws |
| FR | Finite responsiveness. Within an independently specified family of recoverable basic losses of one kind, a fixed graver loss does not keep sufficient priority over arbitrarily many fixed lesser basic protections by its gravity alone. Where support, burdens and history persist as scale grows, some finite scale of joint protection gains sufficient grounds for choice. | `Becoming.LossMatching` (the formal content of strict loss matching, not the warrant) | An additional, revisable comparison commitment proposed by the AI, with affirmative defense and counterreview. It is not a theorem and not an axiom. Record: [finite-aggregation-within-restorable-basic-losses](../results/records/finite-aggregation-within-restorable-basic-losses.md). |

Other named commitments of the practical line (BC1, BC2, LC, LR, CP, NP) are stated with their records in section 6.

**Not assumed.** The theory does not assume:

- that becoming is good;
- inevitable improvement or universal growth;
- a fixed common utility;
- independent owners of value;
- that every change is generative;
- a definite global causal order at every interface. The representation has one shared index of steps (block A); what the laws use of it is the order of change (section 1).

<a id="第一部分不用公理--part-i-without-axiom"></a>
## The theory without axiom (sections 1–6)

Ren proposed this order: “let's aim for the strongest claim without axiom, as the first part, and add one axiom, as the following part, the no axiom part is most important and elegant” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-zero-axiom-part-and-names.md)). Sections 1–6 use no axiom, ethical or substantive. The theorems section 6 cites use the continuation condition at most as a hypothesis, at named points (the inventory is in section 6), and section 6.15 states what follows under it without asserting it. Premises appear only as hypotheses of conditional theorems; the commitments of the representation are disclosed separately (the theory stated in full, section 0). These sections close with [the theory stated whole](#第一部分的总陈述--part-i-stated-whole).

<a id="1-生成同一展开--becoming-one-unfolding"></a>
## 1. Becoming: one unfolding

**Starting point.** The starting point is one unfolding of relations: relata read with one kind, with what each takes, gives, stands at, needs and draws on at each step. `World` is its description free of context. That description exists where the readings of the unfolding glue (item 1 of the theory stated in full; “The whole and its parts” below). The process is one reading of the unfolding: a configuration becomes another through an admissible change. The move can be read within the configuration, and what the law leaves open is the next move. The frame is another reading: which claims are realized at each configuration. Both readings return to the one unfolding. That is a result of `Becoming.Unfolding`, not Ren's wording (below).

- A `Frame` adds only which claims are realized at each configuration. Claims describe differentiated realizations and their relations; they are not independent owners.
- A `Run` is an infinite actual execution.
- `Reach` is a finite admissible path.
- An `Invariant` is a property preserved by every step.
- `Novel` is a claim of a given reading realized now and not realized before.

All are defined through the core's `Trace`, so they connect directly with the core's composition, product and transport theorems ([Becoming.Frame](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Frame.lean)).

**One unfolding and its readings.** Ren asked of the core's first two layers “is this dualistic?” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-12-relational-starting-point.md)). They posit no subject and object, but their form takes four splits as given: a configuration and a move drawn from an alphabet of its own; a law prior to the history it governs; claims read off a configuration; and configurations prior to their relations. `World` takes none of them. The four splits are stated here from the world's side, with no premise ([Becoming.Labelled](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Labelled.lean), and [Becoming.Unfolding](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Unfolding.lean) for the readings that also use laws of the world or of the process).

- *The move is part of the unfolding.* Read within the configuration, the move changes no run. The law leaves the next move open exactly where it was determined before and has one move at each step (`withMove_determined_iff`). Where the move is formed within, by a policy on the configuration, a determined law stays determined, and runs from one configuration agree at every step (`closedBy_runs_agree`). Between parts, a shared move is a relation: once each forgets the move, two forking parts can take different ways at once (`Fork.label_couples`). A world's move is its relata's exchange. A world balances exactly where its levels run by the law of exchange with the exchange as the move (`World.balances_iff_exchange`, which holds by definition). The exchange fixes what stands from a beginning; what stands does not fix the exchange (`exchange_runs_agree`, `Circulation`).
- *A history fixes the law it shows.* Every history is a run of the law it shows, the least law it is a run of. That law is determined exactly where the history never takes the same configuration to two places by the same move, and exactly then some determined law admits the history (`shown_le_iff`, `shown_determined_iff`, `exists_law_iff`). What a law given before the history adds are the moves not taken, which the record does not fix. The correspondence is not an identity. A law can have a step and no history (`DeadEnd.no_history`). Where a history revisits, the law it shows admits histories it never took (`Splice.spliced`). Keeping the history admits only that history (`Run.keepHistory_shown`), at the price that it never returns (`Becoming.Recurrence`).
- *A claim is a relatum.* Along a run, a frame is a world whose relata are claims, each active exactly where it is realized. Novelty is arising, so the laws of activity hold of claims. With configurations as claims, each run is a world that fixes the run (`Frame.world`, `Frame.novel_iff_arises`, `Frame.claims_recur_iff`, `Frame.world_fixes_run`).
- *A configuration is how it relates.* Configurations related by a bisimulation are alike: they realize the same claims and go by the same moves to alike configurations. Likeness is an equivalence, and alike configurations pass the same tests (`Alike`, `Alike.tests`). A description makes sense exactly where it identifies the alike configurations. It is then unique in what it identifies, and where the frame is determined up to likeness it carries a law (`identifiesAlike_iff`, `IdentifiesAlike.unique`, `IdentifiesAlike.carries`). The bridge proves that the periphery's relational identity is this one (`RelationalDynamics`, 6 October 2026, and the branching tests of `ExplanatoryBranching`; periphery, the latter with standard Lean axioms). Determinacy as equality depends on naming. The two successors of twins are alike in every relation, and the twins' frame is alike to a determined frame without being determined itself (`Twins.alike_one`). Determinacy up to likeness passes along likeness (`DeterminedAt.transfer`). Under the law of exchange, levels are alike exactly where they are equal at every relatum (`exchange_alike_iff`).

*Readings.* Ren confirmed one reading: law and history are one unfolding read two ways, the law read off the unfolding rather than set over it. He wrote: “yes, i do agree with your reading, there are no two realities” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-12-no-two-realities.md)). The other readings are the AI's [AI reading, D23]:

- labelled processes and frames are readings of the unfolding, proved in both directions;
- action is not a second kind of cause beside the unfolding;
- the gap between law and history is memory;
- knowing lies within the unfolding;
- the names of configurations carry nothing.

The earlier reading that the starting point is `World` is superseded by Ren's question of 14 October 2026 and the relation-first re-founding: `World` is the reading where the readings glue.

Not derived:

- the shared index of steps;
- whether a move is formed within the unfolding, and by what;
- which law a formation takes its history to show;
- that configurations passing the same tests are alike;
- that the theorems stated with `Determined` hold up to likeness.

`GenerativeCore` is unchanged. This is a reading with theorems in both directions and a bridge, not another foundation. The import tree follows the reading ([study §180](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#180-where-the-spines-import-tree-starts-and-a-plan-to-start-it-at-the-one-unfolding-w-step-1)): it starts from the records of the unfolding (`Becoming.World`, which imports nothing and ties no record to another), these readings are stated in `Becoming.Labelled` right after `Becoming.Frame`, and the trunk from `Becoming.Renewal` to `Becoming.Space` does not import the frame. Records: [process-and-frame-as-readings](../results/records/process-and-frame-as-readings.md), [configurations-alike-by-relation](../results/records/configurations-alike-by-relation.md). Study: [section 91](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#91-一个展开与它的读法过程与框架从世界一侧陈述p69).

**What makes a pair a pair.** Ren asked “what makes a pair, a pair?” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-12-framing-and-pairs.md)). Take a whole of two parts, the combinations that occur. It is rectangular when whatever occurs with one part occurs with every item of the other. Then describing the two parts separately gives the whole back exactly (`separation_faithful_iff`, [Becoming.Description](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Description.lean)). A whole that is not rectangular is a relation between the parts. Describing them separately loses exactly that relation, and the lost relation reappears as extra combinations (`separation_admits_extra`). Three parts can be related as a whole while every two are free, so describing every pair still loses it (`whole_not_in_pairs`).

A relation forms, lasts and ends within the joint law.

- A law that moves each part by itself, lossy or not, never relates unrelated parts (`Rectangular.image_of_separate`).
- A law that copies one part into the other relates them (`copy_relates`).
- Once formed, a relation lasts under every law that moves each part by itself and merges nothing that occurs (`relation_persists`).

So a relation ends only by loss within what occurs, as by forgetting a part (`forgetting_unrelates`), or by acting on both parts together. For example, a coupling relates two parts and, acting again, unrelates them, losing nothing either way (`coupling_relates_and_unrelates`). The coupling is a bijection; what ends the relation is its acting on both parts together.

Under a law, a pair is a split that makes sense. Where position changes by velocity and only position is read, the split that makes sense is the pair of position and velocity; neither alone makes sense (`Motion.neither_alone`). Where nothing passes, position alone makes sense, and the pair is one (`Motion.still_identifiesAlike`).

*Readings* [AI reading, D33]. Ren asked for “What makes two a pair, then, is relation” to be dug deeper, and agreed with the reply that followed. Two are a pair by relation: in what they can be together, or in how they go on together. The members are read off the pair; the pair is not assembled from them. A pair is the least split, not the least relation. Record: [separation-loses-the-relation](../results/records/separation-loses-the-relation.md). Study: [section 94](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#94-描述保留什么失去什么分离关系的生灭与有意义的切分p70).

**What histories fix.** Under Ren's “move towards 100%” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-12-move-towards-100.md)), the dead ends and splices of `Becoming.Unfolding` are the whole law of law and history, with no premise ([Becoming.Histories](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Histories.lean)). The law a family of histories shows takes as its steps the steps its histories take, and it is the least law admitting each of them. A family is the family of some law's histories exactly where it holds its splices, every sequence made of its own steps. It is the family of a determined law's histories exactly where it is also consistent (`family_iff`, `determined_family_iff`). Two laws admit the same histories exactly where they have the same live steps, the steps some history takes (`same_histories_iff`). So laws up to their dead ends correspond one to one with families holding their splices. With histories that stop, the dead end disappears: two laws have the same finite histories exactly where they have the same steps (`same_finite_histories_iff`). Where both frames are determined up to likeness at every configuration, configurations are alike exactly where they pass the same tests (`alike_iff_sameTests`). Without that condition this can fail: a frame choosing after its first move and a frame choosing at it pass the same tests and are not alike (`Choosing.not_alike`). `family_iff` and `same_finite_histories_iff` are close to definitional; `alike_iff_sameTests` is a Hennessy–Milner type result.

*Readings* [AI reading, D32]. A law read off histories is a family of histories holding its splices. What a law set over the unfolding can add are dead ends that no history shows. The splice is memory's absence. Not derived: whether an actual unfolding's histories stop. In the core, branching tests fix likeness constructively on finite frames with decided steps and claims (`alike_iff_sameBranchingTests`, `Becoming.Likeness`). Records: [histories-fix-law-up-to-dead-ends](../results/records/histories-fix-law-up-to-dead-ends.md), [tests-fix-likeness-where-determined](../results/records/tests-fix-likeness-where-determined.md). Study: [section 98](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#98-历史固定什么律与历史的完备对应检验固定相同的条件p76).

**Formation, persistence, transformation, degeneration and dissolution are relational changes along runs, not five stages.** One activity can make a continuation actually possible while destroying another condition. A form that persists need not keep its former capacity to act. The following results establish these distinctions at their scopes.

- **Endpoint possibilities.** Along an actual trajectory, every endpoint possibility is kept exactly where a return exists; otherwise there is a constructive witness of loss ([endpoint-loss-and-return](../results/records/endpoint-loss-and-return.md); an earlier module outside the spine, with no axioms).
- **Current capacity and eventual reachability.** The two differ: preparation changes what can be jointly realized within one step ([effective-capacity-and-eventual-reachability](../results/records/effective-capacity-and-eventual-reachability.md); periphery; standard Lean axioms).
- **The whole space of possibilities.** Pooling the steps of a family of processes keeps exactly the union of their endpoint histories exactly where that union is closed under joining at a shared boundary. Containing every outcome is not the condition under which outcomes can be spliced at will ([becoming-context-coherence](../results/records/becoming-context-coherence.md); periphery; standard Lean axioms).
- **Joint activity.** The joint activity can change while every part's local state stays the same ([joint-correlation-reduced-dynamics](../results/records/joint-correlation-reduced-dynamics.md); periphery; standard Lean axioms). A local distinction can be lost and still be recovered within a retained joint relation ([control-coherence-loss](../results/records/control-coherence-loss.md); periphery; standard Lean axioms).
- **Law and trajectory.** The law of steps and the endpoint trajectories determine each other ([trace-law-equivalence](../results/records/trace-law-equivalence.md)); maps preserving steps transport trajectories ([finite-trace-transport](../results/records/finite-trace-transport.md)). Both are earlier modules outside the spine, with no axioms.
- **Laws with no premise.** The following hold of every unfolding, with no premise about it ([Becoming.Change](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Change.lean), [coming-and-ceasing-keep-pace](../results/records/coming-and-ceasing-keep-pace.md)). A determination comes to hold or ceases to hold across a step. The arising and ceasing of a formation, the forming and dissolving of a dependence, and a condition coming to stand and ceasing to stand are this one change, read of activity, dependence and standing. Over any stretch, an amount at the end with the falls between equals the amount at the start with the rises between. Amounts do not go below zero, so in a finite family recurring falls need recurring rises: in every unfolding, finitely many conditions that keep falling keep rising; with amounts bounded, recurring rises also need recurring falls. So in a finite family coming to be and ceasing to be keep pace. Over any stretch, the members holding at the end with those that ceased between are exactly the members holding at the start with those that came between; the difference is at most the number of members, and each recurs exactly when the other does. With finitely many formations and activity decided, arising recurs exactly when ceasing does: some formation arises at arbitrarily late steps exactly where some formation ceases at arbitrarily late steps. A condition coming to stand recurs exactly where its ceasing to stand recurs, with no premise at all. Finiteness does the work: endless novelty can arise without ceasing (`Accretion`), and endless initial members can cease without arising (`Attrition`). Every law below states the premise it adds, and the premises carve parts of the whole space. The method commitments of the representation are disclosed separately: discrete steps and natural-number amounts (section 0). Decidedness when counting is not among them: it is a condition the counting laws carry (section 0, block B).
- **Change of dependence.** Dependence itself belongs to the unfolding. Which conditions a formation depends on, and how much it needs, can form, hold, break and dissolve across steps, and each change happens at some step ([Becoming.Dependence](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Dependence.lean)). Under the premise that a formation is active only where supported (`Dependent`, which excludes buffering and lag), theorems connect three outcomes of a loss. They neither exhaust nor exclude one another. Continuing with a dependence no weaker than before means the condition was restored. Staying active after a loss with no return means the dependence was transformed. With the dependence unchanged, the formation ends. In one process, from one configuration, after the same dependence breaks at the same step (read by the convention of the step's start), all three outcomes can occur. The reading of the break does not fix the successor configuration; the step that constitutes the break selects the outcome ([becoming-dependence-dynamics](../results/records/becoming-dependence-dynamics.md)). `Dependent` is exactly the zero reserve. A reserve does not rise while the formation is unsupported, and falls while it is active then. With a reserve, a formation can be active beyond its support at most as many times as its reserve allows. Endings after a loss, and coextinction, are deferred, not escaped. Under a decaying reserve, coextinction runs down a chain of dependence of any length, a finite chain such as a food chain included, within a bound at each link. Without decay, with excluded middle as a hypothesis, every relatum on the chain still ends, with no bound. The reserve can be the level of a held condition, and the same unfolding can also be read as dependence transformed onto the held condition ([Becoming.Reserve](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Reserve.lean), [reserve-defers-ending](../results/records/reserve-defers-ending.md)). These are definitions and constraints within the representation, not a proof of the one-sentence statement.
- **Identity across steps.** Which formation at one step continues which formation at the step before is fixed by the representation's naming; the relations at each step do not fix it ([Becoming.Identity](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Identity.lean), [identity-across-steps](../results/records/identity-across-steps.md)).
  - *What naming carries.* Renaming preserves the readings defined by relations; five were checked. Labels carry nothing; the pattern of names across steps carries identity. The relations at each step, each condition's class, the number of takers and “more” do not depend on identity across steps. A formation's class over a run, the tenure and undermining of a dependence, the difference between transformation and “one ends and another forms”, arising, and unceasing arising do. Changing only who continues whom changes all of these (`Turnover`). Arising produced by naming alone can meet every premise of continued generation below (`TideFlip`).
  - *Re-identification against an old naming.* A new naming that keeps the names of continued activity and continued dependence across steps, relative to the old naming, creates no arising and removes no undermining. It can still join a formation that ends to one that begins at or after that step: succession. A lineage arises unceasingly when its members are named apart, and has no arising when they are named as one (`Lineage`). Where a successor continues a predecessor, drawing after the predecessor's loss undermines at every step (`Succession`). Keeping continuity both ways keeps arising and undermining exactly and excludes succession within one step (`no_join`), but succession across dormancy still changes a formation's class (`Dormancy`).
  - *Wholes.* A lineage and its members are two levels of one unfolding. One relation of parts and whole is stated by the whole: a whole is active exactly while some part is. This is a coincidence of activity, not constitution; a lineage is the whole of its members ([Becoming.Wholes](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Wholes.lean), [whole-persists-through-generation](../results/records/whole-persists-through-generation.md)). Take a whole active at every step from some step on. Suppose its parts are brief (never active at two steps d apart), or its parts end with finitely many active at once (all activity ends and the active parts can be listed). Then its parts cannot stop arising: from no step on is it true that none arises. The whole persists through the generation of its parts. With brief parts this can be the same parts arising again (`Layered`); with ending parts it needs ever new parts (`needs_new`): from no step on can parts outside any given list stop arising. Neither is growth in number. With a part that never ends, or with infinitely many held in reserve at once, this fails (`Immortal`, `Countless`); and parts arising unceasingly do not by themselves keep the whole going (`Gaps`). The Lean conclusions here are negations of universal statements, not named steps of arising.
  - *Succession and 生生不息.* Succession is read through the formed relations of continuation, and 生生不息 (shēngshēng bùxī, “life giving rise to life without ceasing”) is read at the level of the whole: 不息 as arising that recurs through renewal, and 生生不息 as 生生 going on with more still able to form, so that a whole which only returns, copying forms it has had, has 不息 but not 生生不息 [AI reading, D5].
  - *More forcing arising.* Under a census, with `present` listing each formation once and only active formations taking (`Taking`), the unceasing arising forced by “more” holds under every one-to-one naming (`More.unceasing_reidentified`). Under every continuation in which no formation continues as two, more also forces arising (`More.unceasingFrom`; merges allowed). No split is sufficient, not necessary: one split need not defeat arising (`Fork`), and reading new formations as budding defeats the conclusion (`Budding`). Without a census, no split is not enough (`Dormant`). Across one step the condition is exact: whatever the formations before and after, more forces arising exactly when no formation continues as two. Under every continuation with no split, arising is forced exactly when there are more after the step than before (`forces_iff_noSplit`, `forced_iff_more`).
  - *The census under continuation.* The census laws do not depend on the identity names fix. Under a continuation, a formation ceases where no active formation after the step continues it. Under a census, without splits, the formations active after the step with those that ceased are at most those active before with those that arose; without merges, at least; with neither, exactly. So without splits recurring ceasing needs recurring arising, and without merges under a bounded count the converse. Take one unfolding with two formations at each step. Read as each formation continuing only itself, it has one arising and one ceasing at each step. Read as budding, ceasing recurs and nothing ever arises; read as joining, arising recurs and nothing ever ceases (`Census.balanceFrom`, `Tandem`, [census-balance-under-continuation](../results/records/census-balance-under-continuation.md)).
- **Two roles of one relatum.** Formations and conditions are two roles of one relatum, not two kinds of being ([Becoming.Roles](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Roles.lean), [life-as-condition-of-life](../results/records/life-as-condition-of-life.md)). The two-kind representation embeds in the one-kind reading in which no formation is a condition. Read so, every listed reading and the connection of valuations stay the same; a formation that was ever active there is neither embodied (standing as a condition exactly while it is active) nor bears on itself. The one-kind reading needs one more correspondence: which condition is which formation. It is supplied by the shared index, a premise of the representation, and the two-kind reading cannot check it (`relabel`). Embodiment and bearing on oneself are readings under this correspondence. Where activity needs support (`Dependent`), a formation depending on an embodied relatum is active only while that relatum is. Activity passes along chains of dependence, and a relatum's ending ends every formation whose chain of dependence reaches it (`coextinct`). The arising of an embodied relatum is the rise of its level as a condition, so generation is itself replenishment ([generation-is-replenishment](../results/records/generation-is-replenishment.md)).

**Attraction.** The account of a general cause–effect system lists path dependence and attractors. On the spine they are one law, holding of every determined law with no premise ([Becoming.Attraction](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Attraction.lean)). A region the law never leaves is absorbing (`Law.Absorbing`). A configuration outside it whose orbit enters it is drawn into it (`Law.DrawnInto`), and what is drawn in is its basin. What is drawn in never comes back (`Law.DrawnInto.never_back`). A configuration is drawn into some absorbing region exactly where it does not return (`Law.drawn_iff_not_returns`): what lies ahead of it is an absorbing region it enters at the first step. The future narrows. What can be had n steps on is an absorbing region, and every possibility lost by step n is drawn into it (`Law.reachable_absorbing`, `Law.lost_drawn`). Of two configurations a law merges, not both return, with no finiteness needed (`Law.Merges.not_both_return`), so not both escape being drawn. Which one is drawn needs deciding, and is stated only in double-negation form (`Law.merge_draws`). Within finitely many configurations, attraction is merging. A drawn configuration means the law merges and loses a possibility; with equality decidable, the two are equivalent; and the region of returning configurations draws in everything else (`Law.Drawn.not_lossless`, `Law.lossless_iff_none_drawn`, `Law.drawnInto_returning`). An open world separates them: counting up draws in without merging, and counting down draws in while keeping every possibility (`Law.succ_drawn_lossless`, `Law.pred_drawn_keeps`). Under every process, once inside an invariant region, configurations outside it can no longer be reached (`Invariant.locks_out`). A region is invariant exactly where every move keeps it, and leaving it needs a move that does not (`invariant_iff_every_move`, `Run.leaving_needs`, `Exit`).

*Readings* [AI reading, D25]. Lock-in is not returning. The future narrows, and an attractor is the unfolding narrowing its own future. In `Becoming.Capture`, the outcome a rule holds is an absorbing region in the rule. The main law is close to definitional; the mathematics reused is the eventual periodicity of orbits on finite sets. Which regions actual unfoldings enter, and how deep or wide a basin is, are not derived. Record: [attraction-is-not-returning](../results/records/attraction-is-not-returning.md). Study: [section 89](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#89-吸引被吸入者不再回来每个律恰吸入不返回者p67).

**When the course branches.** The laws of `Becoming.Recurrence` and `Becoming.Attraction` are stated for determined laws. A process of the spine fixes only which steps are admissible, so its course can branch. Here they are stated for every process, with no premise ([Becoming.Branching](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Branching.lean)). A configuration returns exactly where its own course can have it again at the next step. Every configuration returns exactly where every region the process never leaves keeps every possibility of its own (`returns_iff_fed_ahead`, `returns_iff_keepsWithin`). The three-way equivalence of `Becoming.Recurrence` comes apart under branching. Keeping every possibility does not give returning (`Transit.keeps_not_returns`); a merge need not lose a possibility (`OneWay.merge_keeps`); and merging nothing gives returning along each run within finitely many configurations (`Branching.Lossless.returns`). Every process necessarily draws in exactly what does not return (`necessarilyDrawn_iff_not_returns`). Being possibly drawn in is losing oneself on some course, and a configuration possibly drawn in can return on another course (`possiblyDrawn_iff`, `OneWay.possibly_not_necessarily`). Determinacy is the named case of determinacy up to likeness (`determined_iff_named`). The main theorems of `Becoming.Experiment` and `Becoming.Mechanism` on how configurations relate hold up to likeness. What needs equality is an intervention on one of two alike configurations, a past no claim tells apart and a description no claim tells apart (`Twins.retarget_breaks`, `keepHistory_new_named`, `carries_unregistered`). The rule region of `Becoming.Capture` is absorbing, and perturbing the state alone is drawn back into what the rule holds (`Law.rule_absorbing`, `Captured.state_perturbation_drawn`). The laws along runs hold for every configuration that can keep moving, with no run given. Within finitely many configurations a run is a course into a return, with no decision and no selection. Asked of every process, it is exactly dependent choice (`Becoming.Onward`, below).

*Readings* [AI reading, D32]. Not derived: being drawn in within a bound; a measure on branching courses. Records: [returning-where-the-course-branches](../results/records/returning-where-the-course-branches.md), [necessarily-drawn-is-not-returning](../results/records/necessarily-drawn-is-not-returning.md), [determinacy-up-to-likeness](../results/records/determinacy-up-to-likeness.md). Study: [section 99](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#99-路径分支时返回被吸入与相同之下的确定p81).

**Where a run goes on.** The laws along runs of `Becoming.Branching` take the run as given. Building a run from a configuration that can move takes a continuation at every step, and taking one at every step is a selection the core does not assume. This states where a run goes on with nothing selected, with no premise beyond what each theorem names ([Becoming.Onward](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Onward.lean)). Within finitely many configurations, a course as long as the list repeats a configuration, and the repeated configuration returns. From where a course leads to a return, a run goes round the return, preceded by the course. So a run starts exactly where a course as long as the list starts, exactly where courses of every length start, and exactly where a course leads to a return (`run_iff_continues`, `run_iff_unbounded`, `run_iff_returning`). No decision and no selection are used. Where the successors are listed, whether a run starts is decided (`run_decided`). Where branching is infinite, courses of every length give no run (`fan_no_run`). A family keeps moving where from each member some step leads to a member (`KeepsMoving`). Within finitely many configurations, a run starts at each member and stays in the family (`KeepsMoving.run`). So, within finitely many configurations, the laws along runs hold for every such configuration with no run given. Merging nothing, a configuration returns exactly where a course as long as the list starts from it (`lossless_returns_iff`). Nothing rises within the family, and a rise needs a merge (`lossless_no_rise`, `rise_needs_merge`). A configuration with courses of every length that never returns needs openness (`lossless_unlisted`). Where the configurations are not bounded, consider a test that recognizes, among enumerated moves, a step staying in the family, one wherever there is such a step. It gives a run built by search, as data, with no selection (`Finds`, `search`, `searchedRun`). Where the steps and the family are decided and the steps' moves are enumerated, testing each move is such a test (`decidedTest_finds`). Asked of every process, a run from every member of every family that keeps moving is exactly dependent choice (`runs_iff_selection`, `DependentSelection`). This equivalence is close to definitional, since a run is a dependent-choice sequence; the principle is Bernays's.

*Readings* [AI reading, D43]. Within finitely many configurations, whatever goes on without end does so by coming round. The course itself shows where it returns, nothing is selected, and going on is read off the unfolding. Where the configurations are not bounded, going on without end is given where the moves can be searched. Asked of every process, that every configuration that can keep moving starts a run is exactly dependent choice. Not derived: whether the core gives dependent choice; runs along unbounded configurations where the branching is finite and nothing is searched (given in `Becoming.Lasting` through `EitherThroughout` and a selection); the laws along runs where nothing lists the configurations. Record: [where-a-run-goes-on](../results/records/where-a-run-goes-on.md).

**What listing once commits to.** A list can be listed once exactly where identity among its members is decided (`listsOnce_iff_decided`); its number of members is unique and exists exactly then. Laws in refutation form need no such premise. Fixing every list's number up to relational indiscernibility, in every world, is equivalent to excluded middle (`World.numbers_indiscernible_iff_em`). Reading by occurrence keeps amounts and local laws but does not track occurrences across steps. Open: the connection to likeness of configurations (`Alike`), and corollaries for the other 24 uses classified as premise-free. The earlier inventory of `Distinct` uses is a historical snapshot (at commit 193c5511). Records: [listing-once-is-decided-identity](../results/records/listing-once-is-decided-identity.md), [relata-counted-up-to-indiscernibility](../results/records/relata-counted-up-to-indiscernibility.md). Lean: [Becoming.Listing](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Listing.lean). Study: [section 101](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#101-列出一次所承诺的p77).

**The order of change.** Stuttering keeps a world's premises, the totals over stretches and recurrence; rates and reserves counted in steps depend on the reading. Take a given footprint containing the exchanges. Commuting neighbouring exchanges whose footprints are disjoint keeps every premise, and it reaches exactly the renumberings that keep the partial order of joined exchanges (`Footprinted.sorts_iff`, `Footprinted.converse`). Removing idle steps keeps the premises, the censuses and the totals. The footprint is supplied, and removing idle steps uses the moving steps it is given. The least footprint and censuses along reorderings are given in the next paragraph but one, where idleness and apartness are decided. A Foata normal form and continuous time remain open. Records: [only-the-order-of-change-registers](../results/records/only-the-order-of-change-registers.md), [exchanges-commute-unless-joined](../results/records/exchanges-commute-unless-joined.md), [order-of-exchanges-is-the-invariant](../results/records/order-of-exchanges-is-the-invariant.md). Lean: [Becoming.Order](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Order.lean). Study: [section 102](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#102-变化的次序p78).

**All laws of one step, and of families chosen step by step.** A pattern of one step is realized by a world exactly where it obeys the law of every step and the law of each named local condition (`OneStep.every_iff`, `OneStep.laws`). Nonzero taking needs a taker, and nonzero giving a giver. Givers, capacity and the increments of activity are characterized exactly. Runs on one family add chaining across steps. For families chosen step by step, running down adds continuity counted at positive levels (`OneStep.step_runsDown_iff`), strictly weaker than tracking positions. Completeness is for the stated patterns and conditions. Counts of the active, and capacity and activity over whole runs, remain open. The laws stated earlier in `Becoming.Locality` missed two laws of one step, which the witnesses show. Records: [laws-of-one-step-complete](../results/records/laws-of-one-step-complete.md), [one-step-missing-laws](../results/records/one-step-missing-laws.md), [runs-on-families-chosen-step-by-step](../results/records/runs-on-families-chosen-step-by-step.md). Lean: [Becoming.OneStep](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/OneStep.lean). Study: [section 103](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#103-一步与逐步选择家族的全部规律p79).

**Weighted histories and their losses.** Given weights with their laws, and finite lists of moves and configurations, the sum of histories over a finite number of steps is exactly the stepwise transfer (`Weights.sum_courses`). Instances of possibility are the core's traces, and counting keeps possibility. Through a description with representatives, exact preservation at every number of steps is exactly one-step lumpability (`Weights.lumped_iff`). Truncation, cancellation, unkept memory and loops each have explicit boundary witnesses. Not derived: actual weights, amplitudes with phase, convergence of infinite sums, and recovery of memory for general weights. The laws of the weights themselves are explicit structure fields. Records: [sum-over-histories-exact](../results/records/sum-over-histories-exact.md), [losses-of-the-sum-over-histories](../results/records/losses-of-the-sum-over-histories.md). Lean: [Becoming.Weighted](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Weighted.lean). Study: [section 104](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#104-加权历史及其损失p82).

**The exact conditions and boundary of transmission.** Transmission is exactly inheritance with a bound on need (`Flow.transmitsWithin_iff`). Where no dependence was held before, inheritance is the law of forming; the general split into forming and holding uses decidedness. Dropping one dependence of a giver keeps the other premises and breaks binding transmission (`World.drop_but`). Under the named conditions (finiteness, balance, dissipation, embodiment and support), a reading of drawing, with drawing being depending, gives transmission (`World.transmitting_of_drawn`). This does not derive transmission from the other core premises alone. The link is sufficient only; unbounded levels and the formation of new dependences remain open. Records: [transmission-is-inheritance-and-need](../results/records/transmission-is-inheritance-and-need.md), [transmission-independent-where-it-binds](../results/records/transmission-independent-where-it-binds.md). Lean: [Becoming.Transmission](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Transmission.lean). Study: [section 105](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#105-传递的精确条件与边界p84).

**Fed without any source recurring.** `Successive.relay` is a balanced, dissipative flow in which only active formations give. Its pool is fed at every step, while each source takes part and is active at its own step only. So continuing to be fed needs no source formation to recur. This advances [continuing-self-fed-from-beyond](../results/records/continuing-self-fed-from-beyond.md) and is not a separate result. Each source is still needed at its own step. Not shown: that the same world stays fed after a source is removed, and that the sources form from the flow. Lean: [Becoming.Succession](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Succession.lean). Study: [section 106](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#106-没有哪个来源复现的供给p85).

**The order of exchanges: normal form, census and least footprint.** Take a symmetric joining relation, a finite stretch, and exchanges named by natural numbers. An arrangement is reachable exactly where it orders each joined pair as the original does. Given a decision that distinct exchanges in the stretch are not joined, there is a lexicographically least, idempotent normal form; arrangements are reachable from each other exactly where their normal forms agree. A natural-number-valued complete invariant on a finite stretch needs exactly that decision. Every reordering with its two sides split preserves the existence of activity and standing censuses in both directions; arbitrary commutation preserves it forward. With idleness decided at every step and relatum, there is a least containing footprint, whose joining and order are contained in those of every containing footprint. With equality of relata decidable, a least Boolean containing footprint exists exactly where idleness is decided. The normal form depends on names and on a finite stretch; no unnamed, Foata-layer or unbounded normal form is given. The needed decision is not derived for actual worlds, and no concrete world with undecidable idleness is given. The sides of a reordering remain supplied, and the correspondence with continuous time remains open. [AI reading, D30.] Records: [normal-forms-need-decided-apartness](../results/records/normal-forms-need-decided-apartness.md), [least-footprint-where-idleness-decided](../results/records/least-footprint-where-idleness-decided.md); the census result joins [exchanges-commute-unless-joined](../results/records/exchanges-commute-unless-joined.md). Lean: [Becoming.OrderNormal](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/OrderNormal.lean). Study: [section 109](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#109-交换的次序正规形普查与最小足迹p88).

**The step index read off the relata's own courses.** The order of change starts from a world given with one shared index of steps. The converse starts from relations with no index given ([Becoming.Interleavings](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Interleavings.lean)). Exchanges act locally: each leaves the relata it does not touch as they were, and changes a relatum it touches as the states of the relata it touches determine (`LocalLaw`). What each relatum undergoes is its own course, the exchanges touching it in order (`ownCourse`), and an index is a list interleaving those courses (`Interleaves`).
- With the relata listed, two lists giving every relatum the same own course reach the same configuration, and so do two traces of the exchange process (`run_of_courses`, `trace_of_courses`, `trace_of_interleavings`). Exchanges sharing no relatum commute (`act_comm`, `trace_swap`).
- With exchanges told apart, a scheduler takes at each stage an exchange heading the remaining course of every relatum it touches. It completes, within as many stages as the courses have exchanges, exactly where some interleaving exists, that is exactly where some trace takes every relatum through its given course (`schedule_iff`, `traced_iff`).
- The own courses fix the interleaving exactly where every exchange touches a relatum and every two exchanges are the same or share one (`unique_iff`, `traced_unique_iff`).

Not shown: separate footprints for what an exchange reads and what it changes; courses without end; a join by definition to the footprint of `Becoming.Order`; whether every becoming has a local law (§8.5). *Reading* [AI reading, D43]. The shared index of steps is a reading of the relations, not their ground. Beyond the own courses it adds only an order between exchanges sharing no relatum, which changes no configuration.
