Where every two exchanges share a relatum the index is derived from the relations; elsewhere it is a commitment of the representation, a choice among interleavings that the configurations do not read. Record: [the-step-index-from-own-courses](../results/records/the-step-index-from-own-courses.md). Study: [section 169](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#169-the-step-index-read-off-the-relatas-own-courses-r1).

**Whole and manifold.** The claim of deductive necessity concerns Becoming as a whole. Generativity is a part of it, not a condition every change must meet. Degeneration, ending and mixed change are all within the same unfolding and need not be renamed generation. See [the foundation's scope](BECOMING-FOUNDATION.md#whole-and-manifold) and [how relations change](THEORY-OF-BECOMING.md).

**The whole and its parts.** The space is every world: relata read with one kind, their flow and their web ([Becoming.Space](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Space.lean), [space-and-its-parts](../results/records/space-and-its-parts.md)). The laws with no premise hold of every world.

- *Premises carve proper parts.* Seven premises (activity needing support, balance, only active relata giving, only active relata taking what they draw on, dissipation, embodiment, and a reserve carrying activity) are each met by some worlds and not by others. So the premises carve proper parts; they are not hidden laws. The map of premises replaces the reserve with a census and bounded transmission, giving eight independent premises. Which premises follow from which, and the independence of the remaining eight, are in [the map of premises](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Premises.lean) ([map-of-premises](../results/records/map-of-premises.md)).
- *Counting where the counts exist.* A count is a relation [AI reading, D43], existing exactly where what it counts is decided ([Becoming.CensusCounts](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/CensusCounts.lean), [counting-where-the-counts-exist](../results/records/counting-where-the-counts-exist.md)). Across a step a ceasing count exists exactly where, for each relatum listed before the step, whether it is listed after the step is weakly decided; a coming count likewise (`World.ceasings_exist_iff_listed`, `World.comings_exist_iff_listed`). Wherever the counts of a census exist they are a tally with no decidedness. Under each selection of the premises, under enclosure and in finite worlds, the laws of counting hold of them for every property of the counts (`World.counting_under_counted`, `World.enclosure_bounds_change_counted`, `World.finite_counting_counted`). Where a procedure decides activity, its marks are these counts (`World.countsOf_decided`), so the decided laws follow. The laws where the selected premises bind hold of counted censuses too, with what binding adds: something active, something arising, and both at one step, each where the selection gives it (`World.binding_counting_under_counted` and its finite and enclosed forms, [Becoming.CensusBinding](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/CensusBinding.lean)).
- *Arising and ceasing are one part in finite worlds.* In a finite world whose activity is decided, arising recurs exactly when ceasing does, so they are one part. Only with infinitely many relata do they separate (`Accretion`, `Attrition`), and only where the count of the simultaneously active grows without bound: with at most B active at arbitrarily late steps they recur together even among infinitely many relata ([bounded-census-keeps-pace](../results/records/bounded-census-keeps-pace.md)). Asked of every finite world without decidedness, the law is equivalent to excluded middle (`law_decides`, `law_iff_em`).
- *Living crosses arising and ceasing.* The part in which every event is living crosses arising and ceasing. Among finite worlds, arising recurring or not, and ceasing recurring or not, each occur both with every event living and with some event not living (`shuttle`, `grazing`, `mutualism`, `drawdown`; `living_crosses_ceasing`). Two worlds with the same activity can differ only in whether each side draws on its own level (`shuttle`, `selfDrawing`). But `selfDrawing` fails to be living only under the stepwise reading's verdict on self-drawing at an ending. That verdict is a theorem of the stepwise reading, and no comparison uses it [AI reading, D6].
- *Rest.* In a finite, balanced, dissipative world whose arising is embodied, neither arising nor ceasing recurs, and that the world comes to rest cannot be refuted; no decision procedure is used (`World.rests_unrefuted`). Each premise is needed. The witness without balance exchanges at every step, so its dissipation is not vacuous (`leaking`).
- *Reading.* The author reads this as one structure of Ren's “count all space, generative, degenerative” (an AI argument): laws belong to the whole, premises to parts, and living is one part, not coinciding with arising. Arising recurring and ceasing recurring are the reading of activity. Ren confirmed its English names, arising and ceasing; the Chinese names 生起 and 止息 are the AI's rendering ([words](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-09-arising-ceasing-names.md)). The proposal's “generative” and “degenerative” name a part's widening and narrowing of what can form, not the activity reading [AI reading, D2]. On 9 October 2026 Ren said “generative和degenerate虽然都在一个becoming model上，但是他们不overlap吧” (“generative and degenerate are both on one becoming model, but they don't overlap, do they?”; [words](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-09-renewing-consuming-without-axioms.md)). So the activity reading, on which the two coincide in finite worlds, is not what is meant. Read as relations, renewing and consuming formations do not overlap (`Web.renewing_not_consuming`); given the definitions, this is immediate.
- *What brings rest.* What brings a world to rest is not that the world is finite. It is finitely many standing and finitely many active at each step, with enclosure around what stands from some step on. In a world that so comes to rest, not unboundedly many relata ever stand or act; finiteness is exceeded only by relata that never stand or act (`rest_few`). A balanced, dissipative world with censuses of what is active and of what stands, whose arising is embodied, enclosed around what stands at every step from some step on: that it comes to rest cannot be refuted. Finite worlds, and worlds whose relata are all embodied, are two cases of this rest theorem. Under a census, recurring ceasing needs recurring arising, and not conversely ([ceasing-needs-arising](../results/records/ceasing-needs-arising.md)). Both censuses are needed (`attrition`; `throughput` and `embodiedThroughput` are enclosed at every step and keep arising). In such a world the exchange does not recur either. That needs only the census of what stands, balance, dissipation and enclosure; without any one of the four the exchange can recur (`exchange_not_recurring`, `exchange_needs`).
- *Openness.* So in a world with a census whose relata are all embodied, balanced and dissipative, arising without end needs steps that are not enclosed. The eight premises decide neither enclosure nor openness, and openness with the eight premises does not give arising without end. `exposed` and the mutualists meet the eight; `holding` is never enclosed and never arises; `hatch`, in which every premise binds, comes to rest; and a queue closed once, replacing one member at every step, keeps arising while every premise binds ([Becoming.Openness](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Openness.lean), [Becoming.Counting](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Counting.lean), [continued-change-needs-openness](../results/records/continued-change-needs-openness.md)).
- *Limits of the rest theorem.* Dissipation here is a least loss of one whole unit per uptake from a closed family, which is stronger than the statistical second law. A finite, enclosed, lossless world that never comes to rest exists (`Passing.passes`). At a refining unit, rest fails unless the refinement is counted (`Becoming.Resolution`). The rest theorem is a well-founded descent on natural numbers.

None of this shows that the whole needs no axiom.

<a id="2-组织更新与演化--organization-renewal-and-evolution"></a>
## 2. Organization, renewal and evolution

**Renewal and consumption form one classification, and it belongs to runs.** Read through observables of conditions, every run of every process is one relational unfolding ([Becoming.Renewal](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Renewal.lean)).

- **Renewing.** A formation is renewing when every condition it depends on or draws on keeps supporting every formation that depends on that condition.
- **Consuming.** A formation is consuming when it draws on a condition from which some dependent permanently loses support from some step on.
- The two classes exclude each other but do not exhaust the cases: fluctuating cases are neither ([relational-renewal-classification](../results/records/relational-renewal-classification.md); an earlier module outside the spine, with no axioms). On the spine, that a renewing formation is not consuming follows directly from the definitions (`Web.renewing_not_consuming`).

**One principle bounds continuation without renewal: an observable that never rises from some step on cannot keep falling.** In Lean: given a rank that does not rise from step T on, it is not the case that the rank falls at steps as late as one likes (`falls_not_recurring`). Three bounds are cases of it:

- depletion: where every step lowers a reserve, there is no run;
- recurrent renewal by a natural-number rank: where every step that does not renew lowers the rank, renewal recurs at arbitrarily late steps;
- drawing without renewal: the drawer is not active at arbitrarily late steps.

**The class belongs to the run, not to a configuration or to the formation's own trajectory.** In one process, from one starting configuration, the focal formation is active at every step. In one run it stays within renewal; in another it consumes the sustaining formation's condition, and the sustaining formation then ends (`Renewable.class_belongs_to_the_run`). So persistence, success and growth do not certify renewal (`own_trajectory_does_not_decide`, [RelationalRenewal](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/deeper/RelationalRenewal.lean); an earlier module outside the spine, with no axioms).

**The stepwise reading.** The classification can also be read step by step. An event (a formation at a step) lies within renewal when its step keeps every dependence on the conditions it takes part in (`RenewingAt`). Every active step of a formation renewing over the whole run lies within renewal. A formation can also have every one of its active steps within renewal and still not be renewing over the run.

As the stepwise reading of `living`, the working reading is “active and not undermining” [AI reading, D10]. An event undermines when it draws on a condition at a step after which the condition is below both the present need of a dependence on it and a need that dependence had met at some step of its unbroken tenure (`MetAbove`). Drawing at the step at which some dependence breaks (breaking) is one kind of undermining. This reading:

- counts the broken formation's own events, since it does not draw;
- does not make a drawer undermine through a dependence that was never met at its need during its tenure, even where the need falls (`NewNeed.never_met_not_undermining`, `FallingNeed.never_met_not_undermining`). Being below only a need that has grown to a level never met is not undermining (`RisingNeed.grown_need_not_met`). After a need grows, drawing that takes the level below a need once met undermines without breaking (`Stunted.stunting_undermines`). A dependence interrupted for one step starts a new stretch (`Gap.lapse_starts_afresh`); whether dormancy or migration is represented as a continuing dependence is a duty of the application;
- withholds drawing that continues after a loss. Such drawing no longer breaks anything; in `Renewable` it keeps the condition from coming back (`drawing_after_loss`);
- has a cost: it reads joint participation in an unrecovered shortfall, not the effect of the drawing. `draws` carries no amount, so drawing exactly the renewed amount, or drawing that changes no level at all, is also withheld (`Pool.within_share_undermines`, `Pinned.undermining_without_effect`).

It differs from the whole-run reading in both directions. Drawing before an escalation is counted stepwise while the whole run counts it as consuming. Drawing at the step of a break during a brief dip is not counted stepwise, while the condition later recovers and the whole run counts no consumption ([becoming-stepwise-reading](../results/records/becoming-stepwise-reading.md); the reviews and corrections are in [the study](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md)).

**Return grades renewal.** Between renewal and consumption lie grades by the time of return: kept at every step; returning within a bounded period; returning eventually without bound; never returning. Consumption is exactly some dependence never returning (`consumed_iff`, which unfolds the definition). A seasonal condition renews within a period of two steps. Recovery can slow without bound while the condition is never consumed. Renewal implies renewal within a period of one step; the converse fails (`LateMet`). The reading at the level of periods counts a brief dip under a period of two steps, and withholds an escalation for every period ([becoming-return-grades](../results/records/becoming-return-grades.md)).

**No finite history decides the classification, in the process `Renewable`.** For every horizon, `Renewable` has two runs that agree completely before the horizon: at every step each draws beyond renewal, and every event lies within renewal; after it one renews and the other consumes. So any reading decided by a finite prefix that counts every renewing run of this process counts some consuming run ([becoming-finite-horizon](../results/records/becoming-finite-horizon.md)). The same holds in a law with feedback that gates support. The strength of the conclusion comes from “counts every renewing run”: that renewing run also draws beyond renewal before the horizon, and the whole-run reading counts those draws (`whole_run_includes_overdraw`). A reading by amount (taking no more than is renewed) is decided by finite histories and withholds every overdraw (`amount_reading`). Judging the whole outcome of a stretch of drawing needs inquiry and deliberation about how the run continues, not only observation of the past. The result is shown for this process, not for every process.

**Joint consumption has no single consumer.** Two formations each lie within renewal, yet together consume a shared condition, and both then end. The class belongs to their joint relation (`jointly_consuming`).

- **Feedback.** In a family of laws in which renewal depends on the renewed condition, a loss beyond the threshold of support does not come back within the law (`no_return_below_support`).
- **Repair.** A supported route of contribution can repair the sustaining relation ([supported-renewal-recovery](../results/records/supported-renewal-recovery.md); periphery; standard Lean axioms).
- **Substitution.** In the same family of laws, a consumer that can substitute for the condition continues by transforming its dependence. It is still consuming under the whole-run reading (almost by definition), and the sustaining formation ends. The stepwise reading counts it before the sustaining formation loses support and withholds it from then until it switches (`stepwise_includes_substitution`, `slow_withheld_two_steps`). Its own persistence does not certify renewal ([substitution-preserves-consumption](../results/records/substitution-preserves-consumption.md)).
- **A narrow law class.** In one class of wear laws, renewal and consumption are dynamically distinguishable ([renewing-consuming-formation](../results/records/renewing-consuming-formation.md); periphery; standard Lean axioms).

**Continued generation.** The readings above all read “no loss”. As the author's reading of the second 生 (shēng, “life, arising”) in 生生, a formation arises across a step when it is inactive before the step and active after it. Generation is unceasing when some formation arises at arbitrarily late steps; the same formation may arise again ([Becoming.Generation](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Generation.lean), [unceasing-generation-needs-renewal](../results/records/unceasing-generation-needs-renewal.md)).

- *Depletion.* An event depletes a finite family of conditions when, at each of its steps at which no condition of the family rises, one of them falls. This says only that some condition of the family changes at each of the event's steps; it says nothing about the event's relation to that condition (`depletes_iff`, `Tide.unrelated_depletion`).
- *Renewal recurs.* Where arising depletes the family, unceasing arising makes some condition of the family rise net at arbitrarily late steps. Where every rise is made by some formation's renewal, renewing activity recurs at arbitrarily late steps.
- *Balanced flows.* A balanced flow gives a form that needs no net rise. In a balanced flow, positive uptake at arbitrarily late steps rules out that replenishment is zero at every step from some step on (`uptake_needs_replenishment`). This sees replenishment that comes with the drawing. Takers arising unceasingly are one case, and then replenishment is not zero from any step on (`replenishment_recurs`); a constant population of takers is another (`Steady`). In this form arising works only through the uptake it brings.
- *Strictly stronger.* A tide the moon renews rises at arbitrarily late steps with nothing arising. So under these premises unceasing arising is strictly stronger than recurring renewal (`Tide.renewal_without_arising`).
- *Unrenewed conditions.* Arising conditioned on conditions that are not renewed does not recur. Substitution among finitely many unrenewed conditions defers the end without escaping it.
- *Dissipation.* Where an amount never rises and every arising lowers it (dissipation), generation is not unceasing (`dissipation_generation_not_recurring`). Without dissipation, a closed cycle with a constant total can sustain unceasing arising (`Recycle.closed_unceasing`).
- *No finite part alone.* The author argues from this that under dissipation no finite part can be unceasing on its own. For uptake in a flow, what is proved is its literal form: a finite family on which taking recurs is not closed from any step on, and enclosed around, it must draw in from beyond (see “More” below). Where dissipation holds vacuously, a finite part can bear it alone, and the openness is met by an exchange unrelated to it (`SprayShuttle`).

Only the “unceasing … 生” of Ren's words “不断创造更多生” (“unceasingly creating more 生”) is stated here. The scope of the second 生 is what can form [AI reading, D3].

**More.** Read conditions as amounts: at each step the formations taking part take from and give to conditions ([Becoming.Flow](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Flow.lean), [Becoming.Population](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Population.lean), [more-needs-renewers](../results/records/more-needs-renewers.md)).

- *Balance.* Where a flow balances, every change in a condition's level is exactly the difference of taking and giving. Over one step or a stretch, a family's total level with what is taken equals its total before with what is replenished: depletion balances replenishment. So every rise is given (`Balances.rise_given`), and the premise above, “every rise is made by some formation's renewal”, becomes a theorem.
- *Reading “more”.* The author reads “more” as: for every number, from some step on at least that many formations take from a finite family of conditions. Amounts are natural numbers and each taker takes at least one unit at each step. So such generation rules out any bound on the family's replenishment holding from some step on (`More.replenishment_unbounded`). Where each formation gives at most a fixed amount per step, the renewers have no eventual bound either (`More.renewers_unbounded`). They grow in an averaged sense, yet can be absent at infinitely many steps (`Pulse.pulsed`).
- *More forces arising.* Under a census of active formations, with the flow's `present` listing each formation once at each step and only active formations taking, more without end implies unceasing arising (`More.unceasing`). The expansion and the cycle meet all these premises (`Expansion.counted`, `Cycle.counted`). An oscillator arising again and again meets these premises without being more, so the implication is strict. Without the census, with repetition in `present`, or with takers that are never active, more without end can have no arising at all (`Dormant`, `Repeated`, `Inactive`).
- *A hierarchy.* The replenishment needed thus forms a strict hierarchy. Recurring uptake needs replenishment that is not zero from any step on; more without end needs replenishment with no eventual bound. The renewers can be the takers themselves (`Cycle.closed_more`).
- *Dissipation and openness.* Dissipation: at each step, on each finite family without repetition that is closed at that step, the formations taking part give to it no more than they take from it, and less where they take. Under dissipation, a finite family on which taking recurs, and every finite family containing it, is not closed from any step on (`Dissipative.opens`). The opening exchange may be unrelated to the family. Where the family is enclosed around (the exchange at each step lies within finitely many conditions containing it), it draws in: it is not the case that, from some step on, every formation giving to it takes only from within it (`taking_draws_in`). With finitely many conditions in all, taking does not recur ([Becoming.Dissipation](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Dissipation.lean), [no-finite-part-bears-taking](../results/records/no-finite-part-bears-taking.md)).
- *Rise and fall in one exchange.* In a balanced flow every fall is taken from. At a step dissipated on, the members of a family fall by at least as much as they rise, and by one more for each formation taking. So one member rising (an embodied arising included) comes with another member falling at the same step: the two are two sides of one exchange. A rise with nothing falling, in a family without repetition, shows that the family is not closed at that step. Without dissipation this fails (`Brood.rise_without_fall`). For events whose family is dissipated on and taken from at each step, the depletion used as a premise above follows ([rising-and-falling-in-one-exchange](../results/records/rising-and-falling-in-one-exchange.md)). A fall is neither ceasing nor undermining.
- *Where dissipation binds.* Dissipation constrains only steps at which some finite family is closed. At such steps giving needs uptake (`giving_needs_uptake`). Where no step is closed it is vacuous, and a renewer can give while taking nothing (`Spray`).
- *Reading.* The author reads this as one structure of 生生: more uptake needs replenishment with no eventual bound, given by formations within the unfolding. Under dissipation, recurring uptake, whether from more without end, from generation or from maintenance, is borne by an unfolding that goes beyond every finite part; sustaining a single taker already is (`Lone`). Rises in possibility, diversity and complexity are other readings of “more” and are not stated here.

**Two roles of one relatum, and arising through giving.** Read with one kind of relatum ([Becoming.Roles](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Roles.lean), [life-as-condition-of-life](../results/records/life-as-condition-of-life.md)), a formation can be a condition of others and of itself. Which condition is which formation is supplied by the representation's shared index (`relabel`).

- *Arising is given.* In a balanced flow where only active formations give, an embodied relatum arises only as another active formation gives to it (`Embodied.arises_given`). The formation that gives need not be embodied; it can be a source depending on nothing (`SourceSeed`). Under the representation premise that only embodied relata give, the relatum that gives is embodied too (`Embodied.arises_from_embodied`), and it can still be a source depending on nothing (`EmbodiedSource`).
- *Reading.* Reading embodied relata as 生 [AI reading, D4], the author reads this as one formal content of the relation between the two 生 of Ren's words (an AI argument). It does not replace Ren's words. Generations of formations can arise without end, each given by the one before (`Brood`).
- *Generation is replenishment.* An embodied relatum comes to stand as a condition as it arises, and is given at least its new level. So generation is itself replenishment: where embodied members of a family arise at arbitrarily late steps, the family's replenishment recurs, whatever is taken ([generation-is-replenishment](../results/records/generation-is-replenishment.md)).
- *The requirement follows the rise of level, not activity.* In a balanced flow every rise is given to (`Balances.rise_given`). Coming to stand as a condition is the rise from zero (`Web.ComesToStand`), and a condition can simply keep rising (`Growing`). A seed can be given its level while dormant, and later arise with nothing given (`Provisioned`). Waking from dormancy is not coming to stand and is not bound by the requirement (`Sleeper`). Without support, under a reserve, waking does not recur at arbitrarily late steps; the reserve defers the end without escaping it ([reserve-defers-ending](../results/records/reserve-defers-ending.md)).
- *Dissipation and generation.* A formation giving to a family dissipated on takes from it. So under dissipation each arising of an embodied member in a closed family without repetition lowers the family's stock. Where embodied generation recurs in a finite family, the family is not closed from any step on (`generation_opens`); the opening exchange may be unrelated to the family (`SprayShuttle`). Enclosed around, it draws in: not every giver to it takes only from within it from some step on (`generation_draws_in`). With finitely many relata in all, embodied generation does not recur (`generation_not_recurring`). The physical premise set in `dissipation_generation_not_recurring` under “Continued generation” above is derived, for arisings of embodied members in a closed family, from balance, dissipation and embodiment. Without dissipation (`Shuttle`) or without embodiment (`Flicker`) these conclusions fail. A transfer that burns fresh fuel meets every premise (`Throughput`).
- *Mutualists.* Drawing on a relatum that bears on itself interacts with each of its active events. Where a bee and a flower each depend on themselves and on the other, they are connected with no third condition, and living significance there is exactly the seed with two neutral terms. Where each depends only on the other, neither valuation relates them, though both are embodied: embodiment itself does not enter the valuation (`Mutualism`, `Mutualism.apart_embodied`).
- *Drawing at an ending.* Drawing on an embodied relatum that bears on itself, at the step at which it ends, is breaking, whatever ends it, and so is not living (`Embodied.ending_breaks`, `Predation`). This includes a bystanding drawer, a natural ending and the relatum's own last event (`Wilting`). This is the stepwise reading's verdict. It is not a valuation of the drawer; comparison is by possibilities [AI reading, D6].

**Evolution names specific mechanisms; it is not a synonym for every change.** Transmission, reconstruction, variation and selection each have their own relations and must each be explained through them ([the theory of evolution](THE-THEORY-OF-EVOLUTION.md), [evolutionary organization](../history/EVOLUTIONARY-ORGANIZATION.md)). The connections checked are these (all periphery; standard Lean axioms):

- inherited structural arrangements change later copying ([inherited-structural-copying](../results/records/inherited-structural-copying.md));
- a formed reading changes the fidelity of copying ([formed-copying-registration](../results/records/formed-copying-registration.md));
- formed checking or editing changes the successors already formed ([formed-copying-correction](../results/records/formed-copying-correction.md));
- growth constrains fragmentation and the composition of successors ([growth-constrained-fragmentation](../results/records/growth-constrained-fragmentation.md));
- organizational rules form and are inherited ([organizational-rule-inheritance](../results/records/organizational-rule-inheritance.md)).

**On the spine, evolution through descent has a minimal formal content** ([Becoming.Evolution](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Evolution.lean), [evolution-on-descent](../results/records/evolution-on-descent.md)). Descent is defined by taking part, giving and coming to stand as a condition, with no valuation.

- *Price's equation, counting form.* With no premise, for any finite family of parents and its offspring, the offspring's total divides into the parents' amounts weighted by their offspring, and the change in transmission (`price`). This is an arithmetic identity. The weighted total is not the selection term proper: where every parent has k offspring it is k times the parents' total (`selected_uniform`).
- *Support.* Where only active formations give and activity needs support, an unsupported formation leaves no descendants (`unsupported_childless`), and the parents' weighted need is bounded by the condition's level (`selected_le`).
- *Transmission.* Where descendants take up their parents' dependences (`TransmitsWithin`, a premise), a transmitted need exceeds the condition's level at the giving step by at most the bound of transmission, and with bound zero it was met there (`transmitted_supported`, `Slack`). In a balanced flow, what is taken and given in between sets what can be transmitted later (`transmitted_within`).
- *Drawdown.* A parent's own taking lowers the pool, leaving a descendant that inherits its need unsupported, while a descendant needing less is supported and takes in turn (`Drawdown`): transmission, variation, selection and the feedback of activity on selection lie in one unfolding.

Fitness is not defined. Reproductive rates, mutation, drift and population dynamics are not covered. The mechanisms listed above keep their own scope, and no correspondence between them and this content is yet proved.

**What can form next: admission, parts and regions.** On 10 October 2026 Ren wrote that being generative is that “its existence creates more possibilities of becoming - more future, not less”, and that “"not harm" - this is a phase transition with a clear boundary, not some "outside will or belief"”. Ren confirmed two readings ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-axiom-route-levels-and-possibilities.md)):

- possibilities are what can form next from what exists, one future having more when it keeps all the other has and adds some;
- the whole is never any center's model of it.

The following states that reading on the spine with no axiom ([Becoming.Admission](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Admission.lean), [Becoming.Regions](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Regions.lean)). The formalization is the AI's.

- **Admission and where support comes from.** Levels admit a formation when they meet every requirement it has at that step; support is admission by the levels that stand. Where requirements do not loosen, a formation gains support only through a rise of a condition it requires. In a balanced flow where only active formations give and activity needs support, every rise is given. A formation that gains support cannot have only unsupported givers to the conditions it requires: it is not the case that every such giver is unsupported (`gains_from_supported`). Where nothing beyond gives and requirements do not loosen, what is unsupported stays unsupported: the ending absorbs, and each premise is needed (`unsupported_stays`, `Restoring`; [support-from-the-supported](../results/records/support-from-the-supported.md)).
- **The phase boundary is a net exchange.** Withhold a part (a formation or a system) with the rest of the exchange as it was. The levels without it are unique where they exist. The difference is exact: each level with what the part took equals the level without the part with what the part gave (`Balances.withheld`). So the phase boundary is exact. A formation is supported exactly when, on every condition it requires, its need with what the part has taken so far is at most the level without the part with what the part has given. That is, the part's cumulative net draw stays within the margin by which the levels without it meet the need (`supported_iff_within_margin`). A part closes something that could form only through a net draw beyond the margin on a condition it requires (finding that condition uses classical logic), and opens something only through a net give (`supported_of_net_give`, `admits_of_net_draw`). A part that gives back at every step at least what it takes closes nothing; one that gives no more than it takes opens nothing; one that gives back exactly what it takes changes nothing ([closing-needs-net-draw](../results/records/closing-needs-net-draw.md)).
- **Four cases.** Compare by inclusion the levels with and without the part, through the formations admitted at each step. Informally, with classical logic, a part does one of four things:
  - it widens, as a giver from beyond does (`Inflow.widens`);
  - it narrows, as the clearer does (`Clearing.narrows`);
  - it keeps the same;
  - it is mixed, each side admitting something the other does not, as a conveyor is (`Transfer.mixed`).

  Mixed parts are not ordered by inclusion. Ordering them needs a measure, which is a further premise, and inclusion licenses no imposed ending ([four-cases-of-a-part](../results/records/four-cases-of-a-part.md)). The four cases are named in Lean. The levels with the part widen those without it (`Web.Widens`, including and exceeding); narrowing is the reverse; keeping the same is including each other (`Web.Keeps`); and being mixed is exceeding each other (`Web.Mixed`). Widening goes one way and is transitive, and the cases exclude one another, with no classical logic (`Web.Widens.not_reverse`, `Web.Widens.trans`, `Web.Widens.not_keeps`, `Web.Widens.not_mixed`, `Web.Keeps.not_mixed`). That every part falls under one of the four is not stated in Lean; it is the classical step above. Section 6.13 states the generative space from these with no axiom.
- **Levels of regions.** What holds within a region holds within every region inside it. What is lost beyond a region is lost beyond every region inside it: the smaller the region, the more lies beyond it. Nothing lies beyond the whole. The clearer is active and supported at every step and closes nothing within itself and the source it depends on. Beyond them, the dweller is admitted without the clearer's part and not admitted with it (`Clearing.loses_beyond`; [loss-beyond-a-region](../results/records/loss-beyond-a-region.md)). So a part can continue while closing what lies beyond its dependence. “Only non-degenerating dynamics continue indefinitely” fails for parts, while the whole has nowhere to push the loss.
- **Represented boundaries.** A formation renews over a region of conditions when it is active and its step keeps every dependence on every condition of the region that it takes part in. Compared with its actual dependence ([represented-boundary-against-dependence](../results/records/represented-boundary-against-dependence.md)):
  - over a region covering every condition it takes part in, this is renewal at the step;
  - over a region covering every condition it requires, with requirements not tightening, it keeps its own support yet may undermine another: the clearer renews over its resource and undermines at the first step;
  - over a region omitting a required condition, its own draw can undermine its own dependence, and it loses its support (`Omitted`).

  Ren says a center and its boundary lie within the same cognitive dynamics ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-centers-within-the-dynamics.md)). Here a region is only a parameter of the statement, with no status outside the unfolding.

The earlier phase results remain in `Becoming.Continuation`:

- no condition is drawn down without end (`excess_bounded`);
- joint excess that brings a level below a need ends what depends on the condition at that need (`joint_excess_ends`).

*Readings* [AI reading, D2 and D1]:

- widening, narrowing and keeping the same are read as Ren's generative, degenerative and neutral;
- the regions are read as self, system and whole, and as the boundary a center represents.

The comparison counts formations admitted. It does not count the renewal capacity or the richness of relations that Ren also named. These theorems order the criteria by strength; they do not say which one a formation should follow.

**Feeding and return.** The continuation of a formation is fed by another at a step when the other gives, at that step, to a condition the first then requires (`Flow.FedBy`). The following states, with no axiom, what a continuation actually depends on and how a loss returns ([Becoming.Interdependence](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Interdependence.lean)). The formalization and the names are the AI's [AI reading, D17].

- **The feeding closure.** A region of formations closed under feeding holds every feeder of its members (`Flow.FedClosed`). A formation's feeding closure is what lies in every region closed under feeding that holds it (`Flow.WithinFeeding`). It holds the formation and every feeder, is transitive and is itself closed under feeding, so it is the least region closed under feeding that holds the formation. Nothing beyond it gives, at any step, to a condition a member then requires (`Flow.fedClosed_within`, `Flow.beyond_gives_nothing`; [feeding-closure](../results/records/feeding-closure.md)). In `Clearing`, the region along dependence of section 6.8 is exactly each formation's feeding closure (`Clearing.dependenceRegion_iff`).
- **Consumption ends with its feeders.** Under balance, with only active formations giving and activity needing support, take a formation that takes from a condition at every step at which it is active, while nothing supported gives to the condition. It is active for at most as many consecutive steps as the condition's level at the start (`Flow.Balances.unfed_consumption_ends`). This is so where everything feeding it is unsupported: its consumption ends with its feeders (`Flow.Balances.consumption_ends_with_feeders`; [consumption-ends-with-feeders](../results/records/consumption-ends-with-feeders.md)).
- **An ending returns through a feeder.** In `Overshoot`, each of two formations feeds the other's condition, so the feeding closure of either is the whole. At step 0 one takes from both conditions. It renews over the one condition it requires and keeps its own support across the step, while its draw undermines the other's dependence. The other is unsupported from step 1 on, the first is active for at most one step more, and from step 2 on nothing is supported (`Overshoot.ends_through_feeder`). Where each takes only from its own condition, both continue at every step (`Overshoot.steady_continues`). So a boundary represented over the conditions a formation requires is not enough: what its continuation depends on reaches the continuation of its feeders.
- **What lies beyond the closure need not return.** In `Clearing` the dweller lies beyond the clearer's closure. Everything within the closure is supported at every step and the clearer is active throughout, while the dweller ends (`Clearing.dweller_beyond`, `Clearing.closure_supported`).

The witnesses' endings are stipulated in their activity and shown consistent with balance, support and dependence; the general theorems derive endings. A comparison by withheld levels keeps the rest of the exchange fixed. So an ending that returns through the dynamics is shown on the actual flow, against a separate reference flow.

**Feeding across steps, redundancy and reserves.** Earlier giving reaches a later need through a condition that stands throughout the stretch ([Becoming.Feeding](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Feeding.lean)).

- Feeding at the same step is exactly the case of zero delay. The least closure across steps contains the closure at the same step, and storage can make the inclusion strict (`fedBy_iff_fedAcross`, `WithinFeeding.across`, `Provision.through_store`). Nothing outside it gives to a condition that can be carried to the step of a member's need.
- Carrying requires local balance over the stretch and a positive standing amount in between. It characterizes a possible retention; it does not identify the actual source of what is given.
- Under local balance, the exact stepwise condition for meeting a need is `m + taken ≤ level + given`; the condition over a stretch accumulates what is given and taken. Support does not guarantee activity.
- Either of two redundant feeders can suffice. Withholding a part that only gives, the need stays met exactly where the margin covers the cumulative giving. Withholding both, with the remaining taking fixed, there can be no balancing path (`meets_without_iff_at`, `Redundant.withheld_both`).
- The earlier reserve bounds on ending are reused, not reproved.

Not derived: actual retention, an exact equivalence for a stricter notion of carrying, and how a center represents the closure. Records: [feeding-closure](../results/records/feeding-closure.md), [consumption-ends-with-feeders](../results/records/consumption-ends-with-feeders.md). Study: [section 107](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#107-跨步供给冗余与储备p86).

**Feedback and reach.** A formation is affected by another when the other exchanges with a condition it depends on (`Flow.AffectedBy`). The following states, with no axiom, how far a formation's own feedback reaches, how far a part's exchange reaches, and where the comparison with and without a part is decided ([Becoming.Feedback](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Feedback.lean)). The formalization and the names are the AI's.

- **Five levels** ([levels-of-closure](../results/records/levels-of-closure.md)). A region is closed under a relation when whatever a member bears the relation to is a member, and a formation lies within another's closure when every closed region holding the other holds it (`ClosedUnder`, `WithinClosure`); this is the seed–edge closure from one seed (`withinClosure_iff_closure`, `Becoming.EdgeClosure`). The feeding closure is the closure under being fed (`Flow.withinFeeding_iff`). The closure under being affected is the influence closure, what can affect the formation (`Flow.WithinInfluence`); the closure under affecting is the reach closure, what the formation's exchange can affect, link by link (`Flow.Affects`, `Flow.WithinReach`). Two formations share a condition when both take part in it, by depending on it or exchanging with it (`Flow.Shares`), and the closure under sharing is the system, a class (`Flow.WithinSystem`, `Flow.WithinSystem.symm`). Each formation lies within its feeding closure, which lies within its influence closure; the influence and reach closures lie within the system (`Flow.withinInfluence_of_withinFeeding`, `Flow.withinSystem_of_withinInfluence`, `Flow.withinSystem_of_withinReach`). Each inclusion can be strict, and the influence and reach closures point in opposite directions. In `Clearing` the source lies within the clearer's feeding closure and beyond its reach closure, and the dweller beyond its influence closure and within its reach closure (`Clearing.levels`, `Clearing.reach_middle`); the dweller's reach closure holds only the dweller (`Clearing.reach_centred`). `Trophic` is a trophic cascade: wolves lie beyond a forest's feeding closure and within its influence closure, affecting it through the deer, and a formation far off lies beyond the forest's system (`Trophic.mediates`).
- **One common condition makes the system the whole.** Where every formation takes part in one condition, every system is the whole (`Flow.system_whole_of_common`), as in `Clearing` (`Clearing.clearer_system`, `Clearing.system_whole`). Formations that depend on one condition share a system even where nothing exchanges with it and neither affects the other (`Static.shares_unaffected`). Where every formation depends on one condition and exchanges with it, every reach closure is the whole (`Flow.reach_whole_of_common`). Where at one step every formation requires one condition and gives to it, every feeding closure is the whole, and significance bounded by it, the route without axiom of `Becoming.Interdependence`, is the condition (`Flow.feeding_whole_of_common`, `Flow.feeding_unrestricted_of_common`).
- **Levels follow their exchanges.** Two balanced flows that start a condition alike, and take from it and give to it alike, keep it alike (`Flow.Balances.levels_agree`). So a formation's support follows only the exchanges with what it depends on (`Flow.Balances.support_agrees`). What a part does shows on a condition exactly through its net exchange with it (`Flow.Balances.agrees_iff_even`). The levels with and without the part agree where none of its members exchanges with the condition (`Flow.Balances.agrees_of_quiet`); where they differ at a step, some member exchanged with it at an earlier step (`Flow.Balances.exchanges_before_of_differs`).
- **What feedback carries** ([feedback-and-reach](../results/records/feedback-and-reach.md)). Nothing beyond a formation's influence closure exchanges with a condition that a member of the closure depends on (`Flow.beyond_influence`). Where every member of a part lies beyond the closure, each formation within it is supported with the part exactly where the levels without the part admit it (`Flow.Balances.uncoupled_beyond_influence`). In `Clearing` the clearer's support is the same at every step whether or not it clears the habitat, while the dweller ends (`Clearing.uncoupled`). The influence closure is an upper bound: with the rest of the exchange fixed, a formation's support follows only the parts that reach it directly (`Flow.Balances.admits_unreached`).
- **What a part reaches.** A part reaches a formation when a member exchanges with a condition the formation depends on (`Flow.Reaches`), and reaches it before a step when a member exchanged, at an earlier step, with a condition the formation requires at that step (`Flow.ReachesBefore`). Whatever a part has not reached before a step is admitted alike with and without it there (`Flow.Balances.admits_unreached_before`, `Flow.Balances.admits_unreached`). An exchange that comes after the dependence affects without effect (`Late.affected_without_effect`). What a part whose members lie within a region closed under affecting reaches lies within the region (`Flow.reaches_within_closed`); the reach closure and the system are such regions (`Flow.reaches_withinReach`, `Flow.withinSystem_closed`), and a formation's own part has its members within its reach closure (`Flow.own_within_reach`). What lies within a system reaches only within it, and what lies beyond reaches nothing within it (`Flow.reaches_withinSystem`, `Flow.unreached_within_system`).
- **The comparison is decided within what the part reaches** ([comparison-within-reach](../results/records/comparison-within-reach.md)). Two significances that agree, from a standpoint, at each step on what the part has reached before it compare the levels with and without the part alike (`Flow.Balances.comparison_local`). For any significance that covers what the part has reached before each step, the levels with and without the part retain what has significance along each other exactly as they include each other, both ways, and neither adds a continuation with significance exactly where neither exceeds the other: the condition's comparisons (`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_reach`). Where significance is decidable, adding is exceeding (`Flow.Balances.adds_iff_exceeds_of_decidable`). So significance bounded by a region closed under affecting compares every part within it as inclusion does (`Flow.Balances.closed_comparison`), and the reach closure is the least such region holding a center. In `Clearing` the clearer's part reaches the dweller (`Clearing.reaches_dweller`). Significance bounded by each reach closure is not the condition, yet from the clearer's standpoint the part loses the dweller, as under the condition (`Clearing.reach_counts_dweller`); bounded by each influence closure, the part retains (`Clearing.system_counts_dweller`).
- **Any two flows.** The comparison of any two balanced flows that start alike is decided within what their difference reaches before each step (`Flow.DiffersBefore`, `Flow.Balances.comparison_within_difference`). With the rest of the exchange fixed the difference is a part's exchange; where others respond, it holds their responses as well. The wolves' part does not reach the forest, while the difference from the flow in which the deer respond reaches it (`Trophic.difference_reaches_forest`).
- **Where the comparison stops.** Covering is enough and not needed: a part can reach beyond a formation's influence closure and change no admission there (`Margin.reach_without_misjudgment`). A part with a member beyond a system can be misjudged by significance bounded by that system (`Trophic.mixed_part`). The comparison by withheld levels holds the rest of the exchange fixed, so effects through others' responses do not show in it: with the deer's browsing held as it is, the wolves' part narrows, closing the deer (`Trophic.mediated_uncoupled`); against the flow in which the deer respond, the same taking is mixed (`Trophic.responsive_mixed`). Where a part gives what others take, the levels without it may not exist, and there is no comparison by withheld levels (`Clearing.no_levels_without_source`).

Feedback and a part's exchange point in opposite directions: a formation's feedback carries at most its influence closure, what can affect it, while its own part reaches into its reach closure, what it can affect. Whether a formation knows beyond its feedback, the dynamics do not decide. The comparison by withheld levels keeps the rest of the exchange fixed, and a part is a decided family of formations, so one act of a formation is not separated from its other exchanges and is compared through a second flow, as in `Clearing.uncoupled`; the representation commits to discrete steps and natural-number amounts. Readings of reach, the system one is in and situational awareness are the AI's [AI reading, D20].

**The comparison where the rest responds.** The comparison by withheld levels keeps the rest of the exchange fixed, and the comparison of any two flows is decided within what their difference reaches, without saying where that difference can go. This module states where it can go when the rest responds through what couples into it ([Becoming.Response](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Response.lean)). A formation lies within a part's responsive reach when it lies within some member's closure under affecting through what couples in: a member exchanges, in one of the two flows, with a condition coupled into the formation, or reaches it by such links (`Flow.WithinResponse`). The rest responds through its couplings when every formation outside the part exchanges alike in the two flows across each step up to which the flows have been alike through its couplings (`Flow.RespondsThroughCoupling`); this is a supplied premise, not derived from the dynamics, and the rest held fixed is a case of it (`Flow.respondsThroughCoupling_withhold`). Under it, with the same formations present, balance and a common start on what couples into the formations beyond the responsive reach, the flows are alike through the couplings of each of them and it exchanges alike at every step (`Flow.alike_beyond_response`, an instance of `Flow.alike_within_closed`), and the difference between the flows reaches none of them (`Flow.not_differs_beyond_response`). So for two balanced flows starting alike, any significance covering every formation not beyond the responsive reach compares them as the condition does (`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_response`). The responsive reach is the dual of the coupling closure (`Flow.withinResponse_iff_coupling`) and holds what the part reaches and each member's reach closure; where coupling is by threshold only, with one flow, it is exactly the union of the members' reach closures (`Flow.withinResponse_thresholdOnly_iff`). In the trophic cascade the deer respond through their support: the forest lies within the wolves' responsive reach, which their part does not reach, and the reach is exactly the wolves, the deer and the forest, among which the responsive comparison is decided (`Trophic.within_response_iff`, `Trophic.responsive_comparison`). In `Loop` a part's act changes what couples into another formation, the other responds, and the difference returns to the part's own support, which the part's own exchange never reaches (`Loop.returns`). *Readings* [AI reading, D25]. An act has two counterfactuals: with the rest fixed only what the part reaches directly counts, and with the rest responding through its couplings the responsive reach counts; an escalation loop is the case where each party responds through its couplings and the difference returns to the part; where coupling is by threshold only, the reach closure is where the difference an act makes can arrive when the rest responds through its support. That formations respond only through their couplings and how they respond are not derived; how much difference arrives is answered in `Becoming.Propagation` where the rest follows a law with given coupling strengths. Record: [comparison-where-the-rest-responds](../results/records/comparison-where-the-rest-responds.md). Study: [section 87](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#87-其余回应时的比较在回应所及之内决定p65).

**The response as the dynamics give.** The comparison of `Becoming.Response` supplies two things: the second flow, and the premise that the rest responds through its couplings. This module gives the rest's exchange as a law and derives both ([Becoming.Counterfactual](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Counterfactual.lean)). A law of exchange reads off a level history what each formation takes from and gives to each condition at each step (`ExchangeLaw`); it reads what has stood when, across the step from `t`, it reads the history up to `t` only (`ExchangeLaw.Nonanticipating`). Two balanced flows with the same formations present, starting alike, in which the part acts alike and the rest follows one law of what has stood, have the same levels and the same exchange at every step (`counterfactual_fixed`): once the law is given, the course of an act is not supplied. The law generates a course step by step from starting levels, the orbit of one determined law on the stage and the history so far (`ExchangeLaw.history_orbit`), and the generated flow balances exactly where the law never takes more from a condition than stands on it and is given to it (`ExchangeLaw.course_balances_iff`). So the counterfactual of an act is the course it generates (`counterfactual_is_course`), and where every formation follows one law of what has stood, a balanced flow is the course the law generates (`ExchangeLaw.course_unique`). Where the law reads, for each formation outside the part, only what couples into it (`ExchangeLaw.Through`), the rest responds through its couplings in any two courses of the law (`ExchangeLaw.Through.responds`): the premise of `Becoming.Response` becomes a theorem. Every law of what has stood reads through the coupling of everything; with finitely many conditions it reads through the conditions its exchange is sensitive to (`ExchangeLaw.Nonanticipating.through_sensitive`), and no coupling it reads through, with what the formation depends on, leaves out a condition it is sensitive to (`ExchangeLaw.Through.couples_of_sensitive`, in double-negation form: it cannot fail to hold such a condition): sensitivity is the least coupling, at that strength. So the comparison of two courses of the law is decided within the responsive reach drawn through the law's coupling, which with finitely many conditions is its sensitivity; and so is the comparison of an act with the course another act generates, with neither the second flow nor the response supplied (`comparison_of_courses`, `comparison_through_sensitivity`, `comparison_with_act`). The act is the input of a standard causal model: the course is a trajectory of the model of `Becoming.Mechanism` whose input is the part's move, and an act not taken is an intervention, whose outcome the record does not fix and the model does (`ExchangeLaw.course_trajectory`, `ExchangeLaw.act_not_taken`). In the `Loop` of `Becoming.Response` the second's reply is a law reading only what couples into it by level, `Loop.responds` follows from it, and each flow is the only balanced course of the law with its act (`Loop.responds_by_law`, `Loop.courses_fixed`). As an unfolding of `Becoming.Unfolding`: where formations and conditions are one kind of relatum, the course is a world running by the law of exchange exactly where feasible (`ExchangeLaw.world_exchange_iff`); a determined law steps beyond what a run of it shows exactly at the moves the run never takes (`Run.beyond_shown_iff`, a law about `Shown`), so what lies beyond the course's history is exactly its counterfactuals (`ExchangeLaw.counterfactual_beyond_shown`); with the part's move formed by a law on the configuration, a run of `closedBy` is the course (`ExchangeLaw.closedBy_course`). *Readings* [AI reading, D25]. A counterfactual is not a second world beside the actual one but the course of the same law with the part acting otherwise, existing where feasible and fixed by the act, the law's step beyond its history rather than a second history beside it; for a rest following a law of what has stood, the premise of `Becoming.Response` is no further assumption about how formations behave, and, read as 识 (shí), what couples into a formation is what its exchange is sensitive to. The law itself, that it reads what has stood, and feasibility are not derived. Whether sensitivity is still the least coupling with infinitely many conditions is answered in `Becoming.Sensitivity`: it is wherever the law reads finitely many conditions under each history; whether that condition can be dropped turns on omniscience: given a decision of whether levels vanish eventually the tail law is a counterexample, and with the conditions numbered every counterexample gives the weak limited principle of omniscience; a counterexample exists exactly where a rule decides, of every Boolean sequence, whether it is false throughout (`Becoming.Tail`). When a move is the part's own is answered in `Becoming.Coupling`: exactly where it is formed through the part's configuration. Records: [counterfactual-is-the-course](../results/records/counterfactual-is-the-course.md), [comparison-where-the-rest-responds](../results/records/comparison-where-the-rest-responds.md). Study: [section 100](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#100-回应由动力学给出反事实是规律的进程p80).

**What a law of the whole and no part takes.** `Becoming.Sensitivity` leaves open the exact strength of the existence of a counterexample, between the weak limited principle of omniscience and the decision of vanishing ([Becoming.Tail](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Tail.lean)). A number read off levels is changed by no condition where levels differing at one condition only give it alike (`ChangedByNoCondition`); exactly then levels agreeing from some condition on give it alike, the conditions below being changed one at a time (`ChangedByNoCondition.eq_of_agree_from`, `ChangedByNoCondition.of_agree_from`): what no part changes, only the tail sets. The law of the whole exchanging such a number (`wholeLaw`) reads what has stood, is insensitive at every step to every condition and reads through the conditions from any one on; where the number differs, it does not read through what it is sensitive to, nor finitely many conditions, and the registrations it reads through have no least one (`wholeLaw_not_through_sensitive`, `wholeLaw_not_readsFinitely`, `wholeLaw_no_least_coupling`); the tail law is the instance of the indicator of vanishing (`tailLaw_eq_wholeLaw`). `Becoming.Sensitivity` mixes two histories along a Boolean sequence and then compares two numbers; comparing numbers is decided, so every counterexample gives not only the weak limited principle of omniscience but a rule deciding, of every Boolean sequence, whether it is false throughout (`mixedDecision`, `insensitiveDiffersDecision`, `notThroughSensitiveDecision`; the rule gives `WLPO`, `WLPO.of_rule`). Conversely, given such a rule, whether levels vanish from condition N on is decided (`vanishesFrom`), and whether they vanish from no condition on is decided by the rule again (`neverIndicator`, `vanishesFrom_never_iff`); this indicator is changed by no condition, tells levels all 0 from levels all 1, and with the decision of vanishing given sums with the indicator of vanishing to 1 (`neverIndicator_add_vanishIndicator`); its law is a counterexample built from the rule alone (`neverLaw`). So a number changed by no condition that still differs, a law insensitive at a step to every condition that still differs there, and a counterexample to the least registration each exist exactly where such a rule does (`changedByNoCondition_iff_rule`, `insensitive_differs_iff_rule`, `not_through_sensitive_iff_rule`). The decision of vanishing is exactly the limited principle of omniscience as a rule: it decides of every Boolean sequence whether it has a true, and such a rule decides vanishing in two passes (`lpoRuleOfVanishDecision`, `vanishDecisionOfLPORule`, `vanishDecision_iff_lpoRule`), giving the weak rule and `LPO` (`wlpoRuleOfLPORule`, `LPO.of_rule`); so the tail law takes the limited principle as a rule, where a counterexample needs only the weak one. *Readings* [AI reading, D43]. What depends on the whole and on no part of it is given exactly where it can be decided, of every unending record of yes and no, whether it says no throughout; with the conditions numbered, the least registration fails exactly at this omniscience and needs no more of it, and the core does not assume it; read as whether levels never vanish, a whole of this kind takes only the weak rule, and read as whether they vanish eventually, a related whole, classically its complement but in the core another law, takes the rule of the limited principle. Whether the proposition `WLPO` gives a rule (choosing a function from a relation, which the core does not assume), whether the weak rule gives the limited principle, and what a counterexample gives where the conditions are not numbered by the natural numbers are not derived. See [what-a-law-of-the-whole-and-no-part-takes](../results/records/what-a-law-of-the-whole-and-no-part-takes.md).

**How much difference arrives.** The responsive reach says where a difference can go, and sensitivity whether a level can change an exchange; neither says how much ([Becoming.Propagation](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Propagation.lean)). Across a step the difference of two levels (`gap`, what either has beyond the other) changes by exactly the difference given less the difference taken: in two balanced flows, the first's level of a condition after the step and the second's before, with what the first takes and the second gives, equal the second's level after and the first's before, with what the second takes and the first gives (`levels_step_exact`). So a condition's gap across a step is at most its gap before with the gaps of every exchange with it (`gap_step`), and does not grow where every exchange is alike (`gap_step_of_alike`). A formation's exchange with a condition is coupled to the level of a condition at a step with strength at most k where, under any two histories differing in that level only, what it takes and gives differ in all by at most k times that level's gap (`ExchangeLaw.RespondsWithin`): the measure of sensitivity. For a law of what has stood, strength zero is insensitivity (`ExchangeLaw.insensitiveAt_iff_strength_zero`, `ExchangeLaw.sensitive_iff_strength`); strengths add up over levels changed one at a time (`ExchangeLaw.exchangeGap_le_mixCells`, `ExchangeLaw.exchangeGap_le_strengths`), so the change of a move is at most the part's share, its strengths times the part's change, with the rest's share (`ExchangeLaw.exchangeGap_le_shares`). A finite strength is a condition on a law, not given with every law: a law giving the square of a level has none (`Propagation.Square.not_respondsWithin`). The carrying law has the part give its gaps and each formation outside it give what its strengths make of the levels so far, taking nothing (`ExchangeLaw.carrying`); the carried amount is the course it generates from the starting gaps (`carried`, by `Becoming.Counterfactual`'s generation). Where the rest follows in both flows one law of what has stood with those strengths, the part differing by at most its gaps and the starting levels by at most theirs, every gap at every step is at most the carried amount (`gap_le_carried`). It is attained: the carrying law has those strengths, and its course differs from the quiet course by exactly the carried amount (`ExchangeLaw.carrying_respondsWithin`, `carried_attained`), so the carried amount is the most difference that can arrive (`most_difference_arrives`, an equivalence). It is positive exactly along a route in time of positive strengths (`Conveyed`, `carried_pos_iff`); where there is none the two flows' levels agree (`levels_alike_of_not_conveyed`), and where the positive strengths are registered the routes are those of `TimedResponse`'s spread (`Conveyed.spread`). Carried differences add (`carried_add`), and more gaps and larger strengths carry more (`carried_mono`). Witnesses: one unit given once, which the rest relays each step to a second condition at twice its level, arrives there as 2t by step t + 1, without bound and with no loop (`Propagation.Chain.keeps_arriving`); returned by a loop of strength one, it arrives as 2^t (`Propagation.Loop.runaway`). *Readings* [AI reading, D39]. The coupling strength is the measure sensitivity lacked, sensitivity saying whether and the strength how strongly; how much difference arrives is itself a course, generated by one law and read off the unfolding rather than added to it; a held difference keeps arriving, since levels hold what they are given, under a law that takes nothing and so creates amounts (the carrying law); under the first law this is not shown, and in a finite whole whose rest gives nothing net the levels stay within the stock (`Flow.whole_bounded_of_rest_idle`). The strengths themselves are supplied bounds, and no least strength is stated; infinitely many conditions, continuous time and real amounts are not derived; a general theory of loops is given in `Becoming.Loops`. See [how-much-difference-arrives](../results/records/how-much-difference-arrives.md).

**How a carried difference grows.** `Becoming.Propagation` gives the most difference that can arrive, with witnesses only for loops ([Becoming.Loops](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Loops.lean)). The carried amount expands by order: order j (`carriedWithin`) is what routes of at most j responses carry, each order being what the strengths make of the order below (`carriedFrom`); the orders rise with j to the carried amount (`carriedWithin_mono`, `carriedWithin_le_carried`), and order j is exact up to step j (`carriedWithin_exact`), as each step adds at most one response. The level of one condition is coupled into another (`Couples`) where at some step a formation present, outside the part, exchanges with the second with positive strength on that level; a chain of couplings (`Coupled`) leads from one condition to another, a loop (`OnLoop`) back to where it started, and e lies upstream of c (`Upstream`) where a chain leads from e to c. Where no chain of j + 1 couplings ends at c, order j is exact at c at every step (`carriedWithin_exact_of_not_coupledInto`); with every condition listed, a chain as long as the list passes some condition twice (`loop_of_coupledInto`, by pigeonhole), so where no loop lies upstream of c, the order one less than the number of conditions is exact at c at every step (`carriedWithin_exact_of_no_loop`). Where the couplings hold at every step (`CouplesAlways`), what reaches a loop upstream keeps arriving at c by longer routes, so every order leaves something out (`carriedWithin_lt_of_loop`): a finite order is exact from every starting gap exactly where no loop lies upstream (`finite_order_iff_no_loop`); the couplings must hold at every step, as a loop coupled at the first step only leaves order one exact (`Loops.Once.not_always`). Along a chain with strengths at least w, what reaches its start arrives at its end times the product of the strengths (`carried_along`); around a loop, what has reached it returns multiplied by at least one plus the product of the strengths around it (`carried_around`), and after R times around by that factor to the power R (`carried_runaway`). With the part giving no gap after the start, starting gaps at most D and the strengths into each condition (`inflow`) summing to at most M at each step: order j carries at most D times the sum over i ≤ j of the ways to place i responses among t steps (`Loops.binom`) times M^i, a polynomial in t of degree j (`carriedWithin_le_withinBound`, `Loops.withinBound_eq`); the carried amount is at most D(1 + M)^t (`carried_le_geomBound`); and a cut at order j misses at most their difference (`carried_le_carriedWithin_add`). One condition returning its level to itself with strength M, a self-loop, attains all three exactly (`Loops.SelfLoop.attained`), so no smaller bound follows from D and M alone. Where no loop lies upstream, the carried amount is at most a polynomial in the steps, of degree one less than the number of conditions (`carried_le_polynomial`). With the same formations and strengths at every step (`stationary`), every coupling holds at every step, every loop at least doubles what reaches it every time around, since a positive natural-number strength is at least one (`carried_doubles_around`), and what reaches a loop upstream of c arrives at c so doubled (`carried_doubles_into`); a doubling outgrows every polynomial (`Loops.not_poly_of_doubling`), so the carried amount is bounded by a polynomial exactly where no loop lies upstream of c (`polynomial_iff_no_loop`). By `most_difference_arrives` these are statements about every law with the strengths: where no loop lies upstream, the most difference that can arrive is what the finite order carries (`most_difference_within`), and every gap is at most the polynomial (`gap_le_polynomial`); with the same formations and strengths at every step, the most difference that can arrive is bounded by a polynomial exactly where no loop lies upstream (`gap_polynomial_iff_no_loop`). *Readings* (the AI's, agreed by Ren on 16 October 2026; [source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-16-loops-readings-and-delegated-choices.md)): on 12 October Ren compared the theory with quantum dynamics and "Fermi's diagrams": “there are so many patterns and loops but we can still find a way to to integrate so we can still calculate this effects but how how accurate can we do that uh what is the limitation do we have losses” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-12-move-towards-100.md)); Ren meant Feynman diagrams, and the match is structural, not physical. The integration Ren asked about, for laws that are not determined, is the sum over histories of `Becoming.Weighted` with its four losses; the orders here are the same structural kind, organized by the number of responses as Feynman diagrams are by their vertices, `Loops.binom` counting their time orderings; the two are not yet joined (they are now joined by `Becoming.Routes`: the orders are sums over routes back in time, and a cut is a truncation). The accuracy of a cut is the rest of the sum, and it is attained, the limit for what the strengths and the starting gaps alone allow; where no loop lies upstream a finite order loses nothing, and where one does, coupled at every step, every finite order loses something and what reaches the loop grows at least geometrically. Only a loop makes a difference run away, where running away means growing faster than every polynomial, and of the most difference that can arrive, not of what every law does: without a loop a held difference keeps arriving but grows at most polynomially (`Propagation.Chain` is of degree one), and with a loop that couples at every step it grows at least geometrically (`Propagation.Loop` is the self-loop of strength one); so the most difference that can arrive grows faster than every polynomial only through a loop, and, with natural-number strengths, each positive one at least one, through every loop that couples at every step (narrowed and taken on 18 October 2026, D43: with real strengths below one such a loop need not run away, which is damping), couplings that hold only at some steps being open here. Which loops a world has is part of its situation (Ren, 16 October: “the situation makes it true”): loops form and dissolve within the unfolding, and the theorems take them as they stand at each step; the strengths are supplied. This reading is taken for now, open to revision once a theorem states how loops form within the unfolding, for example where a formation's drawing comes to respond to the level its drawing lowers, where this meets `Becoming.Runaway` (the loop-formation theorem is now `Becoming.Closing`, and Ren agreed to the reading as revised: which loops a world has is part of its situation: loops form within the unfolding by closing, a loop forming at the step at which some exchange comes to respond to a level downstream of the condition it gives to, and dissolve where their couplings stop (once the schedule has settled, a finite order is exact exactly where no loop of the couplings that go on lies upstream); where the strengths are read off the course, which loops form and when is read off the unfolding; the theorems take the loops as they stand at each step, the strengths or the way they are read being supplied; [source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-16-routes-closing-readings.md)). The strengths themselves, how fast a runaway is beyond the product of the strengths, infinitely many conditions, continuous time and real amounts are not derived; couplings that come and go are treated in `Becoming.Remainder`. See [how-a-carried-difference-grows](../results/records/how-a-carried-difference-grows.md).

**What a cut loses, for every schedule of couplings.** The equivalence of `Becoming.Loops` needs every coupling to hold at every step and every condition listed, and a loop coupled at the first step only shows that the first cannot be dropped as it stands ([Becoming.Remainder](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Remainder.lean)). Here what a cut loses, and where, is stated for every schedule of couplings and any list of conditions. What one response carries from given levels (`responded`) is the carrying law with no starting gap and no gap of the part; j responses in turn (`responses`) respond each to what the one before carried. The carried amount is the orders below j plus what j responses in turn carry from the carried amount itself (`carried_eq_orderBelow_add`), so a cut at order j loses exactly what j + 1 responses carry from it (`carried_eq_carriedWithin_add`); the proof only adds, as giving is additive in the levels read (`carriedGive_add`, `carriedFrom_add`). The loss falls as the order rises (`responses_antitone`), is at most the bound of `Becoming.Loops` for every schedule (`responses_le_missBound`), and is exactly that bound for the self-loop (`Loops.SelfLoop.responses_eq`). One response carries something to c by step t exactly where, at a step before t, the level of a condition is coupled into c (`CouplesAt`) and positive (`responded_pos_iff`); a gap is conveyed to c through j responses or more (`ConveyedThrough`) where the route in time of `Becoming.Propagation` (`Conveyed`) reaches a condition and j couplings carry it on to c, each reading a level the one before had reached. The orders below j miss something at c at step t exactly where a gap is conveyed there through j responses or more (`orderBelow_lt_iff`), and order j is exact there exactly where none is conveyed through j + 1 (`carriedWithin_eq_iff`). A chain of j couplings in time order ends at c (`CoupledIntoBy`) where each coupling reads a level the one before had reached: order j is exact from every start exactly where no such chain of j + 1 couplings ends there (`exact_every_start_iff`), and a finite order is exact at c at every step from every start exactly where the chains in time order into c are bounded in length (`finite_order_iff_bounded`), one order then serving every start (`finite_order_uniform_iff`). These need no premise.
