A coupling recurs (`CouplingRecurs`) where it reads levels at steps as late as one likes: where a gap reaches a loop of recurring couplings and recurring couplings lead from it to c, every order misses something at c (`carriedWithin_lt_of_recurring_loop`); where no coupling reads a level from step N on, order N is exact everywhere (`exact_of_reading_before`). Each coupling reads a listed condition, so a chain one longer than the list passes a loop, with no listing of every condition (`loop_of_coupledInto_succ`). The strengths from step N on (`strengthsFrom`) drop every reading of a level before N; a coupling recurs exactly where it is a coupling from every step on (`couplingRecurs_iff`); of a chain in time order of N + j couplings, the last j are a chain of the couplings from step N on (`CoupledIntoBy.tail`). A schedule has settled by step N (`SettledFrom`) where every coupling from N on recurs: then a finite order is exact at c from every start exactly where no loop of the couplings from step N on lies upstream of c (`finite_order_iff_no_loop_from`), with no listing of every condition; whatever the schedule, where no such loop lies upstream the carried amount is at most a polynomial in the steps (`carried_le_polynomial_from`). Couplings at every step have settled from the first step (`settledFrom_zero_of_always`), so `finite_order_iff_no_loop` holds with no listing of every condition (`Remainder.finite_order_iff_no_loop`); `Loops.Once` reads the first step only, has no chain of two couplings in time order, and its schedule has settled from the second step with nothing coupling (`Loops.Once.no_chain_of_two`, `Loops.Once.settled`). By `most_difference_arrives`, for every law with the strengths, every gap from every start is at most what order j carries exactly where no chain of j + 1 couplings in time order ends there (`gap_within_order_iff`). *Readings* [AI reading, D43]. Ren asked on 12 October 2026 “how how accurate can we do that uh what is the limitation do we have losses” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-12-move-towards-100.md)), and on 16 October took the orders of `Becoming.Loops` as the same structural kind as the sum over histories; the exact remainder says what a cut of that expansion loses, in the form of a Neumann series's remainder: the orders below j are the terms of fewer than j responses, and a cut loses what j responses carry from the whole; as in time-ordered perturbation theory (the Dyson series), the responses of a route come in time order, and a cut loses exactly where a route in time with more responses arrives; the loss is the cut's, not the theory's, the carried amount itself being exact. What decides a cut's loss is the time order of the couplings, not loops drawn without time: the loops that matter are those of the couplings that go on, and a loop whose couplings stop is passing. The most difference that can arrive grows faster than every polynomial only through a loop of the couplings that go on, and not through every such loop: one that recurs rarely stays polynomial (`Frequency.Sparse.polynomial`, under “How often couplings act” below). That every schedule settles at some step is not derived (a coupling that does not recur stops, but whether one recurs is not decided here, and the core assumes no excluded middle); nor are how fast a runaway is beyond the product of the strengths, laws reading infinitely many conditions at a step, continuous time and real amounts (`Becoming.Frequency`, under “How often couplings act” below, answers three of these: not through every such loop; that every schedule settles is exactly a principle of reasoning; the rate counts the steps at which couplings act, the spectral radius of given strengths still not derived). See [what-a-cut-loses](../results/records/what-a-cut-loses.md).

**The orders of a carried difference as a sum over histories.** Ren took the reading that the integration asked about on 12 October is the sum over histories of `Becoming.Weighted` with its losses, that the orders of `Becoming.Loops` are of the same structural kind, and that the two were not yet joined ([Becoming.Routes](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Routes.lean)). For every weighting, each step's weight split into a ground and a response part, order j (`Weights.within`) reads order j through the ground part and order j − 1 through the response part, and is the whole sum over histories at every horizon up to j (`Weights.within_exact`); with one configuration, ground one and response M, order j is `Loops.orderSum M j n` and the sum (1 + M)^n (`Routes.SelfLoop.within_eq`, `Routes.SelfLoop.transfer_eq`). Traced back in time, a difference steps by one response to an earlier level, weighted by the strengths summed over the steps at which it can occur with the level holding between (`responseWeight`), or ends at a source (`routeWeight`). The transfer of these counting weights at horizon j + 1 is order j of the carried amount (`routeSum_eq_carriedWithin`), so each order and the carried amount are sums over courses (`carriedWithin_eq_courses`, `carried_eq_courses`), a cut is a truncation (`routeSum_exact`, `carried_le_routeSum_add`), and with the same formations and strengths at every step a finite horizon is exact exactly where no loop lies upstream (`finite_horizon_iff_no_loop`). *Readings* (the AI's, agreed by Ren on 16 October 2026; [source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-16-routes-closing-readings.md)). The two integrations are one, the responses being the vertices of a diagram and the level holding between them as a propagator sums over intermediate times; the loss by loops of `Becoming.Weighted` is the runaway of `Becoming.Loops`; the match is structural, and counting weights do not cancel.

**How loops form within the unfolding.** Ren took the reading that loops form and dissolve within the unfolding, pending a loop-formation theorem ([Becoming.Closing](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Closing.lean)). The strengths before step T (`strengthsBefore`) keep the couplings acting before T (`couples_before_iff`), so the loops formed by T are stated with the definitions of `Becoming.Loops`; every chain is formed by some step, and a world's loops are exactly those formed by some step (`onLoop_iff_formed`). A loop forms by closing: the loops formed by T + 1 are those formed by T and those through a coupling acting at step T that closes a chain (`onLoop_succ_iff`, `forms_by_closing`); an exchange coming to respond to the level of the condition it gives to forms a loop of one (`responds_to_own_level`). The carried amount up to T reads only the couplings before T (`carried_before`), so with no loop formed by T upstream it is at most a polynomial up to T (`carried_le_polynomial_before`); the amounts before a loop forms do not show whether it will (`Closing.Onset`). Where strengths are read from the levels up to each step (`ReadsBefore`), the course generated is the carried amount of the strengths it reads (`formedCourse_eq_carried`): formation is read off the unfolding; in the threshold witness the loop forms at the step a level reaches the threshold and doubles from then on (`Closing.Threshold`). How loops dissolve is in `Becoming.Remainder` (couplings that stop). *Readings* (the AI's, agreed by Ren on 16 October 2026). A loop forms by closing, at the step an exchange comes to respond to a level downstream of what it gives to; its formation is not foreseen from the amounts before it, which meets `Becoming.Runaway`.

**Loops within what a law reads, and the break.** The reading Ren took left open “where a formation's drawing comes to respond to the level its drawing lowers, where this meets `Becoming.Runaway`”, and Ren asked for the two to be joined ([Becoming.Thresholds](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Thresholds.lean)). A law reads a schedule (`ExchangeLaw.ReadsAt`) when, for each formation outside the part, it gives alike under histories agreeing on the conditions the schedule marks. An unread level is coupled with strength zero (`ExchangeLaw.ReadsAt.respondsWithin_unread`), so the strengths restricted to what is read are strengths of the law (`ExchangeLaw.ReadsAt.restrict`); a law reading through a coupling reads every schedule covering it (`ExchangeLaw.Through.readsAt`), and its courses are policies through the couplings in the sense of `Becoming.Runaway` (`ExchangeLaw.Through.policyThrough`). So loops lie within what is read: every coupling reads its level (`couples_reads`), a draw responds to the level it lowers only where it reads it (`self_coupling_reads`), no loop passes through a level nothing reads (`not_onLoop_unread`), and a loop forms at a step only through a formation reading there a level downstream of where it gives (`onLoop_succ_reads`). For every law, two courses apart only in a stock nothing reads are alike everywhere else, the stock's gap held (`unread_carried_nowhere`). A law reads a stock only below a threshold (`ExchangeLaw.ReadsBelow`) when, while the stock stands at the threshold, it reads only that it stands; every law reading through a coupling in which the stock couples by level into no formation outside the part, with the threshold at least each need of it, is such a law (`ExchangeLaw.Through.readsBelow`), so `Becoming.Runaway`'s formation is an instance. For every such law, raising the stock by any amount changes nothing else up to the break (`break_carried_nowhere`): the distance to the break is carried nowhere, for the whole law and not only for one policy with the rest fixed. Before the break the law is its standing law (`ExchangeLaw.standing`, `Flow.follows_standing_before`), which does not read the stock, is coupled to it with strength zero and has no loop through it (`ExchangeLaw.ReadsBelow.standing_no_loop`): the loop through the stock forms at the break. Witness (`Thresholds.Draw`): a formation draws one unit from a stock and gives it to a product at each step while the stock stands at N + 1; every strengths of it couple the stock into itself, a loop of one (`Draw.onLoop`), yet before the break it gives as its standing law (`Draw.forms_at_break`); at a step where one course's stock stands below the threshold and a raised one's at it, the raised course gives one more to the product (`Draw.diverges_at_break`). *Readings* (the AI's, agreed by Ren on 16 October 2026). A runaway's break is where a loop forms; the blindness of `Becoming.Runaway` and the formation of `Becoming.Closing` are one event seen from two sides; what can carry how near the break is, is a reading of the stock's level before it. See [loops-within-what-a-law-reads](../results/records/loops-within-what-a-law-reads.md).

**Observation as a route from what is observed.** `Becoming.Thresholds` left open how a formation comes to observe a stock it depends on; in `Becoming.Runaway` observation is given. Ren approved that approach and asked for it to be continued ([Becoming.Observation](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Observation.lean)). With the starting gap at a stock c alone and none from the part, a gap conveyed to a condition at step t lies downstream of c by the couplings formed by t (`conveyed_upstream`), so the carried amount, the most difference that can arrive, is zero elsewhere (`carried_zero_off_downstream`). A law reads into each condition a schedule (`ExchangeLaw.ReadsInto`) when what a formation exchanges with a condition is fixed by the levels marked for that condition; a law reading a schedule is the case reading alike for every condition (`ExchangeLaw.ReadsAt.readsInto`, `ExchangeLaw.Through.readsInto`), a level unread into a condition is coupled into it with strength zero (`ExchangeLaw.ReadsInto.respondsWithin_unread`), and routes of strength are routes of reading (`upstream_readCouplings`). For every such law, two balanced courses apart at the start only in c are alike, at each step, at every condition not downstream of c by the readings formed by then (`apart_only_downstream`), with no decision on conditions and no finiteness: a formation's exchange responds to the stock's difference only through a level it reads that lies downstream of the stock (`exchange_alike_off_downstream`, `ExchangeLaw.Through.alike_off_downstream`). The stock it observes is the route of length zero. A route forms by closing, as a loop does (`upstream_succ_iff`, `route_forms_by_closing`). Witness (`Observation.Measure`): from step T a formation adds the stock's level to a record and draws from the stock while the record shows at least N; before T only the stock lies downstream of it and no loop passes through it; at T the route stock → record and the loop stock → record → draw form (`Measure.loop_at`); in courses apart only on the stock the record is alike up to T and differs by the raise after it (`Measure.couples_after`), and the draw responds to the record (`Measure.draw_responds`). *Readings* [AI reading, D43]. Observing lies within the downstream: a formation's exchange responds to a difference at a stock only through a level it reads that lies downstream of the stock, so a formation observes only at the end of a route of readings from what is observed, the route of length zero being direct observation; a formation comes to observe by a reading that closes a route. That observation is no second relation beside dependence goes beyond the theorem, which shows only that it does not go beyond dependence; it is kept as a conjecture (§8.4). See [observation-is-a-route](../results/records/observation-is-a-route.md).

**Where a level couples into a formation.** The naming study of 17-18 October adopted "couples into" for what a formation was said to register, by threshold or by level, and Ren asked for the check that the level branch, a supplied relation `obs`, is a coupling in the sense of `Becoming.Loops` and `Becoming.Observation` ([Becoming.LevelCoupling](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/LevelCoupling.lean)). An exchange responds to the level of d at step s when two histories differing at most in that level give a positive exchange gap (`ExchangeLaw.RespondsTo`). Strength zero is exactly no response (`ExchangeLaw.respondsWithin_zero_iff`), and a response forces every strength of the law for that level to be positive (`ExchangeLaw.RespondsTo.strength_pos`), so it is a coupling of every strength array of the law and a route of length one (`ExchangeLaw.RespondsTo.couples`, `ExchangeLaw.RespondsTo.upstream`). For a law reading through the couplings (`ExchangeLaw.Through`), a level that couples into a formation outside the part neither by threshold nor by level is coupled with strength zero (`ExchangeLaw.Through.respondsWithin_uncoupled`). A response therefore comes from a condition that couples in (`ExchangeLaw.Through.couples_of_respondsTo`, as a double negation) and is a route of reading in every schedule covering what couples in, and of the law's strengths restricted to that schedule (`ExchangeLaw.Through.couples_readCouplings`, `ExchangeLaw.Through.upstream_restrictInto`). Where a condition couples into the formation by threshold only, a change of the level that leaves its need met alike changes nothing it exchanges (`ExchangeLaw.Through.not_respondsByLevel`): through the threshold only a crossing passes. Where the condition couples in by level, the law taking that level responds to it within its need at every step (`levelLaw_through`, `levelLaw_respondsByLevel`). So, up to double negation, coupling by level (`obs`) is exactly where some law reading through the couplings couples a level into the formation within its need (`not_not_obs_iff_respondsByLevel`). A response to a condition not coupled in by level is a crossing of its need (`ExchangeLaw.Through.crosses_of_unobserved`), and where the formation requires at a step exactly that condition, with a positive need, some law reading through the couplings responds to a crossing there (`crossing_passes`): by threshold a crossing passes. Among several conditions at one step, such a response changes the formation's admission there, and happens only where its other requirements are met (`ExchangeLaw.Through.admission_changes_of_unobserved`, `ExchangeLaw.Through.others_met_of_unobserved`). Where that admission is decided, for example by a listed support, a crossing of a condition passes into every later exchange exactly where the formation requires the condition there with a positive need (`crossing_passes_iff`, `crossing_passes_of_support`). Not shown: a crossing where admission is not decided, and whether a given law responds to what it may read. *Reading* [AI reading, open]. `obs` is not a second kind of relation beside the couplings and readings: it marks where a level can couple into a formation by level, as dependence marks where it can couple by threshold; what can couple in bounds what responds, and a law's responses are its couplings. This answers, for the level branch, the question that [observation-is-a-route](../results/records/observation-is-a-route.md) leaves open. See [where-a-level-couples](../results/records/where-a-level-couples.md).

**How often couplings act: settling, runaway and its rate.** `Becoming.Remainder` leaves three questions: whether every schedule of couplings settles, whether every loop of recurring couplings makes the most difference that can arrive run away, and how fast a runaway is ([Becoming.Frequency](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Frequency.lean)). A schedule (`on`) marks steps, and `Frequency.count` counts those before step t. With the part giving no gap after the start, starting gaps at most D, and the strengths into each condition at most M at each step and zero at unmarked steps, the carried amount at step t is at most D·(1 + M)^a, a the marked steps before it (`carried_le_count`): the geometric bound of `Becoming.Loops` multiplies only at marked steps; with every condition listed, the steps at which anything couples are such a schedule (`Frequency.coupling`, `carried_le_coupling`). One condition returning its level to itself with strength M at the steps of any schedule couples exactly there (`Frequency.SelfLoop.couplesAt_iff`) and carries exactly D·(1 + M)^a (`Frequency.SelfLoop.carried_eq`): no smaller bound follows from D, M and the steps at which couplings act; by `most_difference_arrives` the rate bounds the gaps of every law with the strengths (`gap_le_count`). A coupling reading an earlier level adds, across its step, at least what had reached that level (`carried_step_ge_at`); a chain in time order carries what reached its start to its end (`ChainInTime`, `carried_along_in_time`), and a return (`Returns`) is such a chain from a condition back to it. Every return at least doubles what had reached the condition, which arrives once held and once around the loop; strengths are natural numbers, so a positive strength is at least one (`carried_doubles_return`), and R returns multiply it by 2^R (`carried_returns`); so where something has arrived and returns come at steps T i with 2^i outgrowing every polynomial in T i, the most difference that can arrive outgrows every polynomial, whatever the strengths (`runaway_of_returns`). Conversely, where 2^a is bounded by a polynomial so is the carried amount, whatever the strengths (`carried_le_of_rare`); for a self-loop the two meet: it is polynomial exactly where 2^a is (`Frequency.SelfLoop.polynomial_iff`). The sparse schedule marks the steps one less than a power of two, 0, 1, 3, 7, … (`Frequency.Sparse`): the self-loop coupling there recurs, so every finite order misses something (`Frequency.Sparse.inexact`), yet it carries at most D·2^M·(t + 1)^M (`Frequency.Sparse.polynomial`): a loop of recurring couplings need not run away. A coupling that stops by step N (`StopsBy`) does not recur; with every condition listed, where each coupling recurs or stops, the schedule has settled by some step (`settled_of_each`): settling is local. Whether a coupling at step m reads a level from step n on is decided (`Frequency.readsLate`), so given the dichotomy that every decided two-place family has every row true somewhere or some row false throughout (`RecursOrStops`), every schedule settles (`settled_of_recursOrStops`); conversely, every schedule of one condition returning to itself settles exactly where the dichotomy holds (`settled_iff_recursOrStops`), and so for every world with its conditions listed (`settled_every_iff`). The self-loop coupling at the first step a decided sequence is true couples at most once (`Frequency.First.once`), and these schedules all settling gives the limited principle of omniscience (`lpo_of_settled`); the self-loop reading level s once every row up to s has been true somewhere recurs exactly where every row is (`Frequency.Rows.recurs_iff`). The self-loop of `Becoming.Loops` and the loop of `Becoming.Closing` forming at T are instances (`Frequency.SelfLoop.always_carried`, `Frequency.SelfLoop.onset_amount`). *Readings* [AI reading, D43]. Ren asked on 12 October 2026 “how how accurate can we do that uh what is the limitation do we have losses” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-12-move-towards-100.md)); how fast a difference runs away is set by how often couplings act: with natural-number strengths, each positive one at least one, frequent returns make it run away (with strengths below one they need not), and rare coupling steps keep it polynomial whatever the strengths, strength changing only how large; for a self-loop, how often it couples decides exactly. On 16 October Ren called the AI's reading of “only a loop makes a difference run away” right, a reading that left couplings at only some steps open ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-16-loops-readings-and-delegated-choices.md)); that case is now closed both ways: only a loop of the couplings that go on can make it run away, and such a loop need not. That every schedule settles is a principle of reasoning, not a fact the theory asserts or denies of the unfolding; the theorems write a settled schedule in as a premise where they need one. Not derived: which of the bound by returns and the bound by coupling steps is met beyond the self-loop, the spectral radius of given strengths, real amounts (a positive strength may be below one, and strengths shrinking fast enough could keep frequent returns from running away), laws reading infinitely many conditions at a step, and continuous time. See [how-often-couplings-act](../results/records/how-often-couplings-act.md).
**Continuity across dormancy, and identity fixed by relations.** First read which relations of the same unfolding still hold across an inactive step. Given a reading of holding `h`, `Web.persisting h` reads activity by it. A formation that wakes having held before does not arise under this reading. Where the names of what holds continue step by step, there is one name over each held stretch, and the renewing or consuming class of a formation that holds throughout does not change (`Web.steady_held`, `Web.renewingIn_held`, `Web.consumingIn_held`). This is stronger than the earlier two-way continuity of activity and tenure (`Hibernation.readings`), and still does not constrain succession at a step where nothing holds (`Dormancy.held_both`). Dependence and reserve give a seed different readings of its ending (`Seed.held_readings`). Both ends of a census must read holding alike; mixing the readings can fail (`Web.persisting_census`, `Sleeper.held_readings`).

A re-identification of a world keeps balance exactly where what it continues at each step lies within the conservation relation `World.Conserves` (`World.balanced_reidentify_iff`, with no further hypothesis). In a balanced world this is equality of the amounts standing after the step, which is likeness of the exchange frames (`World.balanced_reidentify_iff_alike`). With equality of relata decidable, the exact conditions under which identity, arising and ceasing are fixed under every naming that keeps balance are given by `World.fixed_iff`, `World.arises_fixed_iff` and `World.ceases_fixed_iff`. Where the amounts standing after each step tell all relata apart, the classes are fixed too (`World.classes_fixed`). `Lineage.world_readings` and `Hibernation.world_readings` keep the counterexamples: balance can admit different arisings and classes at once. The reading of holding, and reading conservation as continuation, are AI readings; no numbered decision records them. Not proved: undermining and classes under splits or merges, and sufficient conditions for a mixed census. Record: [identity-across-steps](../results/records/identity-across-steps.md). Study: [section 108](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#108-休眠中的连续与关系所固定的同一--persistence-through-dormancy-and-identity-fixed-by-relations-p87).

<a id="3-认识与探究--knowing-and-inquiry"></a>
## 3. Knowing and inquiry

**The scope of an inquiry is given by the dynamics.** The scope of an inquiry policy is the set of configurations its actual executions reach. A sound policy secures its goal on its scope (`Scope.secures`, [Becoming.Inquiry](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Inquiry.lean)). This narrows a parameter that applications previously supplied for themselves.

**A judgment is a one-step inquiry.** Judgment under uncertainty needs no separate concept. A response admissible at every configuration believed possible is exactly a common response of the “judging process”, which takes one admissible response at the configuration being judged and then closes (`commonResponse_judge_iff`). Robust judgment, correction under observation and safe choice are therefore one common response. Refining observation narrows belief, so it preserves them (`commonResponse_antitone`; the periphery bridge [CorrectionInquiry](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/universal-ethics/generative-capacity/CorrectionInquiry.lean), with no axioms).

**Coupling and testing.** Into a formation couple its support (whether the levels admit it), by threshold, and the levels of the conditions coupled into it by level ([Becoming.Testing](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Testing.lean)). “Couples into” replaces “registers”, which rendered Ren's 识 (shí); under the naming decision of 18 October, 识 is kept for a formation's own course through the lumping its couplings make.

- *Threshold.* A condition coupled in by threshold only passes only whether it meets the need (`Web.alike_of_threshold`). Coupled in by level, its level passes (`Web.observed_level_couples`).
- *Exclusion.* A claim is excluded only through a difference in what couples in (`Web.Excludes.not_of_alike`): a claim and its rival must differ where something can couple in. The author reads this as the structure of falsifiability.
- *Testing is relational.* What the clearer cannot test, the dweller can, and the two together can test what neither can alone (`Clearing.tested_by_dweller`, `Clearing.pooled`).
- *Models.* Without a model, a finite prefix does not exclude a claim about what follows it (`Web.tail_unexcluded`). Relative to a model of the exchange, a formation into which a stock couples by level excludes a false claim at the first step (`Flow.excludes_tail_within`). An exclusion relative to a model is an exclusion of the model and the claim together: a wrong model excludes what holds, and what couples in excludes the model (`Web.excludesWithin_iff_conjunction`, `Stationary`).
- *Judgment on the flow.* A judgment made through a formation's couplings is exactly a common response of the judging process over the class alike through them, and later steps and more coupling preserve it (`Web.class_judge_iff`). “A judgment is a one-step inquiry” above thus reaches the flow.
- *Closed regions and thresholds.* Where the members of a region respond only through their own couplings, what lies beyond the region does not show within it (`Flow.alike_within_closed`). Where the exchange follows a formation's couplings, a difference below threshold stays until the two webs' support diverges (`Flow.subthreshold_alike`, `Flow.difference_changes_after_divergence`). Where others respond to the stock itself, this fails (`Attracting.higher_breaks_first`).

Balance is assumed stepwise only on the conditions coupled in. The response hypotheses are supplied.

*Readings* [AI reading, D26, narrowed]. Feedback through support has three kinds of not-seeing, each within its own scope: below threshold, beyond the horizon, and beyond the coupling closure. Reading a runaway as a loop running within one of them is a heuristic, not a statement. Following Ren, 理 (lǐ, “principle, pattern”) is read as the modeling of the laws of structure and change (see “Learning” below); this replaces the AI's earlier reading “a model answerable to registration”. A model is still tested only through what couples in. Records: [testing-within-registration](../results/records/testing-within-registration.md), [subthreshold-registration](../results/records/subthreshold-registration.md). Study: [section 70](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#70-识与检验p49).

**Experiment.** What an act brings about is a comparison of two histories, with the act and without it, and one unfolding has only one of them ([Becoming.Experiment](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Experiment.lean)). Relative to determinacy (`Determined`: the move fixes the step), the record fixes a move's outcome at a configuration exactly where the run has taken that move there (`Run.fixes_of_takes`). An untaken move is not fixed, even with the whole configuration read (`Run.not_fixes_of_untaken`). So what one's own act would have done otherwise is fixed only where the configuration returns (`Run.fixes_on_return`). A run that keeps its whole history is new at every step and fixes, about its own acts, only what it did (`keepHistory_fixes_nothing_else`). Experiment therefore needs a model of what is irrelevant. Where the step factors through a projection into a determined process, one occasion stands for its whole class of configurations with the same projection (`Run.generalizes`), and within finitely many projected configurations the projection must return (`Run.projection_returns`). The model can be wrong. A wrong projection attributes a difference to a move that makes none; it is never excluded while the move goes in step with what the projection leaves out, and is excluded where the two are varied apart (`Confound`).

*Readings* [AI reading, D25]. Replication is return; a control is the model through which the step factors; variation independent of what is left out is the structural form of randomization. Record: [experiment-needs-return](../results/records/experiment-needs-return.md). Study: [section 72](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#72-实验变动自己的动作检验什么需要什么p53).

**Mechanism.** Experiment needs a model of what is irrelevant (`Becoming.Experiment`). This states when such a model exists ([Becoming.Mechanism](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Mechanism.lean)). A projection π of the configuration is a description. Some determined process on the description admits every step read through π exactly where configurations alike under π go alike: two configurations with the same π reach, by the same move, configurations with the same π (`carries_law_iff`). The image of the step read through π is the only candidate that needs checking (`Image.least`, `image_determined_iff`). This needs no premise and holds of every process; through the identity it is determinacy (`goAlike_id_iff`).

- A law can hold at a coarser description and not at the whole (`Coarse`), or at the whole and not at a coarser description (`Switching`). The description that distinguishes nothing always carries a law (`goAlike_const`), so the content lies in which description carries it.
- Where the next configuration depends only on π, what it does not depend on is re-absorbed within one step (`DependsOnlyOn.reabsorbed`). That a rule holds the outcome (`Law.perturbation_reabsorbed`) is its instance with the rule as the projection.
- A system whose state goes on together with its rule is one determined process on the joint configuration. The state has a law of its own exactly where the rule makes no difference to it (`adaptive_state_law_iff`). Where the rule changes itself, the state can have no law at all (`Switching.no_state_law`).
- A law through a projection, even a true one, is fixed by the record only where it has been taken (`Run.not_fixesThrough_of_goAlike`).
- *Along one run, and exactly* ([Becoming.LawAlongRun](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/LawAlongRun.lean), [a-law-through-a-projection-along-one-run](../results/records/a-law-through-a-projection-along-one-run.md)). Some determined process on the description admits a run's steps up to a step, read through π, exactly where any two of them from configurations alike under π, by the same move, reach configurations alike under π (`Run.law_through_iff`). The record read through π is the least candidate (`Run.record_least`), and once the criterion fails it fails from then on (`Run.no_law_later`). Through the identity at every step it is the law a history shows (`Run.consistent_id_iff`, `exists_law_iff`). A run can be consistent where the process does not go alike (`Constant`). A law at a description is not yet an exact model of finite paths: the image is exact in the sense of `Generative.projection_exact_iff` exactly where configurations alike match each other's steps up to π (`image_exact_iff`, `exact_law_iff`), and going alike does not give it (`Stuck`). Study: [section 181](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#181-a-law-through-a-projection-along-one-run-g3).
- *Through what a formation couples into* ([Becoming.LawThroughCoupling](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/LawThroughCoupling.lean), [a-law-through-coupling-along-one-run](../results/records/a-law-through-coupling-along-one-run.md)). Coupling (`Web.AlikeThrough`) is a relation, not a projection, so the criterion is stated up to an equivalence: some process going alike under it admits a run's steps up to a step exactly where any two of them alike under it, by the same move, end alike (`Run.law_under_iff`), the record read under it being the witness; through equal projections this is the criterion above (`Run.consistentUnder_eq_iff`). With fixed conditions, what a formation couples into at a configuration is such an equivalence, exactly `Web.AlikeThrough` on the runs read through the conditions (`Conditions.alikeThrough_iff`), so `Run.law_through_coupling_iff`. A level the formation does not couple into can decide what it next couples into, however determined the process is, and then no law holds at its coupling along the run (`Unseen.no_law`); coupling into that level restores it (`Unseen.consistent_observing`). Study: [section 185](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#185-a-law-through-what-a-formation-couples-into-along-one-run-g4).

*Readings* [AI reading, D25]. A mechanism is a projection that carries a law. Emergence is configurations going alike at a coarser description. A system that changes its own causality needs no further kind of law: its law is on the joint configuration. Record: [projection-carrying-a-law](../results/records/projection-carrying-a-law.md). Study: [section 88](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#88-机制与标准因果模型承载规律的投影作为过程的因果模型p66p68).

**Standard causal models.** x′ = F(x, u, ε) is a determined process whose move is the input and the noise together (`inputs_determined`). An intervention is a move not taken. Where the run has not taken it, the record does not fix its outcome (`inputs_intervention_unfixed`, an instance of `Becoming.Experiment`), while the model does (`inputs_model_fixes`). Noise is a move not represented: with it forgotten, the process stays determined exactly where the noise makes no difference (`forget_inputs_determined_iff`).

*Readings* [AI reading, D25]. An intervention is fixed by a model, not by the record; `retarget` replaces one structural equation at one configuration and move. Record: [causal-models-as-processes](../results/records/causal-models-as-processes.md). Study: [section 88](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#88-机制与标准因果模型承载规律的投影作为过程的因果模型p66p68).

**Runaway.** A policy through the couplings, with the rest of the exchange fixed, is exchange following what couples in ([Becoming.Runaway](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Runaway.lean), `Flow.exchangeFollows_of_policy`). So every such policy that keeps its support never uses a larger stock coupled into it by threshold only, and one that has used it has already lost its support (`Flow.policy_keeps_difference`, `Flow.policy_uses_after_failure`). Before the break, for every horizon, some world with the stock raised is alike with the actual one through the formation's couplings and lasts past that horizon (`Web.break_not_bounded`): how far off the break is does not couple in. Coupling the stock in by level passes it at once (`Web.observed_raise_couples`).

*Readings* [AI reading, D26]. A runaway is a loop through whose own couplings, before the break, the world is alike with a world with as much more stock as one likes. Avoiding it without giving up what is there needs measurement, a model, or another formation's couplings. Record: [runaway-distance-unregistered](../results/records/runaway-distance-unregistered.md). Study: [section 73](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#73-失控经自身之识的回路所不能识的p55).

**The boundary condition for 无明.** 无明 (wúmíng, “not-seeing”; Sanskrit avidyā) is Ren's term. Seeking is read as activity toward a target met at a state, the target formed within the unfolding ([Becoming.Seeking](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Seeking.lean)).

- *The boundary of enough.* Under a determined law, seeking comes to rest with its target met exactly where it reaches a state that the law leaves unchanged and that meets the target (`Law.rests_iff`).
- *Enough under undetermined laws* ([Becoming.Enough](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Enough.lean), [the-boundary-of-enough-under-undetermined-laws](../results/records/the-boundary-of-enough-under-undetermined-laws.md)). Under an undetermined law a configuration has many runs, and enough parts in two. Some run from a configuration comes to rest with its target met exactly where a configuration reachable from it meets the target and has a step that stays (`Enough.possibly_rests_iff`). Every run from it does exactly where every reachable configuration with a step that stays meets the target and every run from it settles (`Enough.assured_iff`); the second part is of the process alone. Every configuration being able to stay and meeting the target is not enough: with two configurations and every step allowed, the alternating run never settles (`Enough.Free.stays_meet_not_assured`). Under a determined law the two are one and are the boundary above (`Enough.law_possibly_iff`, `Enough.law_possibly_iff_assured`).
- *When the runs settle* ([Becoming.Settling](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Settling.lean), [when-the-runs-of-a-process-settle](../results/records/when-the-runs-of-a-process-settle.md)). A run that changes within a loop, between two visits of one configuration, gives a run from the same configuration that goes round the loop and keeps changing (`Settling.recurs_of_loop`). Along a run a list holds, change that recurs is a change within a loop (`Settling.loop_of_recurs`). So in a world a list holds, some run from a configuration keeps changing exactly where a configuration reachable from it has a step to another configuration from which it can be reached again (`Settling.keeps_changing_iff`), and where assured enough holds there is no such step (`Settling.assured_no_undoable`). That no run keeps changing does not give a step from which each run is settled: with two configurations and no way back from the second, no run keeps changing, and every run settling is the limited principle of omniscience (`Settling.OneWay.never_keeps_changing`, `Settling.OneWay.lpo_of_settles`), which the core does not assume.
- *The last change* ([Becoming.LastChange](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/LastChange.lean), [the-last-change-of-a-run](../results/records/the-last-change-of-a-run.md)). Along a run a list of N configurations holds, with no change within a loop, there are fewer than N changes (`LastChange.changes_lt`). Such a run settles exactly where, at every step, it is decided whether it changes again (`LastChange.settles_iff_decided`, with decidable configurations), and with no premise beyond decidable configurations it is not not settled (`LastChange.not_not_settles`). With the limited principle of omniscience it settles, and in a world a list holds every run from a configuration with no reachable change that can be undone settles; that this holds for every process of decidable configurations in such a world is exactly the limited principle of omniscience (`LastChange.settles_of_no_undoable`, `LastChange.lpo_iff_settles`). With that principle, every run from a configuration comes to rest with its target met exactly where every reachable configuration with a step that stays meets the target and no reachable change can be undone (`LastChange.assured_iff_of_lpo`).
- *Targets that cannot rest.* “More than now” is met only by change; met at every step, it needs an open world, one whose states no list holds (`more_needs_open`). “Above all others” is met at a state by at most one seeker, so two such seekers never rest together (`aboveAll_unique`, `Law.aboveAll_never_rests`).
- *Exhaustion.* Where what stands is listed, rising without end needs steps, at arbitrarily late times, at which what stands does not run down: drawing from beyond, or exchange that does not dissipate (`Flow.more_needs_unrun`). Where what stands runs down at every step from some step on, rises do not recur: the pursuit ends by exhaustion, not by reaching enough (`Flow.more_exhausted`).
- *Correction.* Losses reach a seeker only through what couples into it (`Becoming.Testing`, `Becoming.Runaway`); its course through the lumping those couplings make is 识. So locked seeking is not corrected by its own feedback. Coupling that reaches what support does not mark corrects it: measurement, a model, or others.
- *Capture* ([Becoming.Capture](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Capture.lean)). Where every carrier of a target leaves at least a offspring and every other parent at most b, with b < a, the carriers' share among the offspring exceeds their share among the parents (`share_rises`). This is an averaging inequality, not the selection term of Price's equation, which `Becoming.Evolution` leaves unstated. Where what stands runs down without end, descent and selection stop (`Flow.descent_exhausted`). Where the next configuration depends on the rule, perturbing the state alone is re-absorbed (`Law.perturbation_reabsorbed`).

*Readings* [AI reading, D26]. 无明 is the not-seeing that keeps seeking locked onto a target that cannot be met at rest. 明 (míng, “seeing, clarity”) is coupling that reaches what support does not mark. Knowing enough and knowing where to stop (知足, 知止) are targets within the boundary of enough. Capture is a target formed by a selection that rewards what cannot rest and held by rules the winners write. Domination itself is not among what is excluded. These readings rest on little mathematics: the theorems above are short, and the weight is carried by the readings. Records: [seeking-boundary](../results/records/seeking-boundary.md), [target-capture](../results/records/target-capture.md). Study: [section 83](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#83-无明的边界条件寻求何处能静止于够p62).

*Readings* [AI reading, D41, D42]. Enough is a relation of the process with the target. Possible enough is what some course allows, whatever settles which course is taken, not what a seeker can bring about; assured enough adds a part of the process alone, that every run settles. Failing to rest therefore has two sources: the target, one that cannot be met at rest, which is 无明 as read above; and the process, runs that keep changing, which is not 无明 and is named by its structure, a reachable change that can be undone. In a world a list holds the process decides whether its runs keep changing, but knowing that a run will not change for ever is not knowing when it has stopped: the count of its changes is bounded, the step of its last change is not. Records: [the-boundary-of-enough-under-undetermined-laws](../results/records/the-boundary-of-enough-under-undetermined-laws.md), [when-the-runs-of-a-process-settle](../results/records/when-the-runs-of-a-process-settle.md), [the-last-change-of-a-run](../results/records/the-last-change-of-a-run.md). Study: [section 174](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#174-the-boundary-of-enough-under-undetermined-laws-s1), [section 175](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#175-when-the-runs-of-a-process-settle-s2), [section 178](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#178-the-last-change-of-a-run-s3).

**Selection through registration.** A selection is any claim about level histories: which are kept, rewarded or leave more descendants. It goes through a selector's registration when histories the selector registers alike are selected alike ([Becoming.Selection](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Selection.lean), `Web.SelectsThrough`).

- Two histories are selected alike by every selection through `g`'s registration exactly when `g` registers them alike, with no premise (`Web.selects_alike_iff`). Where `g` registers them apart, `g`'s belief is a selection that separates them (`Web.separates`).
- So, with the rest of the exchange fixed, every selection through the registration of a formation within the registration closure selects the world with and the world without a part beyond the closure alike. This holds also for the formations within the closure pooled (`Flow.unselected_beyond_coupling`, `Flow.unselected_beyond_coupling_all`).
- The gap is the reach closure less the registration closure. In `Clearing` the dweller lies within the clearer's reach closure and beyond its registration closure. Every selection through the clearer's registration selects the cleared and the spared world alike at every step, while the dweller is ended in only one of them (`Clearing.unselected`). The dweller's registration, alone or pooled with the clearer's, separates the two worlds (`Clearing.selected_by_dweller`, `Clearing.selected_pooled`).
- *The gap for one's own act* ([Becoming.SelectionGap](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/SelectionGap.lean), [the-gap-for-ones-own-act](../results/records/the-gap-for-ones-own-act.md)). Where the rest follows one law of exchange reading through coupling, an act whose difference stays beyond the actor's coupling closure is selected alike, with and without it, by every selection through any member of the closure, the actor among them, at every step (`Flow.act_unselected`, `Flow.act_unselected_of_coupled`). So a consequence of the act, holding with it and failing without it, is a selection through no member of the closure (`Flow.consequence_unselected`). The rest's response is derived from the law (`ExchangeLaw.Through.responds`), not supplied; where both acts are laws, the courses the law generates with each are compared and nothing is supplied (`act_courses_unselected`). `Clearing` is a world in which such an act ends a formation within the actor's reach. Study: [section 176](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#176-the-gap-for-ones-own-act-g1).
- In `Diversion`, a flow that reaches beyond the registration closure, taking from the source a dweller depends on to raise a store the taker registers, is selected over the restrained flow by a selection through the taker's registration. Every selection through that registration treats it alike with a raise that does not reach beyond (`Diversion.favours`, `Diversion.blind_to_source`).
- *Raising what couples in draws from beyond* ([Becoming.SelectionReach](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/SelectionReach.lean), [raising-what-couples-in-draws-from-beyond](../results/records/raising-what-couples-in-draws-from-beyond.md)). The favour in `Diversion` is a law of any balanced flow. Where a family containing what couples in gains nothing across a step (nothing from nothing, `Flow.GainlessOn`), a rise of what couples in has a formation that gives it more than it takes from it and takes from beyond it (`Flow.Balances.rise_draws_beyond`); with equality decided, from a named condition outside it (`Flow.Balances.rise_takes_beyond`); and it is paid from beyond in full, the rise at most what is taken beyond it (`Flow.Balances.rise_paid_from_beyond`). So, where the family lists what a group couples into, every selection holding only where it rises holds only on flows in which a formation takes from a condition no member of the group couples into (`Flow.favours_reaching_beyond`). `Diversion` is an instance (`Diversion.draws_beyond`), and its supplying flow, which gains, raises the store with nothing taken: the premise binds (`Diversion.supplying_raises_untaken`). Study: [section 177](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#177-raising-what-couples-in-draws-from-beyond-g2).
- *What pooled coupling reaches* ([Becoming.SelectionPool](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/SelectionPool.lean), [what-pooled-coupling-reaches](../results/records/what-pooled-coupling-reaches.md)). A pool's coupling closure is the union of its members' (`withinClosureOf_iff`). With the rest of the exchange fixed and balance on what the closure couples into, every selection through the pooled coupling of any group within it selects the levels with and without a part beyond it alike (`Flow.unselected_beyond_pool`); the pooled form above is the case of a pool within one formation's closure (`Flow.unselected_beyond_coupling_all_of_pool`). Some pooled selection separates two histories exactly when the members do not all couple them alike (`Web.separates_all_iff`), and, with the members listed and each member's coupling decided, exactly when some member couples them apart (`Web.separates_all_iff_member`). An act whose difference stays beyond a pool's coupling closure, the pool's own joint act included, is selected alike by every selection through the pooled coupling of any group within the closure (`Flow.act_unselected_pool`, `act_courses_unselected_pool`); a formation whose admission the act changes lies beyond the closure and separates the act through every pool holding it (`Flow.act_selected_by_pool`). In `Clearing`, with the rest's law derived, pooling with the dweller closes the clearer's gap (`Clearing.pool_closes_gap`). Stated in §7.3 and §7.4. Study: [section 179](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#179-what-pooled-coupling-reaches-m2).
- *What a pool's acts respond to* ([Becoming.PoolResponse](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/PoolResponse.lean), [what-a-pools-acts-respond-to](../results/records/what-a-pools-acts-respond-to.md)). Where a law of exchange reads through the coupling of each formation outside a part, what a group outside the part decides by its acts is a selection through its pooled coupling (`ExchangeLaw.Through.selectsThroughAll`), so the laws above hold of what a pool does. The bound is exact where the coupling classes are decided (`ExchangeLaw.acts_alike_all_iff`), and coupling does not fix the response: the law that exchanges nothing reads through every coupling (`ExchangeLaw.response_not_fixed_by_coupling`). Under the conditions of the gap for a pool's act, nothing within the pool's coupling closure outside the acting part responds to the act (`Flow.response_alike_pool`; with both acts laws, `act_courses_response_alike_pool`). A pool meets a part's responsive reach exactly where the part meets the pool's closure (`Flow.pool_within_response_iff`), and no member responds before a difference has entered what it couples into (`TimedResponse.pool_alike_before`). In `Clearing` what every pool holding the dweller separates goes unresponded outside the clearer's part (`Clearing.separated_unresponded`); in `Loop` a response returns through the actor's coupling closure (`Loop.response_returns`). Stated in §7.4 and §7.7. Study: [section 184](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#184-what-a-pools-acts-respond-to-m3).
- Where a selection gives the parents it holds for more offspring, their share rises among the offspring, an application of `share_rises` (`share_rises_through`).

*Readings* [AI reading, D25]. Any selection decided by what couples in (keeping, rewarding, reproducing, training) reaches exactly what couples into the selector. The gap closes only through dependence, coupling by level, or another's couplings pooled. Where reaching beyond raises what couples in, selection favours it. Read for AI training, the reward process is the selector and training reaches only what couples into the reward; the AI connects this with the title of Ren's paper “When Capability Outruns Correction”. Record: [selection-through-registration](../results/records/selection-through-registration.md). Study: [section 86](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#86-经识的选择选择恰及选择者所识p64).

**Selection revises the lumping.** Reconstruction within one process can change what later activity distinguishes. Where the old lumping is an equivalence, every selection expressible through the old lumping remains expressible through the new one exactly when new indistinguishability implies old indistinguishability. The condition composes along actual histories and directly reuses the selection through the couplings of `Web`. One instance, controlled by current support and the state of an apparatus, actually forms a distinguishing relation, replenishes the support used up and changes the response; the process also allows the relation to be closed. Discrimination, retained record and executable revision do not substitute for one another. The construction recipe, the reconstruction preference, the target and the replenishment remain supplied; that they are acquired by the process, or attract generally, is not derived. Record: [selection-under-registration-revision](../results/records/selection-under-registration-revision.md). Study: [the common-organization study](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/META-ATTRACTOR-RECONCILIATION.md#registration-revision-on-the-spine).

**Consequences revise the next reference plan.** The interface left open by the fixed instance above now has a conditional construction. An actual failure receipt changes the retained contact sequence. The controller executes the new sequence and acquires references; a new record is installed only once a check passes, and then the actual response changes. A mismatch elsewhere revises the plan again, and support is not reset across the history. The newly available distinctions rejoin the same `Through` by the contact domain actually installed, and preservation of old selections directly reuses the revision of the lumping above. Frozen old plans, erased receipts, insufficient support, references that do not separate, and drift after the check keep their failures. Contact primitives, faithful return, the Boolean or stable correspondence and the revision rule remain supplied. Completing this local interface is not arbitrary invention of senses, nor the whole meta-attractor theory. Record: [consequence-revised-reference-plan](../results/records/consequence-revised-reference-plan.md). Study: [handover and counter-review](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/META-ATTRACTOR-RECONCILIATION.md#consequences-revise-reference-plans).

**Formation and continuing revision joined.** The [integrated account](../../meta-attractor/COMMON-ORGANIZATION.md#integrated-account) directly reuses the primitive operations of the reference-plan revision. Actual response first forms from an empty method and a reference apparatus; the productive return of successful activity then pays for succession and the next round of revision. `continuing` is an actual infinite `Run` in the same controller: every round acquires fresh references and actually uses them, and cumulative use and return are not reset. The account has 36 new theorems and 62 source declarations, with empty axiom reports. Cutting the return, freezing the method, or exhausting a tight reserve breaks the continuation; retiring an old contact really loses a distinction. The return law, faithful contact, the task and the correspondence of succession remain explicit. Continuing activity is not taken as normative warrant, nor as perpetual motion from a closed finite stock.

**Learning: induction, generalization, training and the meta level.** Ren asked how completely the meta level, artificial neural networks, cognition and the wisdom about learning are explained, and then wrote: “please, fully complete, 我觉得理更像是对结构和变化的规律的建模 finish wisdom root, and resolve the undissolved” (the Chinese: “I think 理 is more like the modeling of the laws of structure and change”; [source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-12-learning-wisdom-root-and-induction.md); [Becoming.Learning](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Learning.lean)). A course is a sequence of values, such as a determined law's orbit read through a map. An extrapolation is any map from the prefix before step t to a proposed value or to silence.

- *No prefix fixes the next value.* With no premise, no prefix fixes the next value, even among finite worlds (`no_prefix_fixes_next`, `no_prefix_fixes_next_finite`). For every extrapolation, some course answers it at every step and each value it proposes misses; that course keeps its whole prefix, so it is open (`diagonal_misses`, `diagonal_unrested`, `diagonal_open`).
- *Extrapolation by elimination.* Hold candidates in order and move to the next exactly where the one held misses. Such an extrapolation never passes a candidate that accords (`index_le_of_accords`).
- *Finite worlds.* Where a list holds a determined law's orbit, its course through any map repeats periodically from some step on (`lasso_of_held`). Consider an extrapolation given neither the configurations nor their number that reads only a course through a map whose values it can tell apart. It misses fewer than `tri N` times for N configurations; it proposes at every step from step N on; and it is not the case that it misses at arbitrarily late steps (`held_byElimination_bounded`). That it comes to rest cannot be refuted (`held_rest_unrefuted`, a double negation). It rests only on such courses (`lasso_of_rest`), and its prefix never shows that it has come to rest (`no_prefix_fixes_next_finite`).
- *Classes.* Learning over classes is the same learning over a merged enumeration, and every merging has its diagonal sequence (`merged_bound`, `merged_unrested`).
- *理 as modeling.* Following Ren, 理 is read as the modeling of the laws of structure and change. A model of structure is a lumping (`Lumped`); a model of change is a lumping across which the step goes alike; the two are one notion (`goAlike_iff_lumped`).
- *Generalization.* Among the functions that go alike across a lumping, finite data fix an untried value exactly where it is lumped with some observed input (`determines_lumped_iff`). The generalization is right exactly where the world takes one value at the two (`lumped_right_iff`). An outcome of a law of change transfers exactly where the two configurations go alike by the move (`transfer_right_iff`). The class of all functions is free at every untried input, so data alone fix nothing there (`every_determines_nothing`).
- *Training.* Training is selection through the data (`Training.loss_through`; the selection through the couplings of `Becoming.Selection` is an instance). In a class free beyond the data, it leaves the untried value as free as the class (`Training.unselected_beyond_data`). A fit is right beyond the data exactly where the class is determined there by the data (`Training.fit_right_iff`).
- *Periphery bridge.* [LearningCorrespondence](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/universal-ethics/evolution-connectionism/LearningCorrespondence.lean) (periphery; standard Lean axioms) reads the acquisition chain's transfer as generalization through a lumping of laws (`uniform_determines`, `nonuniform_not_determined`). Hebbian development and the route of updating are the `adaptive` systems of `Becoming.Mechanism`: activity alone, or the network alone, has no law exactly where the weights or the route make a difference (`hebbian_no_activity_law`, `updating_no_network_law`).

*Readings* [AI reading, D26]. Induction is resolved into laws, not certified. The uniformity of nature is not a premise of the theory. It is the local hypothesis `Lumped`, named where it is used: that the world goes alike across what the model lumps. For a trained network the class is what training can reach, and generalization comes from the lumping that architecture and updates impose, not from the loss. The meta level is learning again, with no top. Records: [learning-by-refutation](../results/records/learning-by-refutation.md), [generalization-through-lumping](../results/records/generalization-through-lumping.md). Study: [section 92](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#92-学习归纳推广训练与元层次p73p74).

**The split that makes sense, and states read off a history.** Ren asked what the “make sense” of a split itself is. With the definition of `Becoming.Unfolding`, a description makes sense when it identifies exactly the alike configurations. For a determined law read through a map, alike is the same future through the map (`Law.alike_iff_sameFuture`).

- The coarsest split that keeps the map's value and carries a law makes sense and loses nothing the map takes (`Law.identifiesAlike_of_law`, `Law.sameFuture_of_law`, [Becoming.Description](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Description.lean)). Carrying a law is not enough: the split that distinguishes nothing always carries a law and makes sense only where the map's value never differs (`Law.const_identifiesAlike_iff`).
- The map's present value makes sense by itself exactly where it carries a law, a condition on one step (`Law.now_identifiesAlike_iff_goAlike`). A part of the map's value alone makes sense exactly where the rest makes no difference to its next value (`Law.part_identifiesAlike_iff_step`). Where a controller's rule changes with it, it makes sense exactly where the rule makes no difference to it (`Law.adaptive_identifiesAlike_iff`, the condition of `adaptive_state_law_iff` in `Becoming.Mechanism`). Cognition or a controller lies within the split that makes sense exactly where it makes a difference through the map.
- *States read off a history.* States are not given with configurations; they are read off a history of values. Two moments are one state when every value after them is alike (`SameAhead`). Every history of values is a determined law read through a map: the law of moments, which uses only the succession of steps (the representation's shared step order). Its alike moments are exactly the moments of one state (`alike_moments_iff`). The law of moments is the successor map on step indices, so this theorem is close to definitional.
- So, beyond the succession of steps, that an unfolding has a law is not a premise, and a law's content is its compression. There are finitely many states exactly where the history repeats from some moment on (`finitely_many_states_iff`, through a pigeonhole up to a relation with no decision, `shared_below`). A returning configuration has finitely many states through every map (`Law.Returns.states`). The course of two-valued negation has exactly two states, each defined through the other (`Negation.two_states`, `Negation.each_through_other`).

Record: [the-split-that-makes-sense](../results/records/the-split-that-makes-sense.md). Study: [section 94](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#94-描述保留什么失去什么分离关系的生灭与有意义的切分p70).

**A family of readings within what it reads.** What a world reads of itself is read by its parts, and the parts are within what is read ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-12-framing-and-pairs.md)).

- *The diagonal.* A family of readings indexed by what it reads misses a definite reading for any map that moves every value (`Diagonal.gap_unsaid`, Lawvere's diagonal, [Becoming.Diagonal](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Diagonal.lean); the periphery's `RootCoverageBoundary` has the same diagonal). A claim is never alike with its negation, so no family of claims says every reading, and no frame realizes with its own configurations every claim about its configurations (`Diagonal.claims_incomplete`, `Frame.self_incomplete`).
- *The limit lies exactly where there is something to tell apart.* For values compared by equality, a family of readings within what it reads says every reading exactly where nothing among the values can be told apart (`Diagonal.complete_iff_alike`). A family that says every reading gives every map a fixed point (`Diagonal.fixed_of_complete`). So containing itself is possible; what is not possible is holding every reading at once in one description inside.
- *Completing is an unfolding.* A family that keeps what it said and next says what it missed is incomplete at every step; each gap is new and never returns, and no finite stock holds what it misses. Such an unfolding exists (`Diagonal.Unfolds.incomplete`, `Diagonal.Unfolds.never_returns`, `Diagonal.Unfolds.unheld`, `Diagonal.unfolding_open`).
- *The liar.* Read as revision, the liar never rests; two-valued, it returns every two steps. It is a rhythm with no still value, not a contradiction (`Diagonal.revision_never_rests`, `Diagonal.negation_returns`).

*Readings* [AI reading, D33; Ren agreed with the reply containing them]. Of Ren's speculation that the full truth can be derived situationally: from within, any given truth can be said at the next step, and not all of it from one situation. The limit is the projection's, holding the whole in one description inside it; it is not something beyond the truth. Record: [registration-within-what-it-registers](../results/records/registration-within-what-it-registers.md). Study: [section 95](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#95-在所识之中的识对角线补全的展开与没有静值的节律p71).

**Claims shown in context.** Ren asked whether what the framing loses is like “wave/particle” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-12-framing-and-pairs.md)). By type, the frame reads claims as free of context: what is shown of a claim does not depend on what is shown with it. This is named here as a commitment of the representation, with the condition under which it fits ([Becoming.Context](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Context.lean)).

- Claims are shown in contexts. What the contexts show glues exactly where any two contexts one claim lies within show it alike (`Contextual.glues_iff_agree`). A frame of context-free claims fits exactly where what the contexts show glues at every configuration (`Contextual.fitsFree_iff`). The frame indexed by context always fits (`Contextual.indexed_fits`).
- Some worlds glue at no configuration: Specker's three boxes and the Mermin–Peres square. Each is consistent within every context, no context forces any claim, and there is no context-free reading (`Specker.noGlobal`, `Square.noGlobal`).
- Nine observables on two qubits realize the square. No assignment of values is jointly shown on every line by states, where states are represented as nonzero vectors with four integer components (`no_context_free_values`, [Becoming.Quantum](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Quantum.lean)). This is narrower than the full Hilbert space; what quantum states show, so represented, is of this kind.
- Parts fail a whole in two ways, on the same three pairs: by not giving the whole back (the parity of `Becoming.Description`, `evenBoxes_not_determined`), and by allowing what no whole has (`Specker.no_whole`).

*Readings* [AI reading, D33; Ren agreed with the reply containing them]. Wave and particle are both projections onto context-free values. A value is what a context shows, that is, a relation. The truth of Ren's speculation is relational. Record: [contextual-registration](../results/records/contextual-registration.md). Study: [section 96](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#96-语境中的识粘合不依语境的主张作为承诺与部分失去整体的两种方式p72).

**Identification: what a bounded prefix fixes.** Ren speculated that “the full "truth" can be derived, situationally, including the cognition/controller part” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-12-framing-and-pairs.md)). Ren later wrote that “reality is always situational” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-15-situational-and-the-whole-core.md)). What follows says what a finite prefix fixes where the situation has a bound on its number of states ([Becoming.Identification](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Identification.lean)).

- *Two histories.* Two histories of values with at most N and N′ states that agree before N + N′ − 1 agree everywhere, so a prefix of that length fixes the course (`Identification.bounded_agree`). The proof goes through a two-periods lemma after Fine and Wilf, in a form counting states rather than the classical form with the greatest common divisor (`Identification.period_extends`). For every pair of bounds, no shorter prefix suffices (`Identification.bounded_agree_exact`).
- *Given the bound, induction is deduction.* A lasso candidate within N that does not miss on the prefix of length 2N − 1 is the law of the whole course (`Identification.bounded_induction`). The extrapolation given the bound misses nowhere and is right at every step from 2N − 1 on, and no extrapolation can always propose earlier (`Identification.withBound_exact`, `Identification.withBound_earliest`).
- *Not given the bound.* The extrapolation not given the bound (extrapolation by elimination, `Becoming.Learning`) also rests, at a step the bound gives: it is right at every step from N + (2N − 1)·tri N on (`Identification.held_byElimination_rests`), though its prefix never shows this. That step is cubic in N. On two-valued negation along a cycle of N = 2m + 3 places, broken once in every turn, it misses at step (N³ − N)/4, each even shift it passes costing a whole turn (`Identification.byElimination_missed_late`, `Identification.byElimination_rest_cubic`). Where there is no bound, no prefix fixes the next value.
- *The rest step needs no bound.* The extrapolation by the least bound is given no bound. It proposes by the first candidate borne out within the least bound within which the prefix bears out some lasso candidate. On every history with at most N states it is right at every step from 2N − 1 on, for every N (`Identification.leastBound_exact`). No extrapolation, given the bound or not, is right at step 2N − 2 on every such history (`Identification.rest_earliest`). So 2N − 1 is the optimal rest step, and the bound does not move it. What the bound buys is silence: an extrapolation right at step 1 on every one-state history cannot miss nowhere on every two-state history (`Identification.rest_without_bound_missed`).
- *Controllers within the world.* A controller in the world is within what is read. A world that answers the extrapolation given the bound has more states than the bound (`Identification.answer_exceeds_bound`), while the liar's rhythm is derived from step 3 on (`Identification.negation_derived`).
- *Naming by a finite future.* In a world a list holds, naming each configuration by its future through the map for 2N − 1 steps makes sense and carries the law, with no quotient axiom (`Law.name_identifiesAlike`, `Law.name_carries`). Naming by the whole future would need function extensionality.
- *Moore's bound.* Within one world the bound is sharper. Under a determined law, take a list of N members closed under the law, with the members' values compared decidably. Two members have the same future through the map exactly where they agree within N − 1 steps (Moore's bound, `Law.sameFuture_iff_classes`). Agreeing within k + 1 steps is a chain of ever finer equivalences: members with different values give two classes, each strict refinement adds a class, and there are at most N classes (`Refining.steady_within` of [Becoming.Refinement](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Refinement.lean)). The bound is exact for every N ≥ 2 (`Law.sameFuture_classes_exact`).
- *No decision needed.* The bound needs no decision: what is decided is the positions, not the values. Read the law on the positions of the list, whose equality is settled. Merge, pair by pair, the positions that agreements in the prefix join; this gives a labelling of the positions in which each label has one value. Moore's bound holds for the labelling and carries back to the values (`Law.sameFuture_iff_within`, no premise). The same holds for two moments of one history (`Identification.sameAhead_iff_within`).
- *Tests.* Tests are bounded prefixes too. Under a determined law read through a map, in a world of N configurations, two configurations pass the same tests (`SameTests` of `Becoming.Histories`) exactly where their futures through the map agree within N − 1 steps. This is exact for a world wholly held by N configurations. Between two worlds the bound is N + N′ − 1, exact for every pair ([Becoming.Distinguishing](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Distinguishing.lean): `Law.sameTests_iff_within`, `frameBy_within_exact`, `Law.sameTests_iff_below_across`, `frameBy_across_exact`). Between frames whose moves carry the map's value, likeness to depth k is exactly agreement within k steps, so the depth N + N′ − 1 between branching frames is also exact for every pair (`labelled_across_exact`). Read as one world, two histories have Moore's bound N + N′ − 1, the bound of Fine and Wilf.

“Situationally” is not read as the bound, since Ren wrote that reality is always situational (15 October 2026). The bound is the condition under which a situation's finite prefix fixes the whole course through the map. *Reading* [AI reading, D34]: a finite prefix is a projection, and the bound makes it lose nothing of the course. Record: [identification-where-bounded](../results/records/identification-where-bounded.md). Study: [section 97](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#97-辨识有界的记录确定什么p83).

**What an extrapolation can learn through a map.** An extrapolation reads a course through a map and proposes what the map does not give. Take a map `reg` of configurations and a feature `val`. An extrapolation proposes at step t from the prefix before t of the course through the map, and rests on the feature from step T when it proposes the feature's value at every step from T on ([Becoming.Learnable](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Learnable.lean)).

- *No premise.* Two configurations with the same registered future give every learner the same record at every step (`prefixes_same`). So at a step where their targets differ, a claim is right on at most one of them (`claim_not_right_on_both`). With the target's values decidable, it is refuted on one, while the two records are equal everywhere and do not show which (`missed_on_one`).
- A learner resting on both has targets that agree from some step on (`agree_of_rests_both`). Where the targets differ at arbitrarily late steps, no learner rests on both, already where the law leaves both configurations unchanged (`not_rests_on_both`, `still_not_rests_on_both`).
- Where the target is read off the registration (`val s = g (reg s)`), the lasso learner reading registered values is given neither the configurations, nor their number, nor any value of the target. In every world a list holds, it rests from the identification bound on (`rests_through`).
- *Exactly.* In a world a list holds, some learner reading the registration rests at every configuration exactly where configurations with the same registered future have targets agreeing from some step on (`learnable_iff`). The necessity side holds of every learner, whatever it is given.
