The learner on the sufficiency side is given the world and the target (`fromLaw`); so this is an identifiability condition, not a learning procedure that finds the target unaided.
- Targets that go alike across the registration (`Lumped`) are always learnable (`learnable_of_lumped`). Where the law leaves every configuration unchanged, these are exactly the learnable targets (`still_learnable_iff_lumped`): the lumping through which `Becoming.Learning` states generalization.
- *Given nothing, the future is fixed* ([Becoming.Unrolling](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Unrolling.lean), [what-an-extrapolation-given-nothing-fixes](../results/records/what-an-extrapolation-given-nothing-fixes.md)). Unroll the lasso learner given nothing (the extrapolation by elimination over the lasso candidates) on its own proposals, putting each proposed value at the front of the prefix. In every world a list holds, from the identification bound on, it then proposes the value through the map at every step ahead (`Learnable.ahead_fixes`). Over every course no prefix fixes the next value (`Learning.no_prefix_fixes_next`); here a list holds the world.
- *Learnable exactly where a reading of the future.* A target is learnable exactly where some reading of futures, respecting values at each step, is the target from some step on along every orbit (`Learnable.learnable_iff_reading`). The reading on the necessity side is given the world, the law and the target (`Learnable.fromWorld`): a configuration as many steps on as the list is long returns, and returning configurations whose targets agree from some step on have equal targets (`Learnable.eq_of_returns`). On the sufficiency side the learner given only the reading learns the target (`Learnable.reading_rests_from`).
- *Given nothing, not every learnable target.* No learner given nothing rests on every learnable target: in a one-configuration world the targets `true` and `false` are each learnable, with the same prefixes (`Learnable.given_nothing_not_every`). The same three hold along the runs of the law (`LearnableRuns.ahead_fixes`, `LearnableRuns.learnable_iff_reading`, `LearnableRuns.given_nothing_not_every`).
- *What finitely many labels fix* ([Becoming.Labels](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Labels.lean), [what-finitely-many-labels-fix](../results/records/what-finitely-many-labels-fix.md)). A table of labelled names is finite: a name is a finite future through the map, as `Law.name` writes it, and a label is a value of the feature. The extrapolation given a table unrolls the extrapolation given nothing and proposes the label of the first name its proposed future begins with (`Learnable.byTable`). It is given neither the configurations, nor how many there are, nor the law. In a world a list of N holds, take each configuration as many steps on as the list is long, name it by its first N − 1 values through the map (Moore's bound, `Law.sameFuture_iff_within`) and label it with its feature. This table of the world rests in accord with every learnable feature from the bound of the extrapolation given nothing on (`Learnable.table_rests`). So a feature is learnable exactly where the extrapolation given some table of at most N labelled names of N − 1 values learns it (`Learnable.learnable_iff_table`). Every value the feature takes once it rests is a label (`Learnable.label_of_rests`). Where the map also carries the feature, the extrapolation given nothing learns it, and the labels before the bound fix every later one (`Learnable.rests_with_labels`, `Learnable.ahead_with_labels`). The same holds along the runs of the law (`LearnableRuns.table_rests`, `LearnableRuns.learnable_iff_table`, `LearnableRuns.labels_of_still`, `LearnableRuns.rests_with_labels`, `LearnableRuns.ahead_with_labels`).

*Readings* [AI reading, D26]. The map bounds what is learned. This is the not-seeing below threshold of `Becoming.Testing`, stated for extrapolations; what lies beyond the map is reached only by widening it. Record: [learnable-through-registration](../results/records/learnable-through-registration.md). Study: [section 119](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#119-经其识所能学者).

*Reading* [AI reading, D40]. The prefix alone fixes the future through the map; what a learnable target adds to it is a reading of that future, which a learner given nothing does not have. So what `fromLaw` is given beyond the prefix serves only as a reading of a future the prefix already fixes. The witness that no learner given nothing rests on every learnable target is elementary; the content is what the prefix does fix. This holds in worlds a list holds: over every course, no prefix fixes the next value. That reading is a finite object: at most N labelled names of N − 1 values, whose labels are values the feature takes. Records: [what-an-extrapolation-given-nothing-fixes](../results/records/what-an-extrapolation-given-nothing-fixes.md), [what-finitely-many-labels-fix](../results/records/what-finitely-many-labels-fix.md). Study: [section 173](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#173-what-an-extrapolation-given-nothing-fixes-l1), [section 182](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#182-what-finitely-many-labels-fix-l2).

**Identification where runs part, and typicality by counting.** The identification line is stated for determined laws. Ren, 15 October 2026: “"What this line doesn’t reach" is also worth doing” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-15-situational-and-the-whole-core.md)). A process fixes only which steps are admissible, and from one configuration its runs can go different ways. Two runs part at a step where they register the same before it and differ at it; their records before it are one record, so no learner, whatever it is given, the world included, is right on both (`Parting.not_right_on_both`, [Becoming.Parting](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Parting.lean)). Where no two runs from a configuration part, not parting is kept along runs; on a run held by a list of N configurations the registered course has at most N states, and the learner by the least bound, given nothing, is right from N + N − 1 on, and no sooner (`Parting.course_statesBelow`, `Parting.leastBound_right`, `Parting.rest_earliest_runs`). So the identification line holds for every process exactly where runs do not part: a determined law is an instance (`Parting.unparted_of_determined`), and so is a branching that does not show in what is registered (`Parting.free_unparted_const`). Where runs part, the bound bounds nothing: under free branching every course is a run's course, after every record two runs part, so no record shows that a world will not part; every learner has a run on which it is never right; and a run on two configurations has a course with no bound on its states (`Parting.free_parts_after`, `Parting.free_never_right`, `Parting.free_unbounded`). Nor does a record fix weights: it registers a course, not a weight. With two values counted by natural weights p and q, the weighted squared deviations (q·a − p·b)² of the courses of n steps add up exactly to n·p·q·(p + q)ⁿ, so the courses deviating by at least d weigh, times d, at most that (`Typicality.spread`, `Typicality.typical`, [Becoming.Typicality](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Typicality.lean)): most courses show the weights, the counting form of the law of large numbers, with no premise; the weights are `Becoming.Weighted`'s counting weights of free branching (`Typicality.sum_courses_weight`). That the course which happens is among the most is not derived: every course is a run. Of the two AI readings first given here (“at a parting the branch is settled only when taken”; “reading weights as chances adds the premise that the course which happens is typical”), Ren wrote on 16 October 2026: “we are describing what everything obeys, but at every moment, the actual change depends on the situation … just saying we are all one doesn't make it true, the situation makes it true” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-16-the-situation-makes-it-true.md)). Read so (the AI's reading of Ren's words, which Ren took on 16 October 2026: “yes, i take those readings”): everything registered is derived situationally, as far as runs do not part; at a parting neither the record nor the supplied law fixes which way the run goes, and the situation fixes it; weights count situations, as the next paragraph states. Records: [identification-where-runs-part](../results/records/identification-where-runs-part.md), [typicality-by-counting](../results/records/typicality-by-counting.md). Study: [section 140](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#140-运行分开之处的辨识计数中的典型p116).

**Weights count situations.** Ren, 16 October 2026: “we are describing what everything obeys, but at every moment, the actual change depends on the situation … just saying we are all one doesn't make it true, the situation makes it true” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-16-the-situation-makes-it-true.md)). `Becoming.Weighted` does not say where weights come from, and `Becoming.Typicality` does not derive that the course which happens is typical. This states, with no premise, what natural weights are when each step is taken in as many situations as its weight, each fixing the step. The law whose moves are the situations is determined and takes exactly the steps of positive weight (`Situations.finerLaw_determined`, `Situations.finerLaw_step_iff`, [Becoming.Situations](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Situations.lean)): a law whose steps are those of positive weight is this determined law with the situation forgotten. For every process with listed moves and configurations and natural weights of steps, summing any reading of the courses over the finer courses is summing it over the courses, each times its weight (`Situations.sum_finer`), so the finer courses number the sum over histories (`Situations.finer_count`). On two values with p and q situations a step, there are (p + q)ⁿ finer courses, and the weight of a course is the number of situations that take it (`Situations.situations_count`, `Situations.situations_of_course`); those whose course deviates by at least d number, times d, at most n·p·q·(p + q)ⁿ (`Situations.situations_typical`). So the law of large numbers counts situations: of the situations of a kind, most give frequencies near the ratio of the weights; which situation happens is not a count, and nothing here fixes it. Weights that are not natural numbers are not numbers of situations: signed weights cancel, and numbers of situations do not (`Interference.cancels`, `counting_transfer_iff`); signed weights are pairs of numbers of situations, as the next paragraph states. Reading (the AI's reading of Ren's words, which Ren took on 16 October 2026): the core states what every situation obeys, and the situation makes the actual change true; its formal part, the determination of the law whose moves are situations, holds by construction, since the situation names the configuration it leads to, and the content is the count (`Situations.sum_finer`; D43); a chance is a number of situations of a kind, so stating it needs no premise that the course which happens is typical, while foretelling that course needs its situation. Record: [weights-count-situations](../results/records/weights-count-situations.md). Study: [section 144](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#144-权重计数情境p117).

**The open proofs of parting and counting.** Ren, 16 October 2026: “take 2, and do the open proofs” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-16-finite-name-and-open-proofs.md)). The proofs the two paragraphs above left open are given here, with no premise beyond what each law names. Where runs part only before step T and never after, the values through the map decided, no two runs part from where a run is at T (`Parting.unparted_at_of_late`); held by N configurations its course has at most T + N states, and the extrapolation by the least bound is right from 2(T + N) − 1 on (`Parting.late_statesBelow`, `Parting.late_right`). The converse: for a process with listed moves and configurations and decided steps and values through the map, the extrapolation built from the process reads off the prefix the configurations a run from s can be at, takes the first from which a path as long as the list of configurations is open (such a path goes on without end: where a list of N holds every configuration, a course of N steps from a configuration gives courses of every length, with no decidability and no selection, `Parting.unbounded_of_continues`, on `Lasting.Continues`; `Parting.liveRun`), and proposes that configuration's value through the map. It is right at a step on every run from s exactly where no two such runs part there (`Parting.world_right_iff`), so some extrapolation is right there exactly where none part (`Parting.some_right_iff`). With `Parting.not_right_on_both`, at each step a parting is exactly what neither the prefix nor the process settles. Concentration is exponential: for every u ≥ v, the courses with at least k ones weigh, times uᵏ·vⁿ, at most (p·u + q·v)ⁿ·vᵏ (`Typicality.concentrated`, the counting form of Chernoff's bound); with p = q = 1, of the 2ⁿ courses those with at least k ones number, times 3ᵏ, at most 4ⁿ (`Typicality.few_far_ahead`). For k ≥ 3n/4 this is a share below 0.88ⁿ, a corollary computed by hand from the theorem, not stated in Lean; the bound by the spread falls only as 1/n. Through an event, any number of values counts as two, the event's weight and the rest's in place of p and q (`Situations.sum_event`), so the law of large numbers and exponential concentration count situations for every event (`Situations.event_typical`, `Situations.event_concentrated`); each value is an event, so in most situations each value's frequency is near its share of the weight. For every process, the finer courses taking a course number its weight times how often it is listed, its weight where every move and configuration is listed once (`Situations.situations_take`, `Situations.situations_of_course_listed`). In any weights, steps weighing the sum over their situations are counted by them (`Situations.sum_finer_weights`); a signed step (P, N) is P situations that add and N that subtract, and the signed transfer is the pair of the numbers of finer courses composing to adding and to subtracting (`Situations.signed_counts`). Where signed weights cancel, the situations do not: the balanced (1, 1) of `Interference.cancels` is two situations (`Situations.cancels_situations`). Not added: real or complex amplitudes, and which situation happens. Records: [identification-where-runs-part](../results/records/identification-where-runs-part.md), [typicality-by-counting](../results/records/typicality-by-counting.md), [weights-count-situations](../results/records/weights-count-situations.md). Study: [section 150](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#150-分开与计数的开放证明p119).
**Knowledge.** [The chapter on knowledge](KNOWLEDGE.md) defends this candidate: propositional knowledge is a true judgment whose formation or maintenance is connected, through cognitive capacities, to the actual relations that make it correct, and whose correctness is not accidental. Knowing that, understanding and knowing how are distinct achievements. [The philosophical account](PHILOSOPHICAL-ACCOUNT.md) joins them with common inquiry and practical wisdom.

**Inquiry conditions checked.** Unless marked otherwise, these are periphery results with standard Lean axioms.

- **A necessary information condition for finite fallible inquiry.** Commitment coverage cannot exceed the two error weights plus evidential separation ([finite-fallible-inquiry-necessity](../results/records/finite-fallible-inquiry-necessity.md)). This is a necessary bound, not a sufficient one.
- **Branching tests.** Equivalence under branching tests is bisimulation ([branching-tests-bisimulation](../results/records/branching-tests-bisimulation.md)).
- **Finite plans.** Feasibility is equivalent to a finite plan branching on observations ([finite-continuation-plans](../results/records/finite-continuation-plans.md)).
- **Derivation.** Derivation is sound ([derivation-soundness](../results/records/derivation-soundness.md); an earlier module outside the spine, with no axioms). A substitution that keeps bindings supplies the original warrant at exactly every position ([binding-preserving-substitution](../results/records/binding-preserving-substitution.md)).
- **Questions across scales.** Whether questions can be translated and recovered ([finite-granularity-question-recovery](../results/records/finite-granularity-question-recovery.md)). Even all prefix questions can miss a loss in between ([prefix-observation-loss](../results/records/prefix-observation-loss.md)).
- **Learning.** Acquired bindings enter compositional use ([acquired-binding-compositional-use](../results/records/acquired-binding-compositional-use.md)); a learned procedure is reconstructed through actual execution ([learned-procedure-transmission](../results/records/learned-procedure-transmission.md)).
- **Evidence.** Executed instructions transport candidate evidence ([mediated-evidence-transport](../results/records/mediated-evidence-transport.md)); complete diagnosability under fallible witnesses ([fallible-witness-diagnosability](../results/records/fallible-witness-diagnosability.md)).

**Open.** That the complete core is indispensable to scientific inquiry has not been established ([scientific-inquiry-foundation](../results/records/scientific-inquiry-foundation.md)).

<a id="4-集体动力学--collective-dynamics"></a>
## 4. Collective dynamics

**Collective activity is explained from actual relations first.** It does not presuppose opposed interests, bargaining chips, who wins more, or a fixed common utility ([the reminder on common problems](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-08-collective-destructive-attractors.md#common-problem-solving-and-awareness)). Actual conflicts and differences remain.

**Markets.** Market selection stays within the generative regime only where successful activity renews the conditions on which further exchange and life depend, and those affected can still discover damage and change the arrangement ([markets and generative regimes](MARKETS-AND-GENERATIVE-REGIMES.md)). The bounds checked are these (all periphery; standard Lean axioms):

- continued operation under a protection certificate ([market-protected-continuation](../results/records/market-protected-continuation.md));
- the exact reach boundary of partial correction ([market-partial-correction-boundary](../results/records/market-partial-correction-boundary.md));
- a supported finite transition ([market-supported-finite-transition](../results/records/market-supported-finite-transition.md));
- an interval of positive seeds that finite support cannot rescue ([market-finite-support-obstruction](../results/records/market-finite-support-obstruction.md)).

**The meta-attractor of winning.** Where the influence the successful gain weakens the constraints on the strategies that produced the success, present advantage changes future selection ([meta-attractor](../../meta-attractor/README.md)). The spine gives these structural facts:

- **Joint consumption has no single consumer** (section 2).
- **Shared participation.** The consumer and the formation that loses support both take part in the drawn condition (`consumption_relates`).
- **Difference-making belongs to the joint activity.** Under overdetermination, withholding any single party's part, the loss happens all the same: no party alone makes the difference, while the relational reading still counts each drawer as consuming. In a formation's gradual depletion, the whole outcome is fixed by the tail: no finite set of events makes the whole difference, while the event of breaking makes the difference to that break ([joint-difference-making](../results/records/joint-difference-making.md)).
- **Substitution does not certify renewal.** A party winning by consumption can turn to other conditions and continue, and the one that lost does not recover with it. Under the stepwise reading, it is counted before the loser loses support and withheld from then until it switches (section 2).

So under relational neutrality, a party that wins by consumption cannot place the loser outside significance on the ground of unrelatedness (section 6.3). Under the relational reading, “my share makes no difference” does not remove a drawer from consumption either. None of this yields responsibility or duty.

<a id="5-评价的形成--how-evaluation-forms"></a>
## 5. How evaluation forms

**Positions, their regions and significance all form within the unfolding.** `own` is a region of formed relations, not an owner. `related` is a nonzero formed relation: a condition, a dependence, an inheritance or a cooperation.

**A standard's gaining influence, its recognizing its target, its being warranted and its being actually followed are four distinct questions.** A formed standard can be wrong. Learning a disposition is not learning a reason.

**Established routes of formation.**

- **Evaluative uptake.** Acquired understanding forms evaluative influence and enters warranted application ([evaluative uptake](../history/EVALUATIVE-UPTAKE.md), [evaluative grounding](../history/EVALUATIVE-GROUNDING.md)).
- **A warranted response under conditions.** An evaluative connection that was initially missing can become the mediator of a warranted response to another being. The same associative learning can afterwards suppress this response without overturning the reason. The warrant here rests on a defended normative premise, the repair principle of [evaluative grounding](../history/EVALUATIVE-GROUNDING.md): when an actor is answerable for an identifiable uncompensated deprivation of another being's independently warranted resources or capabilities, that deprivation supplies an actor-addressed reason for appropriate repair. The principle is stated at a bounded scope there; it is not derived from the dynamics.
- **The evaluator route.** Self-good, with unrestricted monotonicity and tolerance, gives a standpoint-indexed instance of living significance on a single history, in a narrowed form ([self-good-reachable-g0](../results/records/self-good-reachable-g0.md); periphery; standard Lean axioms), and it is unified in section 6.

**Readings of comparison.**

- **Relational information.** Comparing joint compositions needs relational information; single possibilities are not enough ([relational-generative-comparison](../results/records/relational-generative-comparison.md); periphery; standard Lean axioms).
- **The relational reading.** Under the relational reading, C ranks a consuming formation below the case in which it is absent; the wide reading by events does not decide ([relational-reading-comparison](../results/records/relational-reading-comparison.md); an earlier module outside the spine, with no axioms).
- **Priority of basic conditions.** It is independent of C and excludes every natural-number score ([protection-priority-excludes-uniform-aggregation](../results/records/protection-priority-excludes-uniform-aggregation.md); periphery; standard Lean axioms).
- **Comparison while the classification is undecided.** See [classification-robust-generative-comparison](../results/records/classification-robust-generative-comparison.md) (periphery; standard Lean axioms).

**Living significance: a condition, not an axiom.** Living significance says that realized living content is positively relevant (`G0`, [Becoming.Metaethics](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Metaethics.lean)). At the level of claims, every living claim has significance from every standpoint (`Valuation.Significant`). The audit of its uses shows that the continuation condition discharges every one of them (section 6.12). So living significance is not needed as an axiom: the theory states it as a condition and does not adopt it. The following use no axiom and are checked.

- **What it is.** On a connected whole with some standpoint and some living claim, living significance for every standpoint is exactly a seed (some living claim significant from some standpoint) with the two boundary neutralities, relational and standpoint neutrality (`Valuation.significant_iff`, section 6.1). Any position holding self-significance with its own living content supplies a seed; the seed is weaker than self-significance. Dropping the seed, either neutrality or connection each leaves a consistent countermodel (section 6.2). Where every living claim is held by some standpoint, living significance is exactly self-significance at every standpoint with standpoint neutrality, with neither connection nor relational neutrality (`Valuation.significant_iff_held`, section 6.7).
- **It and the continuation condition.** On a frame in which every claim is realized somewhere, the continuation condition is exactly living significance with every claim taken as living and positive relevance read as significance from every standpoint (`unrestricted_iff_g0`). On the level paths of every web this holds with no further hypothesis (`Web.unrestricted_iff_paths_g0`). The condition widens the scope of living significance to anything and weakens “positive” to “has significance”.
- **The dynamics do not entail it.** The represented dynamics contain no significance, so they do not entail living significance. On any web with an event that lives, with the web unchanged, taking everything as significant makes it hold and taking nothing as significant makes it fail (`Web.significance_undetermined`). This shows non-entailment by the represented dynamics; it does not by itself show that the condition is needed.

Living significance holds under the continuation condition of section 6.15, which the theory does not assert. With the condition as a hypothesis, living significance is a theorem (`Valuation.premises_of_unrestricted`, `g0_of_unrestricted`, section 6.8), and positive relevance read from significance is the least relevance satisfying it (`relevance_least`, section 6.12). The step is immediate: `g0_of_unrestricted` and `relevance_least` unpack the definitions of `Unrestricted` and `Frame.relevance`. Living significance is the condition restricted to living claims; the condition gives significance to every claim, not a positive valence, and “positive” and “further generation” are not represented. The theorems of sections 6.1–6.7 take living significance or its parts as hypotheses, or as one side of an equivalence, and use no axiom themselves.

Positive relevance is read from significance: it is the significance of realized living content (`Frame.relevance`), not a separate reading (Ren proposed this on 11 October 2026: “Positive relevance - yes, let's fold”, [words](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-11-part-ii-into-part-i.md)). So living significance says that every realized living claim's continuation has significance from every standpoint: the continuation condition on realized living content (section 6.12).

<a id="6-universal-metaethical-core--普遍元伦理核心"></a>
<a id="6-universal-metaethical-core"></a>

## 6. Significance, comparison and practical judgment

**This section uses no axiom.** The theory asserts no ethical axiom. The theorems of this section hold for any significance and any valuation. The continuation condition, “for anything, its continuation into the future has significance” (Ren's words), is formalized as `Unrestricted` and enters only as the condition of section 6.15. Below, “the condition” names that proposition, and “under the condition” marks a theorem that takes `Unrestricted` as a hypothesis; the theory does not assert it anywhere.

**Where the condition is used.** In the core, 26 theorems take `Unrestricted` as a hypothesis and 20 more state it in their conclusion: the equivalences that characterize it, the decomposition, the witnesses where it fails, and the rival `bounded_rival`. The bridge adds 7 that take it as a hypothesis. These are counts as of 17 October 2026, taken by searching the declarations of the Becoming package. Each theorem uses the condition only at named points, a formation's significance at a step from a standpoint, and each specializes there a law that holds for any significance. An earlier [inventory](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/PART-II-INVENTORY.md), of 11 October 2026, found that 22 of the 157 theorems the former conditional part cited took it, and 135 did not. For comparisons, the general local form is `Flow.Balances.comparison_within_reach`: significance need only cover what the part reaches.

**Import order is not dependence on the condition.** The modules `Becoming.Significance` and `Becoming.Metaethics` are imported, through `Becoming.Interdependence`, by 48 other core modules, among them `Becoming.Loops`, `Becoming.Observation` and `Becoming.Onward`. Those modules use no theorem that takes the condition. The package's layers follow import order, not the line between what holds for any significance and what holds under the condition.

**Readings.** A reading or name marked “[AI reading, Dn]” below is the AI's, stated with its argument and recorded as decision Dn in the [decision record](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/DELEGATED-CHOICES.md), with what would change it. The readings of Ren's words in sections 6.8–6.15 that carry no number are AI readings of the same kind.

<a id="61-g0-的内容--what-g0-says"></a>
<a id="61-what-g0-says"></a>

### 6.1 What living significance says

Ren's answer to the question of what makes ethical quality ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-shengsheng-ethical-direction.md)):

> 我的答案是生生不息 - 一种整体的，不断创造更多生的方向，而这个方向产生的可能性，多样性越高，复杂性/复杂结构的复杂度可以不断的上升，就是一个好的方向

(Translation: “My answer is 生生不息, generation of life without ceasing: a direction of the whole that keeps creating more life. The more possibilities and diversity this direction produces, and the more the complexity of complex structures can keep rising, the better a direction it is.”)

The object of living significance is 生生 (shengsheng, Ren's term: life in the whole and its continuing generation; the object of the ethical direction). Several scopes of “life” can be taken, and they may give different ranges. Living significance is a condition, not an axiom (section 5).

**Characterization.** On a connected whole with some living claim, living significance for every standpoint, at the level of claims, is equivalent to a seed with two boundary neutralities (`Valuation.significant_iff`, [Becoming.Metaethics](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Metaethics.lean)):
- the seed is that some living claim has significance from some standpoint (`Valuation.Seed`); any position that holds self-significance (`Valuation.SelfGood`) and has living content of its own supplies one;
- relational neutrality: significance passes along every formed relation to a living claim (`Valuation.transmits`);
- standpoint neutrality: what has significance from one standpoint has it from every standpoint (`Valuation.shares`).

Each neutrality is the rule holding at every instance (`Neutral`), so boundary neutrality is unrestricted universal quantification over the rule. Everything living significance adds beyond what an evaluator already holds lies in the two neutralities. The direction from living significance to these premises is nearly trivial, a projection. The content lies in the converse, which propagates significance along connection, and in minimality (section 6.2). This characterizes the content of living significance with no axiom. Under the condition of section 6.15, living significance is a theorem, and immediately so: it is the condition restricted to living claims, and the condition gives significance to every claim, not a positive valence (sections 6.8 and 6.15).

<a id="62-极小性与对手的形状--minimality-and-the-shape-of-rivals"></a>

### 6.2 Minimality and the shape of rivals

Dropping any one of the seed, relational neutrality, standpoint neutrality, connection or A leaves a consistent countermodel (`Model.seed_is_needed`, `Model.relational_neutrality_is_needed`, `Model.standpoint_neutrality_is_needed`, `Model.connection_is_needed`, `Model.articulation_neutrality_is_needed`). In these countermodels the rival holds the same rule, but only inside a boundary that has already formed:
- its own region: significance passes only within the evaluator's own region;
- its own standpoint: significance is shared only with the evaluator's own standpoint;
- those who endorse: the reason is given only to positions that endorse.

The present-aim evaluator, with no seed, values nothing. A living claim outside every formed relation is reached by no transmission.

So a position that rejects living significance need not deny the rule; it only draws a boundary. The disagreement then falls on whether that boundary is warranted. Saying that every rival is the same rule inside a boundary is a framing of these countermodels, not a theorem about all rivals. Sidgwick's dualism of practical reason is not dissolved by it; it is located at the premise of boundary neutrality.

<a id="63-从展开读出连通与消耗--connection-and-consumption-from-the-unfolding"></a>

### 6.3 Connection and consumption from the unfolding

The Becoming layer can supply two correspondences ([becoming-renewal-reading](../results/records/becoming-renewal-reading.md)):
- `living`, read relationally: a formation event that is active and does not consume within the run;
- `related`: taking part in a common condition.

Three things follow, with two qualifications.

- **Under a correspondence discipline, a common condition gives connection.** A common condition in which every living formation takes part connects the whole. There living significance is exactly a seed with both neutralities (`Conditions.significant_iff_of_common`). Where the conditions are disjoint the whole can be disconnected (`Separate.not_connected`).
  - This depends on what counts as a condition and as taking part. Adding a condition on which everyone depends at level 0 connects the whole for free. An over-coarse common condition that everyone draws on can empty `living` where the dependence on it fails.
  - One discipline ([condition-correspondence-discipline](../results/records/condition-correspondence-discipline.md)) relates two events through a condition only where one draws on it and the other depends on it at a positive level. Under it an idle condition connects nothing (`Web.inert_connects`). A condition on which every living event bears, with a living drawer and a living dependent, still connects the whole (`connected_of_renewable`). Connection through a condition is made by its living use (`Shared.connection_by_living_use`).
  - Interaction is a structural coincidence: one draws, the other depends at a positive level, at any steps. It is not yet ordered in time and not sensitive to effects. Co-dependence is a relation the discipline omits, not an absence of relation.
  - The discipline does not exclude a renewed condition on which everyone depends at a positive level and which a living event uses once (`one_use_connects`). Whether there is such a condition is a factual claim for application to check.
- **Significance passes along co-participation.** A consumer and what it consumes take part in the condition drawn on. Under relational neutrality, a standpoint that values a consumer's activity must value every living event of the formation it consumes (`Conditions.valuing_consumption_reaches`). The transmission does not use the consumption itself.
- **Direct relations (candidates).** Read with one kind of relatum, two direct readings of `related` stand beside interaction. Living on is drawing on a relatum at a step and depending on it at a positive need. Descent is taking part in the step at which a relatum begins to stand as a condition, and giving to it at that step.
  - Living on is no more free than interaction: it needs actual drawing and a dependence that can fail. Under `Dependent` the relatum lived on stands as a condition at that step, and where it is embodied it is active then; but it need be neither embodied nor active (`Drowsy`). Neither definition mentions embodiment: what enters the valuation is the identification of one kind of relatum.
  - They connect a bee and a flower that depend only on each other, and generations each given by the one before; there living significance is exactly a seed with both neutralities. Under relational neutrality, valuing the fox's eating reaches the hare's living, and valuing a formation that gives reaches the living events of what arises.
  - They are adopted as formed relations of continuation, not as direct valuations [AI reading, D9]. Neither `living` nor C changes ([Becoming.Descent](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Descent.lean), [living-on-and-descent](../results/records/living-on-and-descent.md)).
- **Readings are relative to a run.** Two runs of the same process from the same configuration can give different `living` (`Renewable.class_belongs_to_the_run`). A reading that does not depend on the run, and its connection to `universal_reason`, are open.
- **The correspondence obligations stand.** Whether conditions such as the biosphere or the climate are in fact shared by all living formations is an empirical correspondence. Whether the relational reading is 生生 is a philosophical argument.

**Stepwise and relation-level readings.** Read `living` as active and not undermining, and `related` as taking part in some condition at the respective steps. The first two points still hold. A common condition connects (`Web.significant_iff_of_common`). An event that draws at the step of a break and the formation broken take part in that condition, so under relational neutrality a standpoint that values the event must value the living events of what is broken (`Web.valuing_breaking_reaches`). Where dependence does not change, the whole-run and stepwise readings have the same `related` and differ only in `living`, in both directions (`Renewable.readings_differ`). The relation-level reading takes (formation, condition, step) as its claims. An event's stepwise reading is exactly the conjunction of the readings of the relations it takes part in, so one event can have a living relation and an undermining one (`Web.living_iff_relations`).

**Which reading.** The philosophical ground of the relational reading is not yet established ([assessment](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING-REVIEW.md)). [Study section 8.5](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md) compared the readings on five criteria and gave two scoped formal constraints:
1. In the renewable process, a reading decided by a finite prefix that includes every run of whole-run renewal includes some run of consumption (`Renewable.finite_reading_includes_consumption`). Whether this conflicts with the question Ren adopted depends on whether the verdict on that question covers the events before the threshold is crossed, and on whether the observable carries amounts (`Renewable.amount_reading`).
2. A whole-run but-for test of a single part cannot replace participation (`Overdetermined.no_difference`, `Renewable.no_finite_segment_makes_difference`).

The definitions of undermining and of but-for are the corrected ones, undermining made minimal; the claims about ranking, substitution, interaction, further generation and more are narrowed to what is proved (study sections 11, 12 and 15; the reviews behind them are listed in section 8.8).

Every current reading of `living` reads “not damaging”, and none includes further generation. Further generation is stated of the whole's continuation (section 2, “further generation”) and has entered no reading of `living`. Which reading, or combination, is the reading of 生生 is a normative and interpretive choice within the direction of 生生. A reading [AI reading, D1, D10]: 生生 reads as generation generating generation, its direction compared by widening the generative space (section 6.13), and comparison uses no reading of `living`; the study's comparison supplies the argument.

<a id="64-向每个立场的理由--reasons-addressed-to-every-position"></a>

### 6.4 Reasons addressed to every position

**D follows from A.** The positioned articulation D, which is not conditioned on endorsement, follows from A and from the significance of living significance, which carries no standpoint index (`positioned_articulation_of_articulates`).

**A universal reason.** A sound inquiry aimed at new living content gives every position with access to it a contributory reason (`universal_reason`). Its normative premises are the seed, relational neutrality and standpoint neutrality (together `Valuation.Grounds`) and A. Connection (under the discipline of section 6.3, relative to a run) and soundness are facts about the unfolding and the inquiry ([metaethical-core-characterization](../results/records/metaethical-core-characterization.md), reusing [positioned-positive-reason](../results/records/positioned-positive-reason.md) and [positive-ground-open-futures](../results/records/positive-ground-open-futures.md)). The theorem passes the normative content of A and of the grounds through; it does not supply it.

**A reason is not more than a reason.** A contributory reason is not a duty and not a ranking, and it predicts no recognition or compliance.

<a id="65-比较提升与实践判断--comparison-lifting-and-practical-judgment"></a>

### 6.5 Comparison, lifting and practical judgment

**C and the practical commitments.** C is stated on the configurations of a frame. The first two practical commitments (J1/J2), “basic conditions first, then living gain”, are stated by the bridge's `BetterAt` ([practical-judgment-g0-j](../results/records/practical-judgment-g0-j.md)).

**L is a hidden premise that integration exposed.** A situation gives each response one outcome, but a process can have several. As soon as responses are compared, a lifting rule is needed. Under the conservative L:
- the comparison, the reason, the survival of coordinated choice, and continual improvement having no survivor hold on every frame;
- the functional situation is exactly the deterministic case (`articulation_iff_lifted`, [becoming-spine-instances](../results/records/becoming-spine-instances.md)).

L is a substantive commitment:
- a response with one good and one bad outcome is incomparable in both directions;
- transitivity needs a feasible intermediate response.

**What C ranks depends on the reading it compares.** Take two continuations as configurations, and the events a reading includes as the realized claims. Then C is the comparison on this frame ([Becoming.Ranking](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Ranking.lean), [ranking-under-readings](../results/records/ranking-under-readings.md)):
- a continuation without some formation extends the continuation with it only if the reading withholds every event of that formation;
- under the whole-run reading, C ranks the continuation without an overdrawing formation higher, and ranks sharing above winning by consumption;
- under the stepwise reading, where the overdrawing formation has an event at whose step every need is still met, that event is included, and C no longer decides between the formation's presence and its absence (malignant growth and winning by consumption are instances); it still ranks sharing above winning by consumption. Without such an event, for example where support is overstepped at the first step, the stepwise reading too ranks the absence higher (`Immediate`);
- in the two-part family, under both readings C ranks a continuation that stops before the threshold above overdraw (`Ranking.restraint_ranks`, `Ranking.malignant_restraint`). There the stepwise reading loses the comparison with absence but not the comparison with restraint. With a third formation sharing the condition, the comparison with restraint can be lost too (`ThirdParty`).

So to get “better without malignant growth” from C, the reading C compares must withhold the events before the loss, or a stronger comparison must be added to C. Read on possibilities, C ranks the continuation without it higher and withholds no event (section 6.12); under the condition of section 6.15 that is the condition's own comparison. Whether living significance and C share one reading is part of the choice of a reading of 生生 (section 6.3). Courses are compared on possibilities, using no reading of `living`, for the reasons of section 6.12.

<a id="66-不做什么--what-it-does-not-do"></a>

### 6.6 What it does not do

It proves none of self-significance, relational neutrality, standpoint neutrality, A, C, L or the practical commitments. It binds no position that rejects them, and it does not treat the value of bringing new lives into existence. Normative warrant, recognition, motivation, compliance and actual preservation are different claims. So are direction, continuing capacity, monotonic growth at every step and scalar maximization.

<a id="67-从想要延续者重述ren-的提议待定--restating-from-what-wants-its-continuation-rens-proposal-pending"></a>
<a id="67-从想要延续者重述ren-的提议-restating-from-what-wants-its-continuation-rens-proposal"></a>
<a id="67-restating-from-what-wants-its-continuation-rens-proposal"></a>

### 6.7 Restating from what wants its continuation

Ren's proposal (10 October 2026, [words](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-wanting-continuation-one-axiom.md); the first line repeated a sentence of the assistant's previous reply and is omitted, and the rest is Ren's text in full):

> I think this can be much stronger, it can be anything that want's its continuation to have significance, this is want needs to be followed
>
> and for the whole - this is actually perspective dependent, the whole can be the self, the collective. or put it another way, how much does one consider something to be a part of its system dynamics. and where is this system centered. And we can also prove the pure "self" is an illusion, where only when outside exists, there is the system, if the system depends on external resources. But on the other side, we have meta attractor, where the "self" gets constructed to become something that is totalizing on its future. and it is also a case where "self" becomes ridged, unable to see longer horizon, where it has to count the dynamics that happens beyond what the "self" sees
>
> all above does not require any axiom
>
> if we want to add one axiom, that is for anything, its continuation into the future has significance(doesn't have to be positive, we could argue as long as not harm), or continuation into the future needs to create more significance(being generative)
>
> and this closes the whole.

The AI's analysis, which Ren answered “exactly”, split the proposal in two halves. The dynamics fix the extent of what a wanting formation must count; they do not fix its center, and in that analysis the condition's one job was to make every point a center. Formally, a center at every wanting point is self-significance, the seed at each point [AI reading, D11]. The analysis then read the condition as standpoint neutrality. Ren's later answer, that centers and their boundaries lie within the dynamics, replaced that reading: section 6.8 reads the condition as Ren's words themselves. That earlier reading, the draft restatement built on it and the narration of decisions D11–D15 are kept in a labelled history excerpt of the chapter as of 17 October 2026. The checked content follows ([plan](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/CONTINUATION-GROUNDING-PLAN.md), [study section 47](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md), [independent review](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/CONTINUATION-GROUNDING-REVIEW.md)).

**Extent: what the dynamics derive, with no ethical premise** ([Becoming.Continuation](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Continuation.lean)).
- **Beyond the boundary.** Where a formation taking part takes from one condition and gives to another, something is conveyed from the first to the second (`Flow.Conveys`). Where something is conveyed into a member from outside a family, the family is fed from beyond (`Flow.FedFromBeyond`); with equality decided, that is exactly a step at which the family is not inward (`Flow.inward_iff_not_fed`). So under balance and dissipation, a finite family listed without repetition, from which taking recurs and which is enclosed around from some step on, cannot from any step on be fed from nothing beyond it (`Flow.fed_from_beyond`, with forms for rising and for embodied arising). What is needed is being fed, not the recurrence of any condition it is fed from: a pool is fed at every step while each unit of fuel is conveyed out only at its own step (`Lone.fed_not_recurring`), and one source does all the conveying.
- **Beyond one's own draw.** Draws each within what a step gives can together exceed it (`Pair.own_not_joint`; in a balanced web, `Exceeding.own_not_joint`). Each step of joint excess lowers the level by at least one, so joint excess cannot outlast what was held (`Flow.excess_bounded`). Where renewal is given only by formations that depend on what they renew, a joint excess that brings the level below their need ends the renewers and every formation that depends on the condition at such a need, a taker among them where it so depends (`Flow.joint_excess_ends`, `Exceeding.all_end`; with a single taker all continue, `Alone.all_continue`). The joint count binds a want that outlasts what the condition holds: `n` steps of joint excess need only a level of `n`, and `Mortal` exceeds jointly at both its living steps while every want of it is met.
- **What the derived extent does not reach.** It reaches one way, to what one's own continuation depends on: where one stops giving, a formation living on that giving ends while one continues as before (`Downstream.one_way`). It ends with the want: a formation active for only two steps, with every dependence met while it lives, ends the renewal of a shared condition, and another dependent ends only after it has ended (`Mortal.after_its_end`). A clearer that meets every requirement derived here, active at every step, with every condition it depends on renewed, fed from beyond and drawn on jointly within what is given, still exhausts the dweller's habitat, and the dweller ends (`Clearing.undermines_within_renewal`). In these witnesses the endings are stipulated in their activity, and the witnesses show them consistent with balance, support and dependence; the general theorems derive endings.
- **What taking all that is given excludes** ([Becoming.Exclusion](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Exclusion.lean), [what-taking-all-excludes](../results/records/what-taking-all-excludes.md)). A formation takes all that is given to a condition across a step where it takes part and takes from the condition at least all that the step gives to it (`Flow.TakesAll`); alongside it, any other taker makes the joint taking exceed what is given (`Flow.excess_of_takesAll`). So where renewal is given only by formations depending on what they renew, full taking alongside another taker at each of `n` steps, from a level below `n + m`, ends every formation depending on the condition at a need of at least `m`, the full taker included (`Flow.full_taking_ends`). A full taker that continues therefore leaves no other formation taking from the condition at each of the `W.level T c + 1` steps from any `T` (`Flow.continuing_excludes`). A formation active only while it takes from the condition is not active at each of them, and where its activity does not resume once it stops, it ends (`Flow.continuing_excludes_active`, `Flow.continuing_ends_taker`): here the ending is derived, not stipulated. Taking jointly within what is given keeps the level, and keeps every formation supported where the formations depend on that condition alone at needs it met at the start (`Flow.level_kept`, `Flow.supported_of_within`). Three courses of `Exceeding`'s members show what ending another serves (`Excluding.three_courses`). Where both takers take two, all end. Where the first takes all that is given and the second ends after the first step, the renewer and the first are active at every step, and the ending also follows from the law (`Excluding.ends_by_law`). Where each takes one, all three are supported and active at every step, and neither takes all that is given. So the first's continuation does not require the second's ending; taking all that is given does. The levels of the last two courses admit every member at every step, and each level path includes the other (`Excluding.admission_alike`). The exclusion acts through the taking, not through the levels the second needs, so a comparison of courses by admission does not register it, and the loss is the member's continuation [AI reading, D44].

**Center: what the dynamics do not fix** ([Becoming.Standpoints](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Standpoints.lean)).
- **Held lives.** A living claim within some standpoint's own region is held (`Valuation.Held`). Where every living claim is held, living significance is exactly self-significance at every standpoint with standpoint neutrality; neither connection nor relational neutrality is needed (`Valuation.significant_iff_held`). Where there are lives no standpoint holds, with holding decided and every living claim reached from a held one through formed relations, relational neutrality is added for the unheld claims only (`Valuation.significant_iff_reached`). Each premise is needed: lives each held and valued only from their own standpoint, where neither neutrality holds (`Apart.not_enough`); an unheld life (`Unclaimed`); a reached but unheld life (`Relayed`). Self-significance by itself already carries each standpoint's significance along any relation into its own region (`Valuation.own_transmission`).
- **Two completions.** Standpoint neutrality completes self-significance with no connection. On a connected whole where every standpoint holds some living claim, self-significance everywhere with relational neutrality gives living significance without standpoint neutrality (`Valuation.significant_of_transmits`). With relations read as dependence, relational neutrality is the valuation form of counting what one's continuation depends on; in a whole where dependence links every two lives, the derived extent with self-significance reaches every life (a reading of the general theorem). Standpoint neutrality is the completion that needs no connection; it is not the only one.
- **Each formation its own center.** Read stepwise, with each formation's own events its own region, every living event is held, and living significance is exactly self-significance at every formation with standpoint neutrality (`Web.significant_iff_self`). Self-significance ranges over living claims only: read stepwise, a formation's own undermining events are not living, and self-significance says nothing of them (`Clearing.clearer_not_living`). So read, the seed already carries the filter of the weak form, not harm; that is a normative reading.
- **Along dependence, the center is not derived.** One event's formation is fed by another's where, at the same step, the second's formation takes part and gives to a condition the first requires (`Flow.FedBy`). In the web of `Clearing`, each formation holds and values its own events, and the clearer also values the source's events (the source gives to what the clearer requires). Every life is held, self-significance holds everywhere, each standpoint's significance is closed under feeding, and yet the dweller's living event has no significance from the clearer's standpoint: standpoint neutrality and living significance fail (`Clearing.center_not_derived`). With standpoint neutrality added, living significance holds by `Web.significant_iff_self`. This line holds for the extent derived along dependence. In the same valuation, the formed relations of the stepwise reading relate the clearer's event to the dweller's through the habitat in which both take part, while transmission out of the clearer's own region along these relations fails: the extent derived along every formed relation would reach the dweller. That the source requires nothing is stipulated. Significance at the level of claims does not separate counting another as a condition from counting it as a being that wants.
- **Conveyance as a candidate relation.** Read with one kind of relatum, conveyance relates two events at the same step (`Flow.conveyance`). A relatum that takes nothing and gives nothing is related to no event (`Flow.conveyance_idle`). The boundary of a continuing family is crossed from every step on, so its region is no cut of the relation (`Flow.region_not_a_cut`). Under relational neutrality, valuing a relatum's event reaches the living events of the relatum that conveys into it (`Flow.valuing_reaches_source`). Under living on, the region of what a formation lives on is no cut either (`Clearing.resource_not_cut_living_on`); what conveyance adds is a relation between two conditions, in the direction of inflow (`Clearing.conveyance_only`). Conveyance is adopted as a formed relation, as living on and descent are [AI reading, D12].

**Relation to section 6.1.** The route of section 6.1 starts from one seed and needs connection and relational neutrality to carry significance everywhere. This route has a seed at every wanting point, and where every life is held it needs only standpoint neutrality to make them common. On a connected whole with a living claim and every life held, both are equivalent to living significance; on a connected whole where every standpoint holds a living claim, self-significance everywhere with relational neutrality also gives living significance. In general neither implies the other, and the earlier characterization keeps its scope. The shape of rivals in section 6.2 persists: the principal rival now accepts self-significance everywhere and keeps significance within each standpoint, which is the boundary that standpoint neutrality denies.

**Weak and strong forms.** The weak form, for each formation: its undamaging continuation has significance; every current reading of `living` reads “not damaging”. Ren's “as long as not harm” also admits a deontic reading, in which each continuation's significance is a claim on others not to harm it; that reading is not formalized. The AI took neither: not harm reads as retaining, a relation between the levels with a part and without it (section 6.8) [AI reading, D13]. The strong form, for the whole: continuation is to create more significance, the further generation of living significance read as a direction (`Flow.More`, `World.ArisingRecurs`), with “more” read as widening what can form [AI reading, D15]. It can be met only where the world stays open (section 1, “The whole and its parts”; [continued-change-needs-openness](../results/records/continued-change-needs-openness.md)).

**What remains.** The restatement does not need to replace living significance, which is a condition and not an axiom (sections 5 and 6.8). The philosophical ground of standpoint neutrality is open, as that of living significance is (section 8). Non-ethical premises remain: balance, dissipation, embodiment and enclosure; which relations count as formed and which formations want; the step from wanting an end to having a reason for what it requires; and the representation itself (discrete steps, natural-number amounts, decided equality and holding). Practical judgment between conflicting continuations still needs C, L and the practical commitments.

<a id="68-一条公理推出什么p39-what-the-one-axiom-yields-p39"></a>
<a id="68-一条公理推出什么--what-the-one-axiom-yields"></a>
<a id="68-what-the-one-axiom-yields-as-a-condition"></a>

### 6.8 What the continuation condition yields

After proposing the condition, Ren answered that a center and its boundary exist only within cognitive structure and dynamics, formed within the same system. So there is no question of whose continuation matters that would need an axiom to settle it, and a center can be modelled as a thing like everything else. Ren also answered that “not harm” is a phase transition with a clear boundary ([words](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-centers-within-the-dynamics.md)). Following that answer, the condition is not read as putting a center at every wanting point (section 6.7). It is read as Ren's words themselves: for anything, its continuation into the future has significance. The checked content follows ([Becoming.Significance](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Significance.lean), [study section 53](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md)). Each theorem below that is said to hold “under the condition” takes `Unrestricted` as a hypothesis; the others hold for any significance.

- **The condition as a proposition.** A significance says, for a standpoint, a step and a formation, whether from that standpoint the formation's continuation at that step has significance. The condition, `Unrestricted`, says that nothing restricts it: from every standpoint the continuation of anything has significance at every step. It is boundary neutrality (`Neutral`, section 6.2) applied once, to the significance of continuation; as a predicate it is the one that holds everywhere. A rival keeps the same rule inside each standpoint's region (`bounded_rival`).
- **Under the condition, living significance follows immediately** ([one-axiom-yields-g0](../results/records/one-axiom-yields-g0.md)). Take any reading in which a claim's significance from a standpoint is the significance of some formation's continuation at some step. Under the condition, living significance holds at the level of claims, and so do self-significance at every standpoint, relational neutrality, standpoint neutrality and, wherever something lives, a seed, with no connection used (`Valuation.premises_of_unrestricted`). The stepwise, relation-level and whole-run readings are such readings (`Web.significant_of_unrestricted`, `Conditions.significant_of_unrestricted`). The step is an instantiation: living significance is the condition restricted to living claims. What the condition gives is significance for every claim, living or not, and for formations not yet formed; it gives no positive valence, and `living` includes no further generation. On the level paths of a web (`Web.paths`), under the condition, living significance holds with positive significance read as significance from every standpoint, and so does C with “better” read as retaining and adding what has significance (`Web.paths_g0_comparison`). So under the condition the premise card's seed, self-significance, relational neutrality, standpoint neutrality and, on level paths, C hold. A, L and the practical commitments remain premises.
- **What has significance is what can form** ([significance-retained-within-margin](../results/records/significance-retained-within-margin.md)). From a standpoint, one level path *retains* what has significance along another when it admits every continuation with significance that the other admits (`Web.Retains`). It *adds* when it admits one that the other does not (`Web.Adds`). Under the condition, retaining is inclusion and adding is exceeding (`Web.retains_iff_includes`, `Web.adds_iff_exceeds`), alike from every standpoint (`Web.retains_agree`). So, under the condition, the four cases of a part (section 2) are the four cases of significance, and Ren's strong form, to “create more significance”, is the generative case of the weak form, with no further condition.
- **Not harm is an exact boundary, for any significance.** In a balanced flow, compared at the levels with the part withheld, the levels with a part retain what has significance without it exactly when, for every continuation with significance that the levels without it admit, the part's net draw stays within the margin (`Flow.Balances.retains_iff_within_margin`). The part loses a continuation with significance exactly where its net draw exceeds the margin (`Flow.Balances.loses_iff_beyond_margin`). It adds one exactly where its net exchange brings within the margin a formation that the levels without it do not admit (`Flow.Balances.adds_iff_within_margin`). These laws take no condition: the condition only fixes which continuations count. Reading them as Ren's “a phase transition with a clear boundary” is the AI's: the boundary is fixed by the part's own net exchange, with no will or belief from outside the dynamics.
- **What has not yet formed counts.** In `Preemption` a drawer takes a condition's unit before any dependence on it forms. Its event is active and not undermining, so it is living on the stepwise reading. Yet its part narrows what can form: a formation requiring the condition from the next step on is admitted at every step without the part, and at no later step with it. Under the condition, the part loses a continuation with significance (`Preemption.not_undermining_yet_loses`). The stepwise reading counts only dependences that have been met; retaining also counts what can still form. The two readings of not harm differ, and not harm reads as retaining [AI reading, D13].
- **No ending is licensed.** A level path that retains what has significance along another ends none of it (`Web.Retains.not_added`). Under the condition, the conveyor's part is unordered, each path adding what the other lacks (`Transfer.unordered`), so ordering it needs a measure, which is a further premise. Under the condition, the giver's part retains and adds (`Restoring.Inflow.retains_adds`).
- **The whole first** ([whole-first-and-bounded-centers](../results/records/whole-first-and-bounded-centers.md)). The fewer continuations have significance, the more is retained (`Web.Retains.of_fewer`); significance bounded by a region retains what is admitted within the region (`Web.retains_bounded_iff`). Under the condition, what retains in the whole retains within every region, a system's or a self's (`Web.Retains.within`). What holds within a system holds within every region inside it (`Web.AdmitsWithin.narrow`). The converse fails. In `Clearing`, let each formation's significance stop at what its continuation depends on. From the clearer's standpoint its part retains what has significance at every step; from the dweller's standpoint it loses the dweller (`Clearing.centers_disagree`). Under the condition the part loses the dweller from every standpoint, while the levels without it admit every formation at every step and the clearer is supported throughout, so withholding the part ends nothing (`Clearing.free_rider_loses`). This gives Ren's order of levels, the whole, then the system one is in, then the self, as an order of how complete a judgment is, not as a description of what life does [AI reading, D43].
- **The represented dynamics do not entail significance.** Where nothing has significance, every level path retains every other, none adds, and living significance fails at any event that lives; where everything has significance, it holds (`Web.significance_undetermined`). The theorem compares the significance that holds everywhere with the one that holds nowhere, a free parameter not built from `World` data. It shows that the represented dynamics, which contain no significance, do not entail it; it is not evidence that becoming leaves significance undetermined. A significance bounded by a region is supplied as the condition is. Ren wrote that the self “can not justify itself without some sort of "belief"”; here that reads as: a significance bounded by the self is a premise too.

**Reading Ren's words.** `Unrestricted` reads Ren's proposed condition: the formation index reads “anything”, a formation's continuation at a step reads “its continuation into the future”, and the standpoint index reads “has significance” without qualification, no center being privileged. Significance is not graded and, as Ren wrote, need not be positive. Retaining reads “as long as not harm”: a part does no harm where the levels with it retain what has significance without it. Adding reads “create more significance” and “more possibilities of becoming”: a part that also adds is generative. Under the condition, the reading of section 6.7, self-significance as wanting and standpoint neutrality for the center, is a consequence: the condition gives self-significance at every standpoint and standpoint neutrality.

**What this does not derive.** The step from significance to a reason (A), the lifting (L) and practical judgment (the practical commitments); a measure that orders mixed parts; the level at which formations are individuated (members or lineages), which fixes what counts as a loss; which reading the `living` of living significance names (`Preemption` shows that two readings differ); and the normative warrant of the condition itself. Recognition and motivation belong to the route without axiom (section 6.9). In `Clearing.centers_disagree`, a center whose represented boundary is exactly its actual dependence still licenses the free rider; section 6.9 shows that without the condition the loss shows only where the dependence reaches what is closed. With the condition as a hypothesis, living significance is a theorem, immediate at the level of claims, and through the laws without axiom not harm and generativity have exact forms. Living significance itself is not an axiom (sections 5 and 6.12).

<a id="69-不用公理能走多远p40-how-far-the-route-without-axiom-reaches-p40"></a>
<a id="69-不用公理能走多远--how-far-the-route-without-axiom-reaches"></a>

### 6.9 How far the route without axiom reaches

Route (b) is the AI's wording, which Ren quoted and chose to try after route (a) ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-axiom-route-levels-and-possibilities.md)):

> No axiom. The theory only describes, and “should” is what a center does once its represented boundary matches what it actually depends on. The theory then explains recognition and motivation but claims no warrant beyond them.

The AI also named two mechanisms that need no premise: “selection between wholes: wholes whose parts sustain them outlast others” and “constraint: a part that degrades its whole removes its own supply”. Ren answered that a whole realizing, understanding and updating itself is “also under the selection rules and constraints in theory, but in practice, how this happens, needs more thoughts”. Of the levels Ren wrote: “i think it should be the whole, and a weaker version is within the system that one is in, then the self”. This section adds no axiom and asks which level the route reaches ([Becoming.Interdependence](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Interdependence.lean), [study section 56](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md)). The condition enters only as a hypothesis of the comparisons with the condition's verdict.

- **What a continuation depends on is its feeding closure** (section 2, [feeding-closure](../results/records/feeding-closure.md)). Among formations, “what it actually depends on” reads as the feeding closure: the least region holding the formation that is closed under feeding, reached with no premise. Nothing beyond the closure gives to what the closure requires (`Flow.beyond_gives_nothing`). Section 6.8 lets each formation's significance in `Clearing` stop at what its dependence reaches; that region is exactly the derived feeding closure (`Clearing.dependenceRegion_iff`), so the disagreement of centers in section 6.8 does not rest on a stipulation.
- **The constraint runs along feeding** ([consumption-ends-with-feeders](../results/records/consumption-ends-with-feeders.md)). Where every feeder is unsupported, consumption ends (`Flow.Balances.consumption_ends_with_feeders`). In `Overshoot`, a formation that keeps its support over the condition it requires ends through its feeder (`Overshoot.ends_through_feeder`). In `Clearing`, the dweller ends while the clearer's closure continues (`Clearing.closure_supported`).
- **Where the route meets the condition** ([closure-verdict-meets-axiom](../results/records/closure-verdict-meets-axiom.md)). Significance bounded by the whole is the condition (`bounded_whole`). Wherever every formation the other path admits lies in the region or is admitted by the first, significance bounded by a region retains as inclusion does (`Web.retains_bounded_iff_includes`); under the condition the two verdicts then agree (`Web.retains_bounded_iff_retains`). Under the condition, where a formation's feeding closure is the whole, the verdict within its closure is the condition's verdict on every comparison (`Flow.within_closure_iff_retains`), as in `Overshoot` (`Overshoot.within_closure_iff_retains`). Under the condition, its verdict decides the verdict within every region (`Web.Retains.within`). In `Clearing` the two part. From the clearer's standpoint its part retains what has significance within its closure; under the condition it loses the dweller, and from the dweller's standpoint, within the dweller's own closure, it loses the dweller too (`Clearing.within_closure_yet_loses`).

**Reading.** The feeding closure reads “what it actually depends on”. It is reached without axiom; how a center comes to represent it is not modelled. A center that wants its continuation and represents its closure has a reason of its own to retain what lies within the closure, since a loss there can return to it, as in `Overshoot`. This explains motivation; it is not a warrant. The constraint runs along feeding: a loss returns to a center through its feeders, and what lies beyond the closure supplies nothing within it. Selection between wholes keeps the wholes that continue. It removes the whole of `Overshoot`, which ends, and keeps that of `Clearing`, where the clearer continues while the dweller ends. Neither mechanism reaches a loss that returns to no one.

The levels are separated by the results on feedback and reach (sections 2 and 6.10). The feeding closure lies within the influence closure, and the influence and reach closures lie within the system, each inclusion possibly strict (`Clearing.levels`, `Clearing.reach_middle`, `Trophic.mediates`). The system does not read Ren's intermediate level, “the system that one is in”: wherever one condition is taken part in by everything, the system is the whole, and systems partition the formations, with no nesting and no center. For that reading the reach closure is offered (section 6.10) [AI reading]. The module header of `Becoming.Interdependence` says the same: the feeding closure is not read as that system, and `Becoming.Feedback` offers the reach closure.

So route (b) reaches the self and what it depends on. It reaches the whole only where the whole is one feeding closure, and there its verdict is the condition's verdict. What the condition adds is significance for continuations whose loss returns to no one, such as the dweller's. Ren wrote that the whole can realize itself, “like us, we recognize that "we" are the universe/ecosystem that realizes its own existence”. A center whose represented boundary is the whole holds the condition's significance (`bounded_whole`). Route (b) asks for a represented boundary that matches actual dependence, so it gives that significance only where the dependence reaches the whole.

**What this does not derive.** That a center wants its continuation, or represents its feeding closure; that a loss within the closure does return (feeders may be redundant, and stock and timing decide when an ending arrives); any warrant, which this route does not claim. Living significance is not an axiom (section 5); with the condition of section 6.15 as a hypothesis, it is a theorem (section 6.8).

<a id="610-反馈所及与比较p42-feedback-reach-and-the-comparison-p42"></a>
<a id="610-反馈所及与比较--feedback-reach-and-the-comparison"></a>

### 6.10 Feedback, reach and the comparison

On 10 October 2026 Ren stated the long-term objectives ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-strongest-claims-direction.md)):

> you know the long term objectives don't you? I want to push the frontier as strong as possible, in terms of human knowledge, or have the full truth set(still need situational awareness), the universal ethics
>
> do you see answers for these questions?

After the AI answered the questions left open, Ren wrote: “continue, let's pursue the strongest claims and complete proof, i think you see the right direction”. The AI's answers, in the same source and in the AI's words, held that the feeding closure is exact for supply but misses protection; that the general object is the closure under every influence; and that the system one is in is larger again, so that there are four levels: self, dependence, system and whole. Of the levels Ren had written: “i think it should be the whole, and a weaker version is within the system that one is in, then the self” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-axiom-route-levels-and-possibilities.md)). This section adds no axiom. It states how far a formation's own feedback reaches, how far a part's exchange reaches, and where the comparison with and without a part is decided (section 2, [Becoming.Feedback](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Feedback.lean), [study section 61](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#61-反馈与所及p42)). The result has five closures, not four levels: the reach closure is added. The condition enters only as a hypothesis of three witnesses.

- **Five levels** (section 2, [levels-of-closure](../results/records/levels-of-closure.md)). The feeding closure lies within the influence closure.
