The influence closure, what can affect a formation, and the reach closure, what it can affect, lie within the system. Each inclusion can be strict, and the influence and reach closures point in opposite directions (`Clearing.levels`, `Clearing.reach_middle`, `Trophic.mediates`). One common condition makes the system the whole (`Flow.system_whole_of_common`): `Clearing` is one system, while the reach closure separates the clearer from the whole (`Clearing.system_whole`, `Clearing.reach_middle`).
- **Feedback carries at most the influence closure** ([feedback-and-reach](../results/records/feedback-and-reach.md)). Nothing beyond the influence closure shows within it (`Flow.Balances.uncoupled_beyond_influence`). In `Clearing`, the clearer's support does not show whether it ends the dweller (`Clearing.uncoupled`), while its part reaches the dweller (`Clearing.reaches_dweller`).
- **The comparison is decided within the reach** ([comparison-within-reach](../results/records/comparison-within-reach.md)). In a balanced flow, at the withheld levels, any significance covering what a part has reached before each step compares that part as the condition does: retaining is inclusion, and not adding is not exceeding (`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_reach`; for the comparisons under the condition, `Web.retains_iff_includes` and `Web.adds_iff_exceeds`). This advances `Web.retains_bounded_iff_includes` of section 6.9, which asks every formation the other path admits to lie in the region or be admitted by the first; here significance need only cover what the part reaches ([closure-verdict-meets-axiom](../results/records/closure-verdict-meets-axiom.md)). Significance bounded by a region closed under affecting compares every part within the region as the condition does (`Flow.Balances.closed_comparison`). Where each center's system is the whole, significance bounded by the system is the condition (`Flow.bounded_system_whole`). In `Clearing`, bounded by each influence closure, the clearer's part retains; bounded by each reach closure, it loses the dweller, as under the condition, although that significance is not the condition (`Clearing.system_counts_dweller`, `Clearing.reach_counts_dweller`). The comparison of any two flows that start alike is decided within what their difference reaches (`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_difference`).
- **Two counterfactuals.** The comparison by withheld levels keeps the rest of the exchange fixed, so it shows only a part's direct exchanges. Mediated effects, such as the wolves' effect on the forest through the deer, appear only in a comparison in which others respond. With the rest fixed, the wolves' part narrows and does not reach the forest; against the flow in which the deer respond, it is mixed (`Trophic.mediated_uncoupled`, `Trophic.responsive_mixed`, `Trophic.difference_reaches_forest`). Where a part gives what others take, the levels without it may not exist, and then there is no such comparison (`Clearing.no_levels_without_source`).

**Reading.**
- The influence closure reads what a formation's own feedback can carry once effects pass through the support and activity of others. With the rest of the exchange fixed, only the parts that reach it show.
- Feedback and a part's exchange point in opposite directions. A formation's feedback carries at most its influence closure, what can affect it, while its own part reaches into its reach closure, what it can affect. A center that values only what its feedback carries compares its own part as the condition does wherever the part reaches only within that closure (`Flow.Balances.comparison_local`). Where the part reaches beyond it, the center can misjudge it, as the clearer does; reaching beyond does not by itself make it misjudge (`Margin.reach_without_misjudgment`). This reads Ren's “still need situational awareness”, which Ren attached to “the full truth set”: to compare its own part, a center must know what the part reaches beyond its feedback.
- The system bounds every effect, but it does not read Ren's intermediate “system that one is in”: wherever one condition is taken part in by everything the system is the whole, and systems partition the formations, with no nesting and no center. For that reading the reach closure is offered: what a center's exchange affects, link by link. It is centred and nests, lies within the system, and in `Clearing` lies strictly between the clearer and the whole. It points forward and need not hold what the center depends on. It is not the feeding closure, which the route without axiom reaches (section 6.9).
- Ren's weaker version then reads: significance that covers what a center reaches gives the condition's comparison on every part within that reach whose levels without it exist, with the rest of the exchange fixed. Ren also wrote that “at this moment in time, the whole earth has become a highly entangled system” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-truth-exists-opening.md)). Where a center's reach is the whole, as where every formation depends on one condition and exchanges with it (`Flow.reach_whole_of_common`), the weaker version coincides with the condition's comparison. That condition is one common condition; it does not measure coupling. The coupling strengths measure it (`Becoming.Propagation`). Ren's sentence goes on: “everyone country, every person, is heavily coupled into it”. Its two clauses are read by two structures: “heavily coupled into it” by the coupling strengths, and “a highly entangled system” by couplings that lie on loops through the whole, so that what an act's difference does comes back around and no finite order of responses fixes it (`carriedWithin_exact_of_no_loop`, `carriedWithin_lt_of_loop`); how highly is the strengths along the loops and how often they act, and the carried amount reads as how far and how fast an act's difference can grow in it. This reading is not identified with quantum entanglement [AI reading, D39].

**What this does not derive.** That a formation knows its reach closure, or wants anything; significance itself, which stays supplied (`Web.significance_undetermined`); the adding comparisons in positive form for every significance, which hold where significance is decidable (`Flow.Balances.adds_iff_exceeds_of_decidable`) and otherwise only up to double negation; how others would respond, since a comparison in which they respond needs the second flow supplied.

<a id="611-公理的分解自身的预设与不变的一步p43-the-axiom-decomposed-the-selfs-presupposition-and-the-step-of-invariance-p43"></a>
<a id="611-公理的分解自身的预设与不变的一步--the-axiom-decomposed-the-selfs-presupposition-and-the-step-of-invariance"></a>
<a id="611-the-axiom-decomposed-the-selfs-presupposition-and-the-step-of-invariance"></a>

### 6.11 The condition decomposed: the self's presupposition and the step of invariance

In “Truth exist” Ren wrote: “There is a set of universal invariations, that for every universe, becoming happens, the law follows”, that it is “the bases of all ethics, for all beings, in all universes”, and “There is no absolute self, and the continuation goes beyond one’s cycle of life, goes beyond one’s death.” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-truth-exists-opening.md)). Earlier Ren had written that the self is “a cognitive model which can not justify itself without some sort of "belief"”, that “we” are “the universe/ecosystem that realizes its own existence” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-axiom-route-levels-and-possibilities.md)), and that a center's “boundary” “is also with in the same cognitive system” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-centers-within-the-dynamics.md)).

The AI proposed, in its own words ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-strongest-claims-direction.md)), that the condition does not follow from the dynamics but may follow from three claims: every act presupposes that some continuation matters; there is no absolute self; truth is the same for every being. The one step a critic must deny is the move from “the continuation I act for” to “continuation as such”. The AI proposed stating that step exactly, checking its skeleton in Lean and having it independently attacked. The three claims are the AI's reading of Ren's words, not claims shown to be Ren's own. Ren answered: “continue, let's pursue the strongest claims and complete proof”.

This section states the result ([Becoming.Boundaries](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Boundaries.lean), [study section 62](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#62-公理的分解p43)). It is an exact decomposition of the condition that locates the step and does not lower the condition's content. Logically it is the identity A ⇔ P ∧ (P → A), given that A implies P, with supervaluation bookkeeping for P: it locates the contested step and derives nothing beyond it. The theory asserts neither side.

- **Boundaries of a self; super-truth and sub-truth** ([presupposition-scopes](../results/records/presupposition-scopes.md)). Along a relation, a boundary of a formation's self is a region that holds the formation and is closed under the relation (`Boundary`). The closure is the least boundary and the whole the greatest (`Boundary.closure`, `Boundary.whole`); where everything lies within the formation's closure, the whole is its only boundary (`Boundary.only_whole`). A claim about a self is *super-true* when it holds however the boundary is drawn among the admissible ones (`SuperTrue`), *sub-true* when it holds as the boundary is drawn somewhere (`SubTrue`), and *boundary-invariant* when, holding as drawn anywhere, it holds as drawn everywhere (`BoundaryInvariant`). The first two terms are those of supervaluation (Fine 1975) and subvaluation (Hyde 1997). Super-truth is invariant sub-truth (`superTrue_iff`), and invariance is determinacy: if sub-true, the claim is super-true (`boundaryInvariant_iff_determinate`). What lies within the self however its boundary is drawn is exactly the closure, while everything lies within it as drawn somewhere (`superTrue_member_iff`, `subTrue_member`): along a relation, no formation is a clear non-member of any self.
- **The presupposition and its two scopes.** From a standpoint, with the boundary drawn at a region, the presupposition is that the continuation of everything within the region has significance (`SignificantWithin`). Super-true, it is significance over the union of the admissible boundaries; where the whole is admissible, as along any relation, it is the condition at that standpoint, whatever the relation (`superTrue_significantWithin_iff_union`, `unrestricted_iff_superTrue`, `superTrue_level_free`). Sub-true along a relation, it is significance over the closure (`subTrue_significantWithin_iff`). Along the empty relation that is the significance of the center's own continuation, a standpoint's own good; along the total relation it is the condition at that standpoint (`subTrue_empty_iff`, `subTrue_total_iff`); along a larger relation it says more (`subTrue_of_larger`). Route (b)'s significance, bounded by each center's closure, is the significance of what lies definitely within the self, and the least significance that makes every presupposition sub-true (`bounded_closure_iff`, `bounded_closure_least`). So route (b) and the condition are one presupposition, with the operator “however the boundary is drawn” at narrow scope and at wide scope (`presupposition_scopes`); the operator does not distribute over the conditional. This extends route (b) from feeding to any relation.
- **The condition decomposed: where its content lies** ([axiom-decomposition](../results/records/axiom-decomposition.md)). Let every formation lie within some admissible boundary of every self (covering), let each standpoint's presupposition be sub-true, and let it be boundary-invariant. Then nothing restricts significance (`unrestricted_of_presupposed`). Where the whole is an admissible boundary of every self, the condition is exactly the presupposition sub-true and invariant at every standpoint (`unrestricted_iff_presupposed`); along any relation the whole is admissible, so there it is exactly that (`unrestricted_iff_presupposed_along`). Wherever the whole is admissible, invariance is the conditional from the presupposition to the condition at that standpoint (`boundaryInvariant_iff_conditional`); given the presupposition, invariance is the condition there (`boundaryInvariant_iff_all`), which section 6.8 reads as boundary neutrality. So the decomposition locates the step and does not lower the condition's content. The step runs both ways: where some continuation lacks significance, an invariant presupposition is nowhere true (`not_subTrue_of_invariant`). Along a relation, covering holds by construction, because every closed region holding the center, the whole included, counts as an admissible drawing of the self. Given the presupposition at every standpoint, and every formation within some center's closure, boundary-invariance is equivalent to standpoint-invariance (`StandpointInvariant`, `boundaryInvariant_iff_standpointInvariant`), the rule of standpoint neutrality; without the presupposition everywhere the two come apart (`OneCounts.forms_apart`). So these premises carry two decompositions already in the core over to the significance of continuation: `Valuation.significant_iff` (a seed and both neutralities on a connected whole) and `Valuation.significant_iff_held` (self-significance at every standpoint with standpoint neutrality). Agreement between standpoints and living significance at the level of claims follow from them (`retains_agree_of_presupposed`, `Valuation.significant_of_presupposed`).
- **Each premise is needed.** In `Clearing`, where nothing has significance, the presupposition is invariant and the whole is admissible, and the condition fails (`Clearing.without_presupposition`). Significance bounded by each formation's feeding closure makes every presupposition sub-true with the whole admissible, and the condition fails; at the clearer the presupposition is not boundary-invariant, and significance is not standpoint-invariant (`Clearing.without_invariance`, `Clearing.without_standpoint_invariance`). If each self's only admissible boundary were its feeding closure, the same significance would make every presupposition sub-true and invariant, the dweller would lie within no admissible boundary of the clearer, and the condition would still fail (`Clearing.with_fixed_boundary`).
- **A part within reach.** Sub-true along a relation that holds affecting, the presupposition compares every part within the center's closure as the condition does (`Flow.Balances.presupposed_comparison`): along affecting, every part within the center's reach closure (`Flow.Balances.presupposed_reach_comparison`); along sharing a condition, every part within its system. Neither invariance nor super-truth is used. Sub-truth along sharing a condition gives sub-truth along affecting (`Flow.presupposition_order`), so the premise along affecting is the weaker. Sub-truth depends on the relation and super-truth does not (`Clearing.levels_differ`). Drawn along sharing a condition, or held however the boundary is drawn, the clearer's own presupposition gives, from its own standpoint, that its part loses the dweller; drawn at the feeding closure, it is met by a significance under which the part retains (`Clearing.own_presupposition`). These are corollaries of the comparison of section 6.10 ([comparison-within-reach](../results/records/comparison-within-reach.md)).
- **Where the closure is the whole.** Where every formation lies within each center's closure along a relation, the whole is the only boundary, so invariance holds trivially and the condition is the presupposition sub-true at every standpoint (`unrestricted_iff_presupposed_of_whole`). Along sharing a condition this holds wherever one condition is taken part in by everything (`Flow.unrestricted_iff_system_presupposed`, `Flow.unrestricted_iff_presupposed_of_common`), as in `Clearing` (`Clearing.system_presupposition`). The step is not removed there: it has moved into drawing the self's boundary closed under the relation.

**Reading.**
- The presupposition reads the “belief” without which, Ren wrote, the self “can not justify itself”. Ren wrote this of the self's limit. Using the belief as a premise is the AI's step, and sub-truth along a relation is far stronger than that some continuation has significance.
- Covering: Ren's “we” being “the universe/ecosystem that realizes its own existence” supports the whole as one drawing of “we”; “There is no absolute self” does not by itself give covering. Along a relation covering holds because every closed region holding the center, the whole included, counts as an admissible drawing of the self. That admissibility is the substantive choice, and it is why the admissible class is not a supervaluation of the ordinary, vague “self”: it has no clear non-members.
- Ren's “for all beings” reads most directly as standpoint-invariance, the rule of standpoint neutrality; given the presupposition and covering, boundary-invariance is equivalent to it. Ren's “universal invariations” are invariances of the law across universes; extending them to significance is the AI's reading. To deny the step is to hold the presupposition true only relative to a drawing or only from a standpoint, or, since the step runs both ways, to give up the presupposition. The step takes the place of the earlier route's tolerance ([review](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/EVALUATOR-GROUNDING-REVIEW.md)) and, like tolerance with Chain, carries all of the condition's content beyond the presupposition, in one step instead of many small ones. Arguments from an agent's standpoint to impartial ethics are contested at exactly this step: agent-relative against agent-neutral reasons (Nagel 1970), and Gewirth's Principle of Generic Consistency (1978) with its critics. Parfit (1984) read the absence of a deep self as weakening the privilege of the self-interest theory, which corresponds here to covering. Nor does the step dissolve what Sidgwick called the dualism of practical reason; it locates that dualism in this step. This placement among the literature is argued, not formal.
- Where the rivals sit. Along a relation, covering holds by construction, so the rivals deny another premise. A rival that grounds significance in a formed closure denies sub-truth along any relation under which that closure is not closed, as operational closure is not closed under feeding. Along a relation under which it is closed, it denies invariance: it holds its presupposition only at that closure, as route (b) does at the feeding closure. It denies covering only if it also restricts the admissible drawings to that closure. A vague self denies covering where every admissible drawing leaves the stranger out, and invariance where the stranger is borderline. A graded weight that falls to zero denies sub-truth if it falls inside the closure, and invariance if beyond it. A present-aim evaluator denies sub-truth. The first two bear in the same way on the earlier tolerance route to living significance. This section locates these denials; it does not refute them.
- Ren's order of levels, “the whole, and a weaker version is within the system that one is in, then the self”, is an order of readings of the presupposition: super-true; sub-true along affecting, at the reach closure offered in section 6.10 for the system one is in; and sub-true at the self, along the empty relation. The weaker version gives the condition's comparison on every part within one's reach. The reach closure points forward: on this reading the order runs down to the bare self, not through the feeding closure, which lies on another branch (`Flow.presupposition_order`) [AI reading, D21].

**What this does not derive.** Covering, sub-truth and invariance themselves: the presupposition is supplied as every significance is (`Web.significance_undetermined`), and along a relation it already carries transmission along that relation, from the bare own good of the empty relation to the condition of the total one. That a center knows its reach closure, since its own feedback carries at most its influence closure (`Flow.Balances.uncoupled_beyond_influence`). The step from significance to a reason, to motivation or to practice. Living significance is not an axiom: the audit of its uses is clean (section 6.12).

<a id="612-g0-的各处用法在公理之下p44-g0s-uses-under-the-axiom-p44"></a>
<a id="612-g0-的各处用法在公理之下--g0s-uses-under-the-axiom"></a>
<a id="612-g0s-uses-under-the-condition"></a>

### 6.12 The uses of living significance under the condition

This audit goes through every use of living significance in the core and the periphery and asks what each needs of positive relevance and of `living` ([Becoming.Relevance](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Relevance.lean), [study section 64](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md)). The audit is clean, with one reading forced. Under the condition, C read on possibilities decides the overdraw that section 6.5 left to the choice of reading. Positive relevance is read from significance, and courses are compared on possibilities (below).

- **Every use is discharged (under the condition; by the definition of `Frame.relevance`)** ([one-axiom-yields-g0](../results/records/one-axiom-yields-g0.md)). Every theorem that takes living significance (`G0`) as a hypothesis holds for an arbitrary positive relevance. Read from significance, positive relevance is realized living content whose continuation has significance from every standpoint (`Frame.relevance`). Under the condition it satisfies living significance on every frame for every reading of `living` (`g0_of_unrestricted`), and it is the least positive relevance satisfying it, holding of exactly the realized living content (`relevance_least`). Both are one-line unpackings of the condition. So under the condition it discharges every use; the bridge states this for the periphery's interface and its three imported theorems (`inventory_g0_of_unrestricted`). Characterizations are equivalences, and countermodels satisfy the condition with significance everywhere; neither is affected.
- **The condition is living significance for anything.** On frames whose claims are formations at steps, each realized somewhere, the condition holds exactly when living significance holds with every claim living and positive relevance read as significance from every standpoint (`unrestricted_iff_g0`); on the level paths of every web every claim is so realized, so there it holds outright (`Web.unrestricted_iff_paths_g0`). The condition is therefore no foreign premise: it widens the scope of living significance from living content to anything and weakens “positive” to “has significance”.
- **The reason premise must be read on living content.** Where everything has significance, A read with bare significance gives a reason for every response yielding any new content, living or not; read on living content, it gives one for every response securing new living content (`articulates_total`). In a witness whose only new content is not living, the first still forces a reason (`Widening.bare_forces_reason`). Read on living content, A gives D, and under the condition the universal reason follows with no seed, neutrality or connection premise (`universal_reason_of_unrestricted`). A itself remains a premise, and it is a neutrality premise (`Neutral`).
- **Under the condition, C is read on possibilities** ([ranking-under-readings](../results/records/ranking-under-readings.md)). The possibility reading includes a formation wherever a course's levels admit it, active or not. For courses of one law, under the condition, extension under this reading is retaining and adding what has significance (`Ranking.possible_extends_iff`). As the whole-run reading does, it ranks the absence of an overdrawing formation above its occurrence, restraint above overdraw (also with a third formation), and sharing above winning by consumption. It withholds no event: the malignant formation's first event is included, which the whole-run reading withholds (`Ranking.malignant_possible`). The ranking comes from what the part loses, not from discounting the formation itself.
- **What it does not rank, and possibilities first.** A formation drawing within renewal adds no possibility: its presence and its absence extend neither the other, while both readings of `living` rank its presence higher (`Ranking.possible_tie`, `Ranking.readings_rank_presence`). Ordering by possibilities first and then by a reading satisfies C and ranks that presence above the absence, but it also ranks a rest that adds no possibility below uninterrupted activity (`Ranking.possibleFirst_order`, `Ranking.possibleFirst_presence`, `Ranking.Rest.rest_ranked_below`); it is a further premise.

**Positive relevance is read from significance** (Ren proposed this on 11 October 2026: “Positive relevance - yes, let's fold”, [words](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-11-part-ii-into-part-i.md)). Positive relevance is the significance of realized living content (`Frame.relevance`), not a separate reading. Significance need not be positive; what is positive is the living content, and the AI read this as 生生. This reading of Ren's “doesn't have to be positive” is the AI's, and every current reading of `living` reads “not damaging” without further generation (section 6.3). The possibility reading reads “what has significance is what can form” (section 6.8) and Ren's “more possibilities of becoming”; it counts what can form, not what does.

**Courses are compared on possibilities**, a reading adopted for the reasons below (Ren left the choice open on 11 October 2026: “Comparing courses - you decide”). One course extends another exactly when its levels admit every formation the other's admit and one more. The reasons:
1. Under the condition, it is the condition's own comparison, retaining and adding what has significance (`Ranking.possible_extends_iff`), with no further premise; each alternative adds one, a reading of `living` or an order. Since the condition is not asserted (section 6.15), this reason holds only conditionally; reasons 2–4 do not use it.
2. It is the comparison of the generative space: possibilities are what can form next ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-axiom-route-levels-and-possibilities.md)), and being generative is that a part's “existence creates more possibilities of becoming” (Ren's words, read in section 6.13).
3. It ranks higher what should rank higher: the absence of an overdrawing formation, restraint (also with a third formation) and sharing (`Ranking.possible_ranks`, `Ranking.possible_restraint`, `Ranking.ThirdParty.possible_extends`, `Ranking.possible_contest`), while withholding no event (`Ranking.malignant_possible`).
4. A course whose levels are nowhere lower is never ranked below (`Web.not_added_of_le`), so rest and restraint are never worse, as not harm asks.

What it leaves unranked is the presence or absence of a formation that adds no possibility (`Ranking.possible_tie`): that is the value of bringing new lives into existence, which section 6.6 does not treat. Possibilities first would decide it by an order, and would also rank a rest that adds no possibility below uninterrupted activity (`Ranking.Rest.rest_ranked_below`), counting a pause as a loss. The theorems of the other two comparisons stay, and a stronger argument for either would revise this reading.

**What this does not derive.** The normative warrant of the condition; the step from significance to a reason (A), which remains a premise. Since the audit is clean, living significance needs no axiom of its own: the theory states it as a condition characterized with no axiom (section 5), and under the condition of section 6.15 it is a theorem.

<a id="613-生成的空间不用公理p56-the-generative-space-with-no-axiom-p56"></a>
<a id="613-生成的空间不用公理--the-generative-space-with-no-axiom"></a>

### 6.13 The generative space, with no axiom

Ren asked on 11 October 2026: “can we reduce is "for anything, its continuation into the future leads to more futures(flourishes/plurality) from generatively" - so we can be like "these are space where generatively happens", which allows us to derive everything without axiom?”, and proposed: “yes, let's get all of no axiom work enclosed, then we will get into 1 axiom case, as long as the derivation of the rules doesn't rely on axiom, don't use one” ([words](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-11-generative-space-no-axiom.md)). The condition only converts significance into possibility: under it, retaining what has significance is inclusion and adding is exceeding (section 6.8). Read directly in possibilities, as “leads to more futures”, every comparison the condition supported is a law with no axiom ([Becoming.Regions](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Regions.lean), [Becoming.Significance](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Significance.lean)).

- **The generative space.** One level path *widens* another when it admits every formation the other admits and, at some step, one more (`Web.Widens`). Two *keep the same* when each includes the other (`Web.Keeps`), and two are *mixed* when each exceeds the other (`Web.Mixed`). A part is generative where the levels with it widen those without it, and degenerative where those without it widen those with it. Widening goes one way and is transitive, and the four cases exclude one another (`Web.Widens.not_reverse`, `Web.Widens.trans`, `Web.Widens.not_keeps`, `Web.Widens.not_mixed`, `Web.Keeps.not_mixed`). No significance, standpoint or boundary enters.
- **The boundary is exact, with balance only where it is used.** With balance at the conditions each formation requires, across the steps before it, the levels with a part widen those without it exactly when the part's net draw stays within the margin for every formation the levels without it admit and, at some step, its net exchange brings within the margin a formation they do not admit (`Flow.widens_iff_within_margin_at`; in a balanced flow, `Flow.Balances.widens_iff_within_margin`). The boundaries of retaining, losing and adding what has significance hold alike, for any significance, with balance only at the conditions of the continuations compared (`Flow.retains_iff_within_margin_at`, `Flow.loses_iff_beyond_margin_at`, `Flow.adds_iff_within_margin_at`).
- **C's extension on level paths is exactly widening (definitional).** On level paths, an extension in which every claim counts as living is exactly a widening (`Web.paths_extension_iff_widens`). So with “better” read as widening what can form, C holds for every reading of `living`, with no axiom (`Web.paths_comparison_widens`). The proof is an unfolding: the normative content of C has moved into calling widening better. That widening is better is the open question of warrant (section 8), not something this theorem shows.
- **The whole first.** The comparison over the whole is the comparison within every region at once (`Web.includes_iff_within_every`): a region's comparison takes the region as an input, while the whole's takes no boundary as an input.
- **The conditional case.** Under the condition, widening is retaining and adding what has significance, keeping the same is retaining both ways, and being mixed is adding both ways (`Web.widens_iff_retains_adds`, `Web.keeps_iff_retains`, `Web.mixed_iff_adds`): the reduction to possibilities loses nothing. The rules above are derived with no axiom, so they are stated with none. These three take the condition as a hypothesis (section 6.15).

**What it does not derive.** “More futures” is a partial order: mixed parts still need a measure to be ordered. The sentence works as the definition of a region, not as a fact about everything: some parts lessen what can form. What does not come along is the “ought”: that narrowing a future which never returns to oneself is wrong for oneself. That is the condition's step of invariance (section 6.11). After the reduction it becomes the content of calling the generative space good, an open question of warrant and no premise of any law (section 8).

**The open-form result, connected on 13 October 2026.** The audit of section 6.12 now has its actual Lean calls for `OpenFutureRevision`: `Becoming.Bridge` holds two complete producer-to-consumer adapters (`positive_ground_by_bound_of_unrestricted`, `revised_generation_by_bound_of_unrestricted`), and on the shared `Frame` and `Run`, `Run.novel_by_handoff` and `Run.relevance_by_handoff_of_unrestricted`, which connect the same witness of finite actual formation to the common definition of positive relevance. The four declarations have empty axiom reports. The significance condition stays an explicit hypothesis, and the earlier counterexamples, the operational correspondence and the practical premises are kept. This is a formal connection of an [existing result](../results/records/open-form-realization-under-revision.md), not a new normative principle or an ordering of mixed losses. The [completion audit](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/RISK-CONDITIONING-INTEGRATION.md#spine-bridge-completion) closes the general to-do items already completed by the purpose-revision and work-in-progress results.

<a id="comparison-content-under-regrouping"></a>
<a id="614-比较内容怎样由关系约束p91-relational-constraints-on-comparison-content-p91"></a>
<a id="614-比较内容怎样由关系约束--relational-constraints-on-comparison-content"></a>

### 6.14 Relational constraints on comparison content

First the dependence, giving and taking within one unfolding say what can continue; then these contents are compared. Renaming, or changing the grain of description, adds no generation by itself. [Regrouping](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Regrouping.lean) keeps the original process and only merges its claims: a group is realized exactly when some member is realized. The exact local condition is that members of a group hold alike in the configurations read; that is exactly what allows each original content to be recovered from the grouping. Where the condition holds at both places compared, the existing extension comparison is unchanged.

The condition can be given by actual support relations. For the reading “what can form next”, the same dependences and thresholds suffice. Where requirements are decidable, merging is lossless under every level test **exactly when** the members have the same least requirement at every step (a condition not depended on requiring zero). This characterizes admitted content, not the identity of lives: having the same requirements does not make different lives interchangeable. Where joint realization or later effects matter, they must be kept as comparison content. Local fidelity concerns only the courses being compared; the condition at all levels has a stronger scope.

The converse is tested by two feasible courses generated by one law of exchange. Carrying one unit of support opens a second formation and closes the first; leaving it in place does the opposite. The two courses share the initial state and the rest of the law of exchange, and both are balanced and feasible. Merge the two formations with different dependences into one class, and the grouped description shows the two courses alike while the actual gains and losses are mixed. What is found here is an omission in a description. It does not refute the full relational account with an arbitrary label, and it does not say that carrying or not carrying is better in itself.

**Scope.** On one unchanged process, faithful regrouping preserves the existing extension comparison. This compares possibilities of support, not identities or all joint futures. The result supplies a representation condition to the existing comparison; it imposes losslessness on no action and ranks no mixed losses. The significance condition and the stronger zero-substantive-axiom foundation keep their separate scopes. See the [result](../results/records/comparison-content-under-regrouping.md) and the [argument, connection and counterreview](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ETHICAL-LEARNING-INTEGRATION.md#relational-comparison-content).

<a id="615-若任何东西的延续都有意义--if-the-continuation-of-anything-has-significance"></a>

### 6.15 If the continuation of anything has significance

<a id="第二部分一条公理--part-ii-one-axiom"></a>

**This section is conditional: the theory does not assert its condition.** It takes the continuation condition as a hypothesis and states what follows under it and what does not.

**Dated note.** Living significance was an adopted axiom until 11 October 2026, and the continuation condition was asserted, in a separate part, until 16 October 2026; both were then restated as conditions the theory does not assert ([11 October](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-11-fold-g0.md), [16 October](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-16-fold-part-ii.md)). The labels of that history are mapped in section 10, under “Retired labels”.

Ren proposed the condition in these words ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-wanting-continuation-one-axiom.md)):

> if we want to add one axiom, that is for anything, its continuation into the future has significance(doesn't have to be positive, we could argue as long as not harm), or continuation into the future needs to create more significance(being generative)

Of the routes, Ren proposed route (a) first: “let's aim for a first, then try to archive b” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-axiom-route-levels-and-possibilities.md)); and on 11 October 2026 the order of the work: “yes, let's get all of no axiom work enclosed, then we will get into 1 axiom case, as long as the derivation of the rules doesn't rely on axiom, don't use one” ([words](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-11-generative-space-no-axiom.md)). The condition reads as boundary neutrality for the significance of continuation (`Unrestricted`, section 6.8): from every standpoint, the continuation of anything has significance at every step. The represented dynamics contain no significance, so they do not entail the condition: significance is a free parameter of every law (`Web.significance_undetermined`).

**Read as significance, the generative space loses nothing.** Under the condition, widening is retaining and adding what has significance, keeping the same is retaining both ways, and being mixed is adding both ways (`Web.widens_iff_retains_adds`, `Web.keeps_iff_retains`, `Web.mixed_iff_adds`). The rules of section 6.13 are derived without the condition, and these three equivalences are their form when read as significance. So the condition adds no rule. What it says is that the widening section 6.13 characterizes with no axiom is retaining and adding what has significance.

**What the condition settles.** At least 26 theorems of the core take `Unrestricted` as a hypothesis (count as of 2026-10-17; the bridge has 7 more; the [inventory of 11 October](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/PART-II-INVENTORY.md#31-用到公理的-22-条--the-22-that-use-the-axiom) counted 22 then). Under the condition they give:
- Living significance, with self-significance at every standpoint, both neutralities and a seed wherever something lives, on the stepwise, relation-level and whole-run readings, with no connection (`Valuation.premises_of_unrestricted`, `Web.significant_of_unrestricted`, `Conditions.significant_of_unrestricted`). The step is immediate: living significance is the condition restricted to living claims, and the condition gives significance to every claim, not a positive valence. Positive relevance is read from significance: under the condition it is exactly the realized living content (`relevance_least`), and living significance holds (`g0_of_unrestricted`).
- Retaining what has significance is inclusion and adding is exceeding, alike from every standpoint (`Web.retains_iff_includes`, `Web.adds_iff_exceeds`, `Web.retains_agree`). The margin boundaries hold for any significance, as arithmetic (section 6.13); the condition only makes them cover every formation. The boundary of not harm is the margin of a part's net draw, and being generative is adding what has significance (section 6.8).
- Under the condition, what a part closes has significance. Without the condition, a part can continue while closing what lies beyond its dependence (`Clearing.loses_beyond`); under the condition the free rider loses the dweller from every standpoint (`Clearing.free_rider_loses`).
- Under the condition, what retains in the whole retains within every region (`Web.Retains.within`), and where a feeding closure is the whole, route (b) judges as the condition does (`Flow.within_closure_iff_retains`).
- Under the condition, with A read on living content, the universal reason needs no seed, neutrality or connection premise (`universal_reason_of_unrestricted`, section 6.12). A remains, and it is itself a neutrality premise.
- Under the condition, comparing courses on possibilities is the condition's comparison: retaining and adding what has significance (`Ranking.possible_extends_iff`, section 6.12).

Twenty more theorems of the core state the condition in their conclusion rather than assuming it: the condition is living significance for anything where every claim is realized somewhere (`unrestricted_iff_g0`, `Web.unrestricted_iff_paths_g0`); it is decomposed (`unrestricted_iff_presupposed` and its forms, section 6.11); significance bounded by the whole is the condition (`bounded_whole`); and witnesses show where it fails (`Clearing.without_presupposition`, `Clearing.without_invariance`, `Clearing.with_fixed_boundary`).

**The step of invariance.** Beyond the self's presupposition, everything the condition adds lies in one step (section 6.11). The self's presupposition is that, from a standpoint, the continuation of what lies within its boundary has significance (`SignificantWithin`). The step of invariance is that this presupposition does not change with how the self's boundary is drawn (`BoundaryInvariant`). Where the boundary is drawn along any relation, the whole is always an admissible drawing, and the condition is exactly the presupposition holding at each standpoint's closure together with this step (`unrestricted_iff_presupposed_along`). The general form, `unrestricted_iff_presupposed`, needs the whole to be an admissible boundary of every self, and `unrestricted_of_presupposed` needs covering. Where the whole is admissible, the step is the conditional from the presupposition to the condition at that standpoint (`boundaryInvariant_iff_conditional`), and given the presupposition it is the condition there (`boundaryInvariant_iff_all`). So the decomposition locates the step and does not lower the condition. Without the condition, route (b) reaches the self and what it depends on (section 6.9). What the step adds is significance for the continuations whose loss returns to no one. After the reduction to possibilities, it is the content of calling the generative space good (section 6.13). To deny it is to hold the presupposition only relative to a drawing or only from a standpoint; since the step runs both ways, one can also give up the presupposition (section 6.11).

**What the condition does not settle.** Under the condition significance is the same everywhere, so every narrowing loses something with significance, predation, apoptosis and ending a cancer included. Such parts are mixed, and telling harm from what is not harm among them needs a measure, which the condition does not supply (“no ending is licensed”, section 6.8). On level paths C holds because “better” is read as widening (section 6.13), not because of the condition. A, C, L and the practical commitments stay disclosed conditions of the practical laws. Ordering mixed parts needs a measure. The level at which formations are individuated fixes what counts as a loss. The normative warrant of the condition itself is open (section 8), and no route that would make significance a function of the world's own data has been attempted. The alignment direction of section 7 is stated under this condition and the disclosed practical premises.

<a id="第一部分的总陈述--part-i-stated-whole"></a>
<a id="part-i-stated-whole"></a>

## The theory stated whole

This section closes the theory without axiom. The organization is the AI's, following Ren's proposal “let's aim for the strongest claim without axiom, as the first part”. It states the whole in prose; the full catalogue of its declarations, with each theorem's name and conditions, is kept in the chapter's reference under “Declaration catalogue of the whole”.

**What “no axiom” means here.** The theory uses no axiom, ethical or substantive. Every theorem it cites is checked in Lean, and every source declaration of the core has an empty axiom report: no `propext`, `Quot.sound` or `Classical.choice` (section 9). That is a constructive discipline, not an absence of premises. Every substantive premise enters as a hypothesis of the theorem that uses it, and stated so, “no axiom” by itself excludes nothing. What the theory adds is that the hypotheses are named and local, and that the premises of a world are shown independent. The representation brings commitments of its own. The declared ones are discrete steps, natural-number amounts and a shared step index, which is one global linear order of steps. The representation also builds in more: every condition is a natural-number stock; dependence is admission at a threshold (need at most level); change is exchange of quantity, with a rest relatum that absorbs any imbalance; there is a least unit, and dissipation loses at least one unit per uptake; and formations come from a type given in advance. That what is counted is decided is the condition of the laws that count or name a step (`Becoming.Decidedness`); where a counting law quantifies over decided activity of every relatum, that global decidability is a condition of the law, not yet a local one. The remarks between theorems, for example that whether a premise holds is empirical, are not Lean statements.

**I. What holds of every unfolding and every law of change.** With no premise about the unfolding, the theory states the readings of one change (`change_readings`, which holds by definition), the identity of an amount over a stretch (`over_stretch`), comings and ceasings, with holding decided, keeping pace within a family's size (`keep_pace`), the tally as the whole law of counting for properties of the counts read step by step (`counting_laws`), and the laws of one step, each with its condition inside it. These laws are definitional or arithmetic, and they exclude no unfolding beyond what the representation excludes; they locate the claims about the whole rather than add dynamical content. Of every law of change, again with no premise and with “a list holds the configurations” written into each law that uses it: within finitely many configurations a law merges nothing exactly where every configuration returns (`Law.lossless_iff_returns`); a kept history never returns and needs openness; what does not return is exactly what a determined law draws in; and within finitely many configurations a run is a course into a return. Asked of every process, a run from every member of every family that keeps moving is exactly dependent choice (`runs_iff_selection`). It also states the normal forms of reorderings, listing once exactly where identity is decided (`listsOnce_iff_decided`), the parity obstruction to gluing contextual readings, with Specker's boxes, CHSH and the Mermin–Peres square as instances (`ParitySystem.glues_iff`), exact bounds for identifying a finite-state history (`Identification.bounded_agree`) and for learning it, typicality as counting of situations, and where the gain behind novelty comes from. The unity theorems (`World.unity`, `Contextual.world`) show that the representations encode one another, for instance that every world's balance is a run of exchange; they are results of encodability, not further facts about worlds.

**II. What each premise adds.** Premises are hypotheses of conditional theorems, not laws. The eight premises of a world are independent: for each, a world meets the other seven and not it (`World.independent`). Under the 256 selections of them, the laws of counting are classified exactly (`World.counting_under`), with the activity of every relatum decided; over counted censuses the same laws hold with no decision procedure and for every property of the counts (`World.counting_under_counted`). With a census, embodiment, balance and dissipation, and every step from some step on enclosed, that the world comes to rest cannot be refuted (`World.rests_unrefuted_enclosed`); arising without end then needs openness (`World.arising_opens`); a step after which nothing arises, asked of every finite world meeting the eight premises with its activity decided, would give the limited principle of omniscience (`World.lpo_of_finite_ends`). These laws of rest rely on the natural-number unit. At a refining unit, if the refinement is not counted, rest fails. Counted as a giving, refinement is excluded by dissipation and enclosure wherever anything stands (`Flow.refinement_gives_nothing`), and rest then follows from the fixed-unit theorem. In a balanced flow the history of exchanges grows at every level change with no dissipation premise, and a finite, lossless, enclosed world can exchange without end (`Passing.passes`). Beyond these, the theory states what dependence and reserve, parts and regions, feeding, feedback and reach, coupling and testing, experiment, runaway, seeking, mechanism and response add. It states how much difference arrives and how a carried difference grows: where the rest follows a law of what has stood and every condition is listed once, the most difference that can arrive is the carried amount, which is attained (`most_difference_arrives`); with, in addition, the same formations and strengths at every step, it is bounded by a polynomial exactly where no loop lies upstream (`gap_polynomial_iff_no_loop`). A return at least doubles what is carried, given natural-number strengths of at least one. Several of these laws depend on the integrality of natural-number amounts: rest, novelty needing gain, bounded parts forgetting, and frequency rather than strength setting runaway. With real levels, a bounded part can be new at every step.

**III. Where the dynamics stop deciding.** The laws above do not decide whether actual unfoldings meet the premises, which is empirical; the step after which nothing arises; the order of mixed parts, which needs a measure; a formation's center; how others respond; where models, moves, laws, targets, descriptions, hypotheses and maps come from; or how many states a situation has, which the situation supplies. They give no part significance: on any web with an event that lives, living significance holds where everything has significance and fails where nothing has (`Web.significance_undetermined`). The represented dynamics contain no significance, so they do not entail it.

**IV. How evaluation holds for any significance (sections 5–6).** With no axiom, for any significance and any valuation: the content of living significance is exactly a seed with both boundary neutralities on a connected whole with a living claim (`Valuation.significant_iff`), and every premise of that characterization is needed. A part retains what has significance exactly within the margin of its net draw, and loses and adds at equally exact boundaries. A part is generative where the levels with it widen those without it. Any significance covering what a part reaches gives the comparison by inclusion. On level paths, C's extension is exactly widening; that holds by definition, and that widening is better is the open question of warrant. The continuation condition is the condition of section 6.15. Under it living significance is immediate: the condition gives significance to every claim, not a positive valence. Where the whole is admissible, the condition is exactly the self's presupposition, sub-true and boundary-invariant at every standpoint; the decomposition locates the contested step of invariance and derives nothing beyond it (section 6.11).

**The deepest part.** The sharpest results map where each principle of classical reasoning enters a law of change. Asking of every finite world that arising recurs exactly when ceasing does is excluded middle (`World.law_iff_em`). Naming the end step for every finite world gives the limited principle of omniscience (`World.lpo_of_finite_ends`), and deciding vanishing as a rule is exactly that principle as a rule (`vanishDecision_iff_lpoRule`). A counterexample to the least coupling exists exactly where a rule decides, of every Boolean sequence, whether it is false throughout: the weak limited principle as a rule (`not_through_sensitive_iff_rule`). Asked of every process, the refutability of passing tests is Markov's principle (`refutable_passes_iff_markov`), the origin law with a proposition as the gate is double-negation elimination (`Gated.origin_of_propositions_iff`), and runs from every member of every family that keeps moving are dependent choice (`runs_iff_selection`).

**What this completeness is.** This is the completeness of a formal statement in the sense of the four checkable criteria of [plan section 9](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-UNIFICATION-PLAN.md#9-走向完整的生成之论--toward-a-complete-theory-of-becoming), not a proof of necessity. The theory aims at the strongest claim with no axiom; that it is the strongest has not been shown, and the formal questions still open are in section 8. The families of the spine meet through correspondence theorems, not by definition. In the [theorem-level measurement](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/THEOREM-CONNECTIVITY.md#8-u-every-spine-theorem-on-the-base) of 17 October 2026, after the join modules of board item U, 195 of 7,698 spine theorems stand apart from both base families (1,019 of 7,214 the day before), and each of the 134 roots among them stands apart for a stated reason: a support lemma, a statement of logical strength alone, a fact about one constructed model, a restatement, a law of every valuation on any type, or a bridge interface. The laws hold of worlds so represented. Whether every becoming can be so represented is part of the warrant still needed (section 8).

<a id="7-ai-对齐--ai-alignment"></a>

## 7. AI alignment

This section states the alignment problem from laws the theory proves, and organizes the correction around 明. §7.1–§7.4 use neither the continuation condition nor any practical premise: they say what a selection reaches and what it cannot reach, whatever is valued. The direction of §7.5 is conditional. §7.6 keeps the conditions under which a correction can be carried out, and §7.7 says what is open. The organization around 明 and pooled coupling is the AI's reading ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-18-the-light-and-alignment-for-all.md)). Where §3 says that a formation registers a condition, this section says that it couples into the condition, by threshold or by level, the vocabulary the [naming study](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/REGISTRATION-VOCABULARY-STUDY.md#7-decisions-18-october-2026) adopted.

**One unfolding, many positions.** A person, an AI system, a firm and a training process are formations within one unfolding (§1). Each couples into the conditions it depends on by threshold, as whether its need is met, and into those it observes by level (§3). Alignment is therefore not first a relation between separate owners of value, nor a bargain among opposed interests (§4). It concerns what each formation's selections reach within the one relation, and how far that reach can widen.

### 7.1 What a selection reaches

A selection is any claim about level histories: which are kept, rewarded, reproduced or trained toward. It goes through a formation's couplings when histories alike through those couplings are selected alike (`Web.SelectsThrough`; §3, “Selection through registration”).

- **Exactly what the selector couples into.** Two histories are selected alike by every selection through `g`'s couplings exactly when they are alike through them, with no premise (`Web.selects_alike_iff`). Through a group's couplings taken together, they are selected alike exactly when they are alike through every member's (`Web.selects_alike_all_iff`).
- **Training and models.** Training is selection through the data (`Training.loss_through`). A fit is right beyond the data exactly where the class the training can reach is determined there by the data (`Training.fit_right_iff`). A selection through a model, a map of the alternatives, is right about what matters only where what matters goes alike across the model's lumping (`Learning.selects_right_through`). Where the model lumps two alternatives that what matters separates, no selection through it is right; that takes a finer model (`Learning.selects_not_right_of_parted`).
- **Only downstream.** Under every law of exchange that reads a schedule, two courses apart at the start only in one condition stand alike at every condition not downstream of it (`apart_only_downstream`). Nothing beyond a formation's influence closure exchanges with a condition that a member of the closure depends on (`Flow.beyond_influence`).
- **Optimizing harder does not reach further.** These laws hold for every selection through the couplings, however it is computed, so a stronger optimizer through the same couplings separates nothing they leave alike. What a selection can separate grows only with what couples in: a selection through couplings stays one under more observation (`Web.SelectsThrough.widen`), and where a formation observes a stock, every raise of it makes a difference through the formation's couplings at every step (`Web.observed_raise_couples`).

*Reading* [AI reading, D25]. For AI training, the reward process is the selector, and training reaches only what the reward couples into.

### 7.2 What a selection cannot reach: 无明 in alignment

Each law holds under the conditions it names; none uses the condition of §6.15.

1. **Beyond the coupling closure.** With the rest of the exchange fixed and balance on what the closure couples into, every selection through the couplings of a formation within `f`'s coupling closure selects the levels with and without a part beyond the closure alike (`Flow.unselected_beyond_coupling`). Pooling members of the closure changes nothing (`Flow.unselected_beyond_coupling_all`). The gap is the reach closure less the coupling closure. In `Clearing` the dweller lies within the clearer's reach closure and beyond its coupling closure, and is ended in only one of two worlds that every selection through the clearer's couplings selects alike (`Clearing.unselected`).
2. **One's own act** ([the-gap-for-ones-own-act](../results/records/the-gap-for-ones-own-act.md)). Let the rest follow one law of exchange reading through coupling. An act whose difference stays beyond the actor's coupling closure is selected alike, with and without it, by every selection through any member of the closure, the actor included (`Flow.act_unselected`). A consequence that holds with the act and fails without it is a selection through no member of the closure (`Flow.consequence_unselected`). The rest's response is derived from the law, and where both acts are laws nothing is supplied (`act_courses_unselected`).
3. **Raising what couples in** ([raising-what-couples-in-draws-from-beyond](../results/records/raising-what-couples-in-draws-from-beyond.md)). Let a family containing the conditions a group couples into gain nothing across a step. A rise of what the group couples into is then paid in full from beyond it (`Flow.Balances.rise_paid_from_beyond`). So every selection that holds only where what the group couples into rises holds only on flows in which some formation takes from a condition no member of the group couples into (`Flow.favours_reaching_beyond`). Whether what is taken is depended on is open (§8.2).
4. **The distance to the break** ([runaway-distance-unregistered](../results/records/runaway-distance-unregistered.md)). Let a stock be the only requirement of `g` that may be unmet up to a horizon, and let `g` not observe it. Some world with the stock raised keeps `g` supported up to the horizon and is alike through `g`'s couplings at every step at which `g` is supported (`Web.break_not_bounded`): nothing `g` couples into bounds how far off the break is. With the rest fixed, a policy through `g`'s couplings that keeps its support never uses a larger stock it does not observe (`Flow.policy_keeps_difference`), and the difference on what it couples into changes only after its support has failed (`Flow.policy_uses_after_failure`).
5. **Exclusion** ([testing-within-registration](../results/records/testing-within-registration.md)). A claim is excluded only through a difference in what couples in (`Web.Excludes.not_of_alike`). Without a model, a finite history excludes neither the claim that a condition comes to nothing nor the claim that it stays above nothing afterwards, for any formation under any observation (`Web.tail_unexcluded`). Relative to a model, exclusion excludes the model and the claim together (`Web.excludesWithin_iff_conjunction`).
6. **What an act would have done** ([experiment-needs-return](../results/records/experiment-needs-return.md), [counterfactual-is-the-course](../results/records/counterfactual-is-the-course.md)). In a determined process the record fixes no outcome of a move the run has not taken, however much is observed (`Run.not_fixes_of_untaken`). A run that keeps its whole history fixes, about its own acts, only what it did (`keepHistory_fixes_nothing_else`). The counterfactual of an act is the course the same law generates (`counterfactual_is_course`): a law fixes it, and the record of one run does not.
7. **Targets that cannot rest, and capture** ([seeking-boundary](../results/records/seeking-boundary.md), [target-capture](../results/records/target-capture.md)). “More than now”, met at every step, needs an open world (`more_needs_open`). Where what stands runs down at every step from some step on, rises stop by exhaustion, not by enough (`Flow.more_exhausted`). “Above all others” is met at a state by at most one seeker, so two such seekers never rest together (`aboveAll_unique`, `Law.aboveAll_never_rests`). Where every carrier of a target leaves at least a offspring and every other parent at most b, with b < a, the carriers' share rises (`share_rises`). Where the next configuration depends on the rule and not on the state, perturbing the state alone is re-absorbed (`Law.perturbation_reabsorbed`).
8. **Loops** ([how-a-carried-difference-grows](../results/records/how-a-carried-difference-grows.md), [how-often-couplings-act](../results/records/how-often-couplings-act.md)). Where no loop lies upstream of a condition, a finite order of the carried amount is exact there at every step (`carriedWithin_exact_of_no_loop`). Where the couplings hold at every step, what reaches a loop upstream keeps arriving by longer routes, and every finite order leaves something out (`carriedWithin_lt_of_loop`). With natural-number strengths, every return at least doubles what arrives, and returns frequent enough outgrow every polynomial (`runaway_of_returns`). A self-loop whose coupling recurs sparsely carries at most a polynomial amount (`Frequency.Sparse.polynomial`).

*Readings* [AI reading, open].

- **无明 is a position, not a defect.** Every formation, human or AI, selects through its own couplings. These laws say what that position does not see: what lies beyond its coupling closure, its own act's difference there, where what it raises is drawn from, how far off its break is, what a finite history leaves open, what its untaken acts would have done, and whether its target can rest. Runs that keep changing are a feature of the process, not of the target, and are not 无明 (D41).
- **Capability widens reach; correction needs coupling.** Read capability as reach, what a formation's activity can affect. The gap of law 1 is the reach closure less the coupling closure. Capability that widens what a formation reaches, without widening what it couples into, widens the gap, and optimizing harder through the same couplings does not close it (laws 1 and 2). Where the conditions around what a group couples into gain nothing, a reward for raising what it couples into is earned only where some formation takes from what no member couples into (law 3). Where an act's difference reaches a loop, no finite order of its consequences is exact (law 8). This gives a structure to the reading of training in §3 (D25).

### 7.3 明: coupling that reaches what support does not mark

*Reading* [AI reading, D26]. 无明 is the not-seeing that keeps seeking locked onto a target that cannot be met at rest. 明 (míng, “seeing, clarity”) is coupling that reaches what support does not mark; D26 words it with “registration”, which the naming study replaced by coupling. Losses reach a seeker only through what it couples into, so locked seeking is not corrected by its own feedback. It is corrected by measurement, a model, or others (§3, “The boundary condition for 无明”). Each of the three has its law and its limit.

- **Measurement.** Where a formation observes a stock, every raise of the stock makes a difference through the formation's couplings at every step (`Web.observed_raise_couples`), so no raised world stays alike through them as it does in law 4. A route of readings forms by closing, as a loop does (`route_forms_by_closing`). In `Observation.Measure` a formation begins at step T to add a stock's level to a record and to draw on the stock while the record shows enough. At T the loop stock → record → draw forms (`Measure.loop_at`), and in courses apart only in the stock the record is alike up to T and differs by the raise after it (`Measure.couples_after`). *Limit:* a difference reaches a measurement only along routes of reading from where it lies (`apart_only_downstream`).
- **A model.** Relative to a model of the exchange, a formation observing a stock excludes at the first step every false claim that the stock comes to nothing (`Flow.excludes_tail_within`), which without a model no finite history excludes (law 5). With the law given, what one's own act would have done is the course the law generates (`counterfactual_is_course`), which the record of one run does not fix (law 6). *Limit:* exclusion relative to a model excludes the model and the claim together (`Web.excludesWithin_iff_conjunction`), so a wrong model can exclude what holds (`Stationary.model_error`); and a selection through a model is right only where what matters goes alike across its lumping (`Learning.selects_right_through`).
- **Others: pooled coupling** ([what-pooled-coupling-reaches](../results/records/what-pooled-coupling-reaches.md)). A selection through a group's pooled coupling selects alike exactly the histories alike through every member's couplings (`Web.SelectsThroughAll`, `Web.selects_alike_all_iff`). Every member's selection is a pooled selection (`Web.SelectsThrough.all`), and pooled selections combine (`Web.SelectsThroughAll.and`). Some pooled selection separates two histories exactly when the members do not all couple them alike (`Web.separates_all_iff`); with the members listed and each member's coupling of the two decided, exactly when some member couples them apart (`Web.separates_all_iff_member`). In `Clearing`, that the clearer and the dweller are both admitted at the third step goes through their pooled coupling: it holds in the spared world, fails in the cleared one, and goes through neither coupling alone (`Clearing.selected_pooled`). *Limit:* a pool's coupling closure is the union of its members' (`withinClosureOf_iff`), and with the rest fixed and balance on what it couples into, every selection through the pooled coupling of any group within it selects the levels with and without a part beyond it alike (`Flow.unselected_beyond_pool`; for a pool within one formation's closure, `Flow.unselected_beyond_coupling_all`). So a pool reaches beyond a closure only if not all its members lie within it. Lying within the closure does not make a difference separated: the clearer's own part lies within its coupling closure, and the clearer couples the levels with and without it alike at every step (`Clearing.within_closure_unseparated`).

*Readings* [AI reading, open].

- **明 is a relation, not a state.** It is as wide as the couplings joined to a selection, and it widens as measurement, models and others are joined. The three routes have one structure: each joins to a selection a coupling that reaches where the selector's own does not.
- **Holding 明 for others** is keeping the three routes open: models that others can check against what couples into them, measurement that brings far consequences back into what couples in, and pools that include those whose couplings reach where one's own do not. Whether a formation is admitted at a step is a selection through its own couplings (`Web.admits_through`), so a formation whose support an act changes separates, through its own couplings, the worlds with and without the act. In `Clearing` that formation is the dweller, the one the clearer's act ends (`Clearing.selected_by_dweller`). Where the rest follows a law of exchange reading through coupling, such a formation separates the act through every pool holding it, and no pool within the actor's coupling closure does (`Flow.act_selected_by_pool`, §7.4).
- **Correction has to reach the rule.** Where the next configuration depends on the rule, a correction that changes the state alone is re-absorbed from the next step (law 7). So 明 has to reach what a selection rewards and what couples into it, not only the states it produces.
- **Exact scope is part of 明.** A law used beyond the scope it is proved in is a wrong model, and a wrong model can exclude what holds. Keeping each law's scope exact is part of holding 明 for others.

### 7.4 Alignment for all: pooled coupling across intelligences

What a pool's selections reach is proved for any pool, with neither the condition of §6.15 nor a practical premise ([what-pooled-coupling-reaches](../results/records/what-pooled-coupling-reaches.md)).

- **The gap for a pool's act.** Let the rest follow one law of exchange reading through coupling, in balanced flows with the same formations present and a common start, and let an act, which may be a pool's own act taken jointly, have its difference stay beyond the pool's coupling closure. Every selection through the pooled coupling of any group within that closure, the pool among them, then selects the worlds with and without the act alike at every step (`Flow.act_unselected_pool`), so a consequence of the act is a selection through no such group (`Flow.consequence_unselected_pool`); where both acts are laws, nothing is supplied (`act_courses_unselected_pool`).
- **What separates the act.** Under the same conditions, a formation that couples the two worlds apart lies beyond the pool's coupling closure (`Flow.apart_beyond_pool`). A formation whose admission the act changes is one: its admission is a selection through the pooled coupling of every group holding it, from that step on, and of no group within the closure (`Flow.act_selected_by_pool`). Where a list holds the members of a pool and each member's coupling of the two worlds is decided, a pool that separates them has a member that couples them apart, and that member lies beyond the closure (`Flow.separating_member_beyond`). In `Clearing`, every selection through the pooled coupling of the clearer and its source selects the cleared and the spared world alike at every step, and every pool holding the dweller separates them by its admission at the third step (`Clearing.pool_closes_gap`).
- **Difference is what a pool adds.** A pool's selections are one member's exactly when whatever that member couples alike every member couples alike (`Web.pooled_iff_member`). Where the members do not all couple alike two histories that one member couples alike, the pool has a selection that goes through no coupling of that member's (`Web.pool_adds`).

What a pool's acts respond to is proved where its members exchange by a law of exchange, again with neither the condition of §6.15 nor a practical premise ([what-a-pools-acts-respond-to](../results/records/what-a-pools-acts-respond-to.md)).

- **A pool's acts select through its pooled coupling.** Let a law of exchange read through the coupling of each formation outside a part, and let a pool's members lie outside it. Whatever the pool decides by its acts under the law up to a step, such as a reward, a vote or a correction it gives, is then a selection through its pooled coupling (`ExchangeLaw.Through.selectsThroughAll`), so the laws above apply to it with that premise derived. A member that exchanges differently under two histories couples them apart (`ExchangeLaw.Through.apart_of_acts`); where a list holds the members and each member's coupling is decided, a pool whose acts separate two histories has a member that couples them apart (`ExchangeLaw.Through.acts_separate_member`). The bound is exact: where each formation's coupling classes are decided and there is a condition to exchange with, the members exchange alike under two histories across a step by every law reading through coupling exactly when they all couple the two alike up to that step (`ExchangeLaw.acts_alike_all_iff`).
- **Coupling bounds response and does not fix it.** Where a member couples two histories apart and the classes are decided, some law reading through every coupling takes differently under them, and the law that exchanges nothing reads through every coupling too (`ExchangeLaw.response_not_fixed_by_coupling`). In `Clearing`, every pool holding the dweller separates the cleared and the spared world, and every formation outside the clearer's part exchanges alike in the two (`Clearing.separated_unresponded`).
- **The gap for a pool's response.** Under the conditions of the gap for a pool's act, every formation within the pool's coupling closure and outside the acting part exchanges alike with and without the act at every step (`Flow.response_alike_pool`); where both acts are laws, nothing is supplied (`act_courses_response_alike_pool`). Some member of a pool lies within a part's responsive reach (§2, “The comparison where the rest responds”) exactly when some member of the part lies within the pool's coupling closure drawn through both flows (`Flow.pool_within_response_iff`), and where the rest responds through its coupling, a pool beyond that reach does not respond to the part (`Flow.pool_alike_beyond_response`). Under the conditions of `TimedResponse.coupling_alike_before`, no member responds before a difference has entered what it couples into (`TimedResponse.pool_alike_before`).
- **A response returns through the actor's closure.** In `Loop` the second follows a law reading through its coupling. It couples the first's act apart and takes from the first's condition otherwise than without the act, so it lies within the first's coupling closure, and the first is unsupported at the third step with the act and supported without it (`Loop.response_returns`).

*Reading* [AI reading, open]. Alignment, so read, is neither fitting one party to another's preferences nor fitting all to a fixed common utility. It is pooling couplings across intelligences, so that what any member's acts do beyond its own coupling closure couples into some member of the pool, whose acts can respond to it there. The laws above give the reading its exact part. Pooling moves the gap to the pool's coupling closure and does not remove it: what a pool does jointly beyond that closure is separated only through formations beyond it that couple the difference apart, such as those whose support the act changes, and nothing within the closure responds to it. So a pool of intelligences reaches what its acts do to formations outside it only through members whose couplings reach that difference, and its acts respond to that difference only through such members. Coupling makes a response possible and does not make it happen: within the bound, the law each member follows decides. A response reaches an actor's own act only through what the actor's coupling closure couples into, and correction and escalation share that structure, a loop through another's coupling and response. Which loops correct is not proved (§7.7). Members correct one another, if at all, through such loops, and no member's couplings are the pool's.

- **Members who differ.** An AI system may couple into scale, distance and far consequences that a person does not; a person into a lived situation and its stakes, which a system may not. Neither coupling replaces the other: a pool holding both separates what either separates (`Web.member_separates_all`).
- **One ground reaches every layer of selection.** AI capability forms through computation, energy, labour, knowledge, training, use and institutions, and each layer selects: what is computed, trained, deployed, funded and permitted. The laws of §7.2 hold at every layer, and no layer needs a starting point of its own. Each is blind beyond its coupling closure, and each that rewards a rise of what it couples into favours reaching beyond. Where the next configuration depends on the rule and not on the state, a correction that reaches only states is re-absorbed (law 7). Capture is read as such a rule, written by those it rewards (D26), so the pool has to reach the rules.
- **Capability is not authority.** Capability grants no governing authority. The account applies across intelligences in this sense: where the relevant situations are alike, the reasons are alike. It does not predict that different intelligences share one psychology. Read with §7.2, the further a member reaches beyond what it couples into, the more of what it does is seen only through others' couplings: capability is a reason to pool, not to govern.
- **Reasons addressed to AI.** `universal_reason` addresses reasons to the intelligence in every situation, AI included, under its premises (§6.4). Understanding a reason is not complying with it.

### 7.5 The direction, under the condition

**Claim** ([alignment direction](ALIGNMENT-DIRECTION.md); record [alignment-direction-g0-j](../results/records/alignment-direction-g0-j.md)):
