**We propose aligning AI formation, learning, action and revision with actual living and further generation within the whole: enabling richer co-development of life, minds, relationships, ways of living and organization, while governing tradeoffs through real losses, basic living conditions, bounded responsibility and legitimate participation, with effective opportunities to understand, challenge and correct that development.**

**Under what.** This is the conditional alignment route. The direction takes two premises: the continuation condition of §6.15 and the disclosed practical premises. The theory does not assert the condition. §7.1–§7.4 need neither premise. Which differences matter is what the condition and the premises say, and the dynamics do not decide it: on any web with an event that lives, living significance holds where everything has significance and fails where nothing has (`Web.significance_undetermined`).

**One evaluation for every layer.** Under the condition, ends, evaluations and rules of selection also change living and the conditions under which it continues. So they enter the same evaluation, by living significance and the practical commitments, and no layer of selection needs a starting point of its own ([generative alignment through change](ALIGNMENT-THROUGH-CHANGE.md)).

**Where the two parts meet** [AI reading, open]. Read through §7.3, the claim's “effective opportunities to understand, challenge and correct that development” are the routes of 明 kept open: measurement, models others can check, and pools that include those whose couplings reach where an actor's do not. The laws of §7.2 say what makes such an opportunity effective: a correction selects what an act does beyond its actor's coupling closure only through a coupling that reaches it.

### 7.6 Correction must be an executable sequence

A disposition to accept correction does not by itself mean that correction can reach the system and be completed. The checked conditions include:

- [a margin reserved for shutdown during recovery](../results/records/reserved-shutdown-during-recovery.md);
- [recovering evidence after interrupted change, and the bound on reaching correction](../results/records/recoverable-mediated-change.md);
- [the criterion for assured arrival](../results/records/assured-route-criterion.md);
- [the difference between a risk bound and an actual outcome](../results/records/risk-certificate-outcome-distinction.md).

Each of these is a conditional inference about a stated protocol. Deploying any of them needs separate evidence for an identified installation: persistence, complete mediation, a protected stop authority and reserve, bounds on scheduling and delay, and actual stopping effects. More abstract checking does not supply that evidence. §7.3 says what a correction has to couple into; these records state, for their protocols, when it can also be carried out.

### 7.7 What is open

- **Seeing is not valuing.** Widening what couples in changes what a selection can separate, not which differences matter. That is the condition's to say, and its warrant is open (§6.11, §8.5).
- **Coupling is not compliance.** That a system couples into a consequence does not make its selections respond to it. Where a member's law reads through its coupling, coupling bounds what its acts respond to, exactly, and fixes nothing within the bound (`ExchangeLaw.response_not_fixed_by_coupling`; in `Clearing`, `Clearing.separated_unresponded`). Which law a system's acts follow, how training installs one that responds to what it couples into, and the evidence for an identified installation are open (§7.6, §8.7).
- **Which responses correct.** A response reaches an actor's own act only through the actor's coupling closure, and correction and escalation both have that structure (§7.4, `Loop.response_returns`). Which loops correct, toward what, and members that read one another's coupling directly are open (§8.2, “Selection, causes and experiment”).
- **How pools form, last and resist capture.** The laws say what a pool reaches, not how pools of intelligences form, how they are kept, or how they resist rules written by their winners (§4; §8.2, “Seeking, capture and runaway”).
- **What a rise draws from beyond.** Whether what it draws is depended on, and, with transmission, descent and openness, the rising share of what reaches beyond (§8.2, “Selection, causes and experiment”).
- **Strengths below one.** The doubling in law 8 needs natural-number strengths; real strengths, and how fast a runaway is, are open (§8.2, “Laws of exchange, coupling and loops”).
- **Correspondence.** Identifying the actual coupling closures, reach and loops of an AI system and of the institutions around it is empirical (§8.7).

The readings marked open in §7.2–§7.5 are the AI's.

## 8. Open programme

This section lists only what is open. What has landed is stated in sections 1–6, in the theory stated whole and in the records; it appears here only as the context an open item needs. Where a row names records, each record's `next_delta` is the authoritative list of its next steps. The items were checked on 17 October 2026 against the [work board](../../../_process/BECOMING-WORKBOARD.md), the status and `next_delta` of every record, and the git log through 1d18d005c.

Each open item belongs to one of four classes: an open reading, philosophical warrant, empirical correspondence, or a further proof. The adopted readings are in §8.3, each with its argument; their reasons and what would change them are in the [decision record](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/DELEGATED-CHOICES.md). The open readings are in §8.4. The formal statement being complete (see [the theory stated in full](#理论的完整陈述--the-theory-stated-in-full)) is not a proof of necessity.

Within their own scopes, finite consistency and the mutual constraints of loss through action and of the ways dependence changes are established. The record shows no progress entry since the early spine; the wording of the target is a goal scope, which changes only with Ren. |

<a id="83-readings-decided-on-rens-delegation"></a>

### 8.3 Readings adopted

Each item below is a reading: an interpretation of checked content, adopted by the AI with its argument. The arguments, and what would change each reading, are in the [decision record](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/DELEGATED-CHOICES.md), numbered D1–D43. Ren took D1, D5, D22 and D39, revised, and the readings of D43 on 18 October 2026 ([words](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-18-central-readings-taken.md)); his taking them is their provenance, not their warrant. The readings change no theorem and add no premise to any law, and a better argument revises them. Whether they stand as a whole is open (§8.4).

- 生生 is read as generation generating generation: what forms preserves, restores, transmits or creates the conditions and generators of further forming, so a course changes what can form, and the capacity to form, along with the state. The generative space is what can form now, and the direction of 生生 is compared by widening it. The three scopes of 7 October 2026 are scopes of what is counted. Living significance and C share this reading, and courses are compared on possibilities (D1, D22, revised on 18 October 2026).
- "Generative" and "degenerative" name a part's widening and narrowing of what can form. Keeping the same is neutral; each exceeding the other is mixed. At the level of formations they are renewing and consuming formations. The reading of activity is declined; arising and ceasing remain its names (D2).
- The second 生 is what can form, active or not. Arising that depends on depletion is classed through the comparison. Embodied relata are 生, and generation is not restricted to embodiment (D3, D4).
- Succession is read through the formed relations of continuation. 生生不息 is read at the level of the whole: 不息 as arising that recurs through renewal, and 生生不息 as 生生 going on with more still able to form. In a bounded whole whose rest is idle 不息 is return, which falls short of 生生不息; under the first law, more without end needs inflow from beyond every bounded part, not growth (`Flow.new_beyond_every`). Return is forced by natural-number amounts, a commitment of the representation (D5, revised on 18 October 2026; `Becoming.Gain`).
- Drawing at an ending, and undermining, are verdicts of the stepwise reading, not valuations of the drawer; comparison is on possibilities (D6, D10).
- "Openness" names exchange beyond every finite family. Inflow is gain, stated separately (D7; `Becoming.Gain`).
- Where a held condition is represented, valuation uses the possibility reading. Living on, descent and conveyance are formed relations of continuation, not direct valuations. No order is added to rank bringing new lives into existence (D8, D9, D12).
- The restatement from what wants its continuation: self-significance is the reading of wanting. The condition ranges over anything, as Ren's words say, and self-significance with standpoint neutrality is adopted. The derived extent runs backward along dependence and forward along reach. "Not harm" reads as retaining, and its boundary is the margin, Ren's phase transition. Harm is read at the whole, a weaker version within the system one is in, then at the self (Ren's answer of 10 October 2026). Formations are classed as lineages, while a member's ending stays a loss. "More" reads as a direction, widening (D11–D15).
- `Unrestricted` reads the continuation condition, and A is read on living content. Route (b) falls short of the scope of living significance, and both are kept. The feeding closure reads "what it actually depends on", and the reach closure reads "the system that one is in". The whole realizing itself reads as a represented boundary that is the whole. "Situational awareness" reads as a center knowing what its part reaches beyond its feedback (D16–D20).
- Where the law reads finitely many conditions under each history, the registration through which the rest responds, in the premise of `Becoming.Response`, is read formally as the law's sensitivity, the least registration it reads through; registration as 识 is Ren's own reading. What depends on the whole and on no part of it reads as a tail, in the exact sense of tail events, without probability (D37).
- The coupling strength is read formally as how strongly a formation registers, and how much difference arrives as a course that one law generates. In Ren's sentence, “heavily coupled into it” reads as coupling strength, and “a highly entangled system” as couplings that lie on loops through the whole, how highly being the strengths along the loops and how often they act; this is not quantum entanglement. A held difference keeps arriving under laws that create amounts, and under the first law this is not shown (D39, revised on 18 October 2026).
- The presupposition reads Ren's "belief". The admissible drawings of a self include the whole. The order of levels reads as three readings of the presupposition (D21).
- These are adopted with each record's scope (D23–D35): one relational starting point; where a move is formed (six readings with their qualifiers, the declined readings listed in the record); cause and effect; learning and wisdom; the relation-first re-founding (curvature only as an analogy of method); entropy (the record in the conserved amount); exchanges and steps; transmission and binding (the map of premises unchanged); histories and branching; what a description keeps and loses (`Becoming.Gluing` gives the exact law for parity constraints); and identification. "Situationally" is not read as the bound: reality is always situational (Ren, 15 October 2026), and the bound is only the condition under which a situation's finite prefix fixes the whole course through the map (D34).
- Names: the AI's working names are adopted where the statement has their exact meaning, and 生起／止息 is adopted as the Chinese for arising and ceasing (D36).
- What an extrapolation given nothing fixes, the boundary of enough under undetermined laws and when the runs of a process settle are read with exact scope: in worlds a list holds a prefix fixes the future through the map, and a reading is a function, not a model held; enough is a relation of the process with the target, and failing to rest because runs keep changing is not 无明; not changing for ever is not settling (D40-D42, on Ren's words of 18 October 2026).
- Taken by Ren on 18 October 2026 with the decision brief's recommendations (D43): the step index is a reading of the relations, not their ground (R1); a count is a relation (R2); only a loop makes a difference run away, and every loop that couples at every step does so only with natural-number strengths, since a damped loop need not (P118, narrowed); a cut loses what longer routes carry, and a loop of recurring couplings need not run away (P120, P123); observing lies within the downstream (P125, weakened; that observation is no second relation beside dependence is a conjecture); the tail's strengths, for a whole of this kind and a related whole (P126); going on by coming round, and dependent choice in general (P127); the run is the formal root of the package, not a ground beneath the world, since its index of steps is a reading of the relata's own courses (P130, revised after R1); the counterfactual is the course the same law generates (P130); the joins' readings (U, U2); lifting where branching is finite (I4b-4); the whole first, as an order of how complete a judgment is. The likeness of `Novelty`'s quantification to `BoundaryInvariant` is an analogy only.

**Identification and situations.** The readings: the core states what every situation obeys, each theorem holding for every process, world and run and fixing no situation; the actual course is fixed by the situation, which at a parting fixes what the record and the law leave open; natural weights count situations, and which situation happens is not a count (`Becoming.Situations`; the determination of the law whose moves are situations holds by construction, and the content is the count, `Situations.sum_finer`, D43). They read Ren's words of 16 October 2026, “we are describing what everything obeys, but at every moment, the actual change depends on the situation … the situation makes it true”, and Ren agreed with them the same day (“yes, i take those readings”). Of the finite name, the second reading is taken (“take 2”, Ren, 16 October 2026): within a bound, kinds have finite names; 名可名 is an analogy only, not a claim about the text. It is not derived with no axiom that every situation is bounded; for every world, the core states what can be derived in it.

<a id="84-what-stays-rens"></a>

### 8.4 Open readings

The readings and choices below are open. Each is decided by argument, as the readings of §8.3 are; where the readings are the AI's, the records state them in full. Who said a word is provenance; it does not settle a reading.

**Ren's texts and terms.** OPENING's body is Ren's text, and Ren's terms keep Ren's wording: the Chinese term for coming to rest (归于静止 or 停) and the heading "the rest theorem" (静止定理); "closure bounds change"; 识 and 理 as Ren reads them. These change only with Ren; what they are read to mean is open to argument like any reading.

**Provenance.** Whether a reading is Ren's own, and whether Ren holds the step of invariance (§6.11), are questions of provenance, answered from Ren's words. Neither decides whether the step holds, which is a question of warrant (§8.5).

**The commitments of the representation.** Discrete steps, natural-number amounts, the census where infinitely many stand, and decided equality and listing are commitments of the representation (class b; Ren reserved them on 11 October 2026: "(b) belongs to the representation, which needs my decision"). Whether each should stay a commitment, be refined or be derived is open, and is decided by what the representation must express (§8.5, the scope of representation) and by the theorems at finer grains (`Becoming.Units`, `Becoming.Resolution`).

**Readings not yet adopted** [AI reading, open].
- [where-a-level-couples](../results/records/where-a-level-couples.md): `obs` is not a second kind of relation beside the couplings and readings; it marks where a level can couple into a formation by level, as dependence marks where it can couple by threshold; registration bounds coupling, and a law's responses are its couplings.
- Kept as a conjecture, not a reading ([observation-is-a-route](../results/records/observation-is-a-route.md)): observation is no second relation beside dependence. `apart_only_downstream` shows only that observing lies within the downstream, the reading taken on 18 October 2026 (D43).
- §7.2–§7.5: 无明 read as the position of every formation in alignment; capability read as reach, the gap being reach less coupling; 明 read as a relation that widens as measurement, models and others are joined; holding 明 for others (measurement, models others can check, pools that include those an act reaches), with correction reaching the rule and each law's scope kept exact; alignment for all as pooled coupling across intelligences, whose acts respond to a difference only through members that couple it apart, with correction and escalation read as one loop through another's coupling and response; the conditional direction's opportunities to correct read as the routes of 明 ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-18-the-light-and-alignment-for-all.md)).

Ren took the other readings this list held on 18 October 2026, as D43, together with the revised D1, D5, D22 and D39 ([words](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-18-central-readings-taken.md)).

**The adopted readings as a whole.** Whether the readings of §8.3 stand as a whole (item 50 of the eighteenth review's fix list, finding D-f). Ren's "agree with other readings" of 16 October is provenance; it does not settle them. Ren took D1, D5, D22, D39 and D43 by name on 18 October, and D40-D42 rest on his words of that day. The [decision brief](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/READINGS-PENDING-REN.md#c-the-register-itself-d-f-review-item-50) recommends no blanket ratification: a reading of one of Ren's phrases needs his word on that phrase, and a reading of a theorem needs only argument.

**The generativity chain** (item 51 of the eighteenth review's fix list). Ren took the proposal on 18 October 2026: `Selection`, `Observation` and `Parting` enter the learning and wisdom links, each with its exact scope (D43). The chain is Ren's curated argument and changes through its maintenance record, where the proposal has been applied: `Selection` entered the wisdom entry, and `Observation` and `Parting` the learning entry ([chain](../results/GENERATIVITY-CHAIN.md), [maintenance record](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/GENERATIVITY-CHAIN-INDEX-RECORD.md#learning-and-wisdom-links-18-october-2026)).

**Questions from the whole-repository review of 16–17 October.**
- Whether an independent review beyond the eighteenth, an AI review that built no Lean, is needed (§8.8).
- The join of `IdealWarrant` under a structural name.
- Who integrates, and who owns the open whole-deduction target (W-root).
- Whether to split the three senses of 生成: the whole (生成之论), formation, and the generative (生成的空间).
- Whether the chapter's sentence on living significance under the condition says all the Lean shows: immediately, living significance is the condition restricted to living claims, and the condition gives significance to every claim, not a positive valence.

The review's question whether 生生 may be glossed by the Lean predicate `living` is answered by D1's revision: it may not, since the `living` readings do not include further generation.

**Normative warrant** is not a choice of reading. It is listed under philosophical warrant (§8.5).

### 8.5 Philosophical warrant

- **The deductive necessity of Becoming as a whole, with no added substantive axiom.** This means establishing that the whole's determinations are internally related moments of one unfolding, not merely a collection of instances ([foundation §9](BECOMING-FOUNDATION.md#9-the-content-of-the-whole-deduction); [theory-of-becoming-foundation](../results/records/theory-of-becoming-foundation.md)).
- **A stronger universal practical warrant:** grounding reasons without an externally supplied end ([universal-practical-warrant](../results/records/universal-practical-warrant.md)).
- **The necessary relational foundation of scientific inquiry:** establishing the core's indispensability from independently stated achievements ([scientific-inquiry-foundation](../results/records/scientific-inquiry-foundation.md)).
- **The grounds of living significance, A and the neutralities.** Open: the philosophical ground of living significance, the externalist defence of A and the philosophical ground of the neutralities, none yet independently reviewed.
  - On held lives, the restatement concentrates what living significance adds beyond self-significance in standpoint neutrality (§6.7). On a connected whole where every standpoint holds a living claim, it can be concentrated in relational neutrality instead. Either ground is as open as that of living significance.
  - Living significance is a theorem under the continuation condition. Its ground is therefore that of the condition (§6.15), and is open likewise.
  - Route (b), without the condition, claims no warrant (§6.9).
- **Calling the generative space good.** After the reduction to possibilities, the content of calling it good is the condition's step of invariance (§6.11). That widening is better is a question of warrant, not a premise of any law (§6.13): C's no-loss extension is exactly widening by definition, so the definition carries no warrant.
- **The scope of representation.** Can every becoming be represented as the spine represents it, with discrete steps and natural-number amounts? The shared index of steps is not itself a further question: for exchanges acting locally it is read off the relata's own courses (`Becoming.Interleavings`), so what remains is whether every becoming has such a local law with finite courses. A shared index identifying conditions with relata is needed only where the readings glue into `World`; the readings in context always form one world (`Contextual.world`, study §125). Where a becoming cannot be so represented, the spine is to be extended (§8.2).

### 8.6 The logical commitment

The core is constructive. No principle of classical logic is assumed anywhere in it. Where a law needs one, the principle is the law's own hypothesis, or the law is proved equivalent to it. Each entry below names its declarations.

**Decidedness is local** (`Becoming.Decidedness`, Ren, 11 October 2026). That what is counted is decided is the own condition of each law that counts or names a step: a count exists exactly where what it counts is decided (`counts_iff_decided`). The census between counts, the never-stopping forms of recurrence in finite families and the rest theorems use no such condition. The [decidability inventory](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/DECIDABILITY-INVENTORY.md) lists every theorem still conditioned on it, with its reason. The counting laws (`World.enclosure_bounds_change`, `World.counting_under` and their family) take decidability of activity at every step and for every relatum. Over counted censuses (`Becoming.CensusCounts`) the laws under the premises, each selection, enclosure and finite worlds take none, and over counted censuses the laws where the premises bind take none either (`Becoming.CensusBinding`, §8.2).

**Excluded middle (EM)** is a hypothesis wherever it is used, never an axiom. Asked of every case, these laws are each exactly EM:
- The finite-world law, that arising recurs exactly where ceasing does, asked of every finite world without decidability (`World.law_decides`, `World.law_iff_em`). Where activity is not decided, the law is stated with EM as a hypothesis (`World.law_of_em`). Coextinction along a chain without decay is stated the same way (`Carries.chain_ends_of_em`).
- A fixed number of relata, counted up to indiscernibility, for every world (`World.numbers_indiscernible_iff_em`).
- Deciding likeness of finite frames (`alike_decided_iff_em`).
- Branching tests fixing likeness without decided steps (`alike_of_sameBranchingTests_iff_em`).
- Matching parts by joint tests without stability (`matchesParts_of_compact_iff_em`).
- Reading every recipe as one branching test (`Recipe.holds_after_iff_em`), and every program as one test (`diamond_correct_iff_em`).
- The robust comparison under every classification (`classification_robust_iff_iff_em`).
- Forming and holding making inheritance under every descent (`split_iff_em`).
- In the selection through a model of the options, that every undominated member has a property wherever each member lacking it is bettered (`undominated_has_iff_em`).

**Weak excluded middle.** The other selection result asks of every irreflexive, transitive relation that every nonempty listed menu have an undominated member. That is exactly weak excluded middle, ¬P ∨ ¬¬P for every P (`exists_undominated_iff_weakly`).

**Double-negation elimination (DNE).** Asked of every process, the origin law with the gate a proposition is exactly ¬¬Q → Q for every Q (`origin_of_propositions_iff`).

**The limited principle of omniscience (LPO).** Every Boolean sequence has a true term or is false throughout; EM gives it, and it has no constructive proof (`LPO`). Each of the following gives LPO:
- That every formation never supported, with a carried reserve and decided activity, has a step after which it is never active, even one active at most once (`Web.lpo_of_ending`).
- That every determination listed at each step, whose coming to hold does not recur, has a list holding every member that ever holds (`Listed.lpo_of_few`).
- Naming the step from which a finite world rests (`lpo_of_finite_ends`).
- In `Becoming.Lasting`, every history meeting a target within one step does not give reaching it: in the search witness, reaching within one step is LPO for the given sequence (`Lasting.Search.reaches_iff_lpo`).

So the core states the weaker forms: "not active at arbitrarily late steps" and "not unboundedly many ever holding". The rest theorems conclude only that coming to rest cannot be refuted, ¬¬∃ (`World.rests_unrefuted`, `World.comes_to_rest_unrefuted`, `World.rests_unrefuted_enclosed`).

Two further principles sit at or above LPO:
- Deciding of each level sequence whether it vanishes from some condition on gives LPO (`lpo_of_vanish_decided`). As a rule, the decision of vanishing is exactly LPO as a rule (`vanishDecision_iff_lpoRule`, `LPO.of_rule`).
- That every schedule of couplings settles is exactly `RecursOrStops` (`settled_iff_recursOrStops`): every row of a decided two-place Boolean family holds somewhere, or some row holds nowhere. It gives LPO (`RecursOrStops.lpo`, `lpo_of_settled`). It is excluded middle at level Σ⁰₂ of the arithmetical hierarchy (Akama, Berardi, Hayashi and Kohlenbach 2004), strictly stronger than LPO.

**The weak limited principle of omniscience (WLPO).** Every Boolean sequence is false throughout or not; LPO gives it (`WLPO.of_lpo`), and it has no constructive proof (`WLPO`).
- With the conditions numbered by the natural numbers, take a law of what has stood that gives differently under two histories agreeing on everything it is sensitive to. It gives WLPO (`wlpo_of_not_through_sensitive`). So the core states the least coupling only for laws that read finitely many conditions under each history (`Becoming.Sensitivity`).
- A law of the whole and no part, insensitive to every condition yet differing between two histories, gives WLPO (`wlpo_of_insensitive_differs`).
- As a rule, a counterexample exists exactly where a rule decides, of every Boolean sequence, whether it is false throughout (`not_through_sensitive_iff_rule`, `changedByNoCondition_iff_rule`, `WLPO.of_rule`).

**Markov's principle.** A Boolean sequence that is not false throughout is true somewhere (`Markov`). Asked of every process, the reading of tests is refutable exactly under Markov's principle (`refutable_passes_iff_markov`).

**The lesser limited principle of omniscience (LLPO).** Of a Boolean sequence with at most one true term, the even places are all false or the odd places are; LPO gives it (`LLPO`, `LLPO.of_lpo`), and it has no constructive proof.
- `EitherThroughout` and `EitherNever` are each exactly LLPO for decided families (`eitherThroughout_iff_llpoForDecided`, `eitherNever_iff_llpoForDecided`, `LLPOForDecided`).
- Lifting where branching is finite takes `EitherThroughout` and a choice of an unbounded successor wherever one exists (`Lasting.lifting`).

**Choice.**
- Asked of every process, a run from every member of every family that keeps moving is exactly dependent choice (`runs_iff_selection`, `DependentSelection`). Within finitely many configurations a run is a course into a return, with no choice and no decision; where a test recognizes the moves it is built by search.
- Where the values a step keeps have choice into plans, a class is kept exactly where some plan keeps it (`keptFor_iff_plan_of_choice`). With listed values the same holds with no premise (`keptFor_iff_plan`).

**Periphery.** The periphery may use Lean's standard axioms (`propext`, `Quot.sound`, `Classical.choice`). Each periphery result says which it uses, through its own receipt (§9).

### 8.7 Empirical correspondence

- The actual realization of common conditions and of each mechanism: identification, and tests that discriminate between accounts.
- Whether actual unfoldings meet the premises: activity needing support, balance, only active formations giving and taking, dissipation, embodiment, reserve and transmission (`Flow.TransmitsWithin`). Dissipation as stated (at least one unit lost per uptake in a closed family) is stronger than the statistical second law, and `Passing` is a finite, enclosed, lossless world that never rests.
- How wisdom actually forms. Its formal part is complete in the laws without axiom ([wisdom-root-part-one](../results/records/wisdom-root-part-one.md)). Whether actual humans and AI form understanding and correction along these routes is a task of explanation and evidence, not a further proof.
- The classes actual learners and networks hold or can reach: which lumping an actual network's architecture, starting points and updates impose, and how it corresponds to the world ([generalization-through-lumping](../results/records/generalization-through-lumping.md)).
- For each conditional mechanism of §7: evidence for an identified installation.
- The stronger direction hypothesis, that reality generally develops more shared and more responsive renewal. It is open and not used as a premise.
