<a id="9-形式脊柱--the-formal-spine"></a>

## 9. The formal spine

All new formal results are stated on one spine. When the spine cannot express a result, it is extended rather than another model built. Existing generic ethical modules are neither copied nor rewritten: the spine gives instances of their interfaces. The [instance classification](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/BECOMING-INSTANCE-CLASSIFICATION.md) records the first such pass, on 9 October 2026, and is now a historical record; the current instances are the bridge and periphery rows of the module table. One package and one checker are not yet one web of dependence: the [theorem-connectivity measure](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/THEOREM-CONNECTIVITY.md#9-u2-the-periphery-measured-and-joined), rerun on each landing, finds 303 of the 9,225 spine theorems apart from both base families at the last rerun (§8.2, Integration of the spine).

**Three rings.** The rings are fixed by the checker's lists, and the [package README](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/README.md) describes them.
- **Core.** The three foundation modules `GenerativeCore`, `InquirySufficiency` and `RelationalRenewal`, with the package's base and four layers: 224 modules counting the root `Becoming`, and 227 in the core closure with the foundation.
- **Bridge.** Eight modules in the package but outside the core: `Becoming.Bridge`, `CompositionBridge`, `JoiningBridge`, `GranularityBridge`, `RepairComparison`, `Instances`, `RenewalBridge` and `CoursesBridge`. They prove that the definitions of existing modules are the core's. The core imports none of them. What reaches no periphery constant sits in the core, not the bridge: the lifting rule L (`Becoming.Lifting`), the seed–edge closure (`Becoming.EdgeClosure`), the support-gated renewal family as a balanced flow (`Becoming.RenewalFlow`) and classified beliefs after observation (`Becoming.ClassifiedBeliefProcesses`) ([fold study](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-BRIDGE-FOLD.md)). The constructed repair process of `RepairComparison` reaches none either, but stays in the bridge: its vocabulary is an application's.
- **Periphery.** Everything else: the existing ethics and dynamics modules, the Mathlib leaves and their bridges [CorrectionInquiry](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/universal-ethics/generative-capacity/CorrectionInquiry.lean) and [LearningCorrespondence](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/universal-ethics/evolution-connectionism/LearningCorrespondence.lean), the instance modules, and the Python experiments. The periphery may use any suitable tool, and its dependence runs towards the core. [`Becoming.FallibleInquiry`](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/FallibleInquiry.lean) sits in the package directory but is periphery, with its own checker and receipt.

**Four layers on one base.** `import Becoming` brings in the whole core.
- The base is `Becoming.World`: the records of the unfolding (web, flow, world, exchange) with no premise among them. It imports nothing, so the import tree starts there ([study §180](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#180-where-the-spines-import-tree-starts-and-a-plan-to-start-it-at-the-one-unfolding-w-step-1)). Balance and the other premises are gluings or local states, stated where they are used (§1, "One unfolding and its readings", and `Becoming.Readings`).
- L0 is the foundation.
- L1 is `Becoming.Frame`, with `Becoming.Labelled`: the labelled process and the frame as readings of the unfolding, and the run readings of renewal, dependence, return and generation.
- L2 holds inquiry, recurrence, the diagonal, context and the quantum square, together with assurance, segmentation, gating, viability, keeping, countdown, lasting and copying.
- L3 runs from `Becoming.Renewal` onward. From `Renewal` to `Space` it does not import the frame: its run readings are in `Becoming.Labelled` and `Becoming.LabelledRoles`.
- L4 runs from `Becoming.Metaethics` onward, with the frontier modules, `Becoming.Runs` and the modules after it among them.

The layers follow import order. They do not follow the split between the laws without axiom and the condition of §6.15: 49 modules that use no axiom reach `Significance` and `Metaethics` through their imports. Nor does every module import only lower layers: `Assurance` (L2) imports `Joint` (L3), and `Lasting` (L2) imports `Likeness` (L3).

**A pure core.** [Ren required](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-09-pure-core.md) that the core import nothing outside itself. The [consolidated checker](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/check_becoming.py) enforces the following on every run:
- The import closure is exactly the foundation and the core.
- Every source declaration in the core has an empty axiom report.
- The core compiles from Lean's prelude alone, with the foundation's `import Std` stripped.
- The constants Lean generates are audited too. The only ones that depend on an axiom are the injectivity lemmas of constructors with fields (`injEq`, on `propext`), and no source declaration uses them.
- The bridge constants have empty reports apart from such `injEq` lemmas.
- Every inherited module's current bytes match a passed receipt by sha256.
- No module of the superseded register (`SUPERSEDED`, since F3) is in the run; each keeps its bytes, recorded with the core modules stating it.

Living significance (`G0`), C, D and the positive ground of assurance are stated once in the core. The bridge proves that the existing modules' definitions are these.

**The current receipt.** The [current receipt](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/becoming-verification.json) is run `20261018T204907.920360Z`, frozen in [`becoming-checks/20261018T204907.920360Z/`](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/becoming-checks/20261018T204907.920360Z/verification.json). It was checked with Lean 4.29.1 on main fd4be59a0 (after F3R sub-chain 2, `Becoming.ActivationCapacity`) with F3Q, the inquiry and composition family (`Becoming.PartialReadout`, `Derivation`, `CodeAcquisition`, `RouteFormation`, `ResponseLearning` and `LearnedGrouping`, [verdicts](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/development/bridge-fold/port-q.md)) in the working tree, and it passed. Its counts:

| What | Count |
|---|---:|
| Core modules (with the root `Becoming`, 0 theorems) | 224 modules, 8,689 theorems |
| Foundation modules | 3 modules, 74 theorems |
| Core closure | 227 modules, 8,763 theorems |
| Bridges (the eight bridge modules) | 8 modules, 261 theorems |
| Pure periphery joins | 28 modules, 275 theorems |
| Spine (core and bridge) | 9,225 theorems |
| Audited spine declarations | 12,519 |
| Periphery-join modules (`…Inherited`) | 26 modules, 240 theorems, inheriting only `propext`, `Quot.sound` and `Classical.choice` |
| Inherited modules bound to passed receipts by hash | 77 (the 3 foundation modules and 74 earlier modules) |
| Superseded periphery modules (out of the run, bytes kept) | 1 (`ExplanatoryResiduals`) |
| Modules in the compile order | 363 |
| Generated `injEq` lemmas on `propext` | 124 in the core closure, 5 on the bridge side (`Becoming.RepairComparison.State.mk.injEq`, and four of the join modules' moves and records in `InstallationTraces`, `ReconfigurationProcesses` and `TreeSearchRuns`) |

Further facts about the receipt:
- The source was unchanged during the check, and the core compiled from the prelude.
- Unnamed instances in nine modules (Quantum, Change, Population, Resolution, Counting, Identity, Entropy, Gluing and SelectionReach) are not audited by name. The audit of the whole core environment covers them.
- One declaration name carries a market word: `Flow.Balances.rise_paid_from_beyond` (paid).
- That run reused 1,152 cached Lean steps from `~/.cache/becoming-lean` and ran 91. The last fresh run, [`20261016T130738.412710Z`](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/becoming-checks/20261016T130738.412710Z/verification.json), on the landing merge d21b311a before U, ran all 703 steps of that core with none reused and passed with that core's counts ([I5 report](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/development/i5-checker-reproducible/REPORT.md)).

**The periphery's own checks.**
- [CorrectionInquiry](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/universal-ethics/generative-capacity/CorrectionInquiry.lean) connects the Mathlib leaves to the core. It is checked with Lean 4.30.0-rc2 and Mathlib (receipt `20261015T164111.081887Z`). The core compiles there too, and the bridge's 7 theorems have empty axiom reports.
- [MachineInstances](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/universal-ethics/generative-capacity/MachineInstances.lean) connects older peripheral machines, whose definitions use standard axioms, as instances of the general laws. It is checked by its own checker: 96 theorems, standard axioms only.
- Seventeen further instance modules connect in the same way, each checked by its own checker with standard axioms only. They cover the copy machines, source histories, markets, the correction and shutdown protocols, versioned tasks, the shared stock, continuation plans, the transmission of learned procedures, material preparation and fragmentation, judging a risk certificate, renewing and consuming formation, binding-preserving substitution, organizational formation, the origin of an organization's pools, the prefix order of infinite traces, finite-branching lifting, and selection through a model of the options.
- `LearningCorrespondence` (15 theorems) is checked likewise.
- `FallibleInquiry` has its own receipt, `fallible-inquiry-verification.json` (run `20261016T120403.241963Z`): 31 theorems and 37 declarations, of which 17 depend on `propext` or `Quot.sound`.

**Names.** Witnesses and the declarations named after them follow the naming criteria of 10 October 2026: structural, formal and systems words ([Ren's words](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-zero-axiom-part-and-names.md)). A further batch was renamed under the same criteria on 14 October 2026: for example `IdentifiesAlike`, `Negation`, `Life` and `Loop.paired`; a reading that "credited" an event now "includes" it; and `netGive` and `fragile_b3_better_than_none`. Both old-to-new tables are in [plan §10](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-UNIFICATION-PLAN.md#10-第一部分与第二部分--part-i-and-part-ii). Historical records and older receipts keep the old names and are read through the tables. The [unification plan](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-UNIFICATION-PLAN.md) gives the direction and phases.

**The module table.** Every module of the core, the bridge and the periphery, with its layer, what it states and its theorem count, is in the [module table](THE-THEORY-OF-BECOMING-REFERENCE.md#module-table).

<a id="complete-episode-comparison-anchors"></a>

**Complete-episode comparison.** The frozen receipt [`20261013T204910.042715Z`](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/becoming-checks/20261013T204910.042715Z/verification.json) checks the 36 theorems and 65 source declarations of `Becoming.RepairComparison`, with empty axiom reports.
- The original 17 theorems, first checked in `20261013T122128.341270Z`, were only renamed. The 19 new ones read the repair process as an instance of the general laws of [Becoming.LossMatching](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/LossMatching.lean).
- The normative sufficiency of complete-episode matching remains an explicit hypothesis. Actual content correspondence remains to be established.
- The module reuses the shared `Frame`/`Run` and the existing loss-comparison interface and does not enter the core's imports. The current receipt lists it among the bridge modules, though the earlier table called it a peripheral application.

See the [complete argument and counter-review](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/CROSSED-CLAIMS-ARGUMENT.md#complete-episode-comparison-anchors).

<a id="10-术语表--glossary"></a>

## 10. Glossary

Each term keeps its Chinese name beside the English. Where a reading is marked as the AI's, it is an adopted reading, recorded with its argument in the [decision record](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/DELEGATED-CHOICES.md), unless the row says it is open. Labels retired from earlier texts are mapped under “Retired labels” below the table. The Lean column names the declarations that state the term; a dash means the term has no single declaration.

| Term (Chinese name) | English meaning | Lean anchor |
|---|---|---|
| Becoming (生成／变化) | The whole unfolding made up of the forming, holding, transforming, degrading and dissolving of relations. | — |
| 生生 (generativity) | Ren's term: the arising within the whole and its going on generating further, generation generating generation: what forms preserves, restores, transmits or creates the conditions and generators of further forming (D1, taken by Ren on 18 October 2026); the object of the ethical direction. Its direction is compared by widening what can form now, the generative space. It is not the Lean predicate `living`, whose readings do not include further generation. | none; `living` is narrower; the direction: `Web.Widens` |
| process (过程) | States the admissible changes. The one unfolding is read by splitting configurations into what the law fixes and what it leaves open (the moves), and a move can be read into the configuration (`Becoming.Unfolding`). | `Generative.Process`, `withMove` |
| frame (框架) | A process together with the claims each configuration realizes; read along a run as a world whose relata are the claims (`Becoming.Unfolding`). | `Frame`, `Frame.world` |
| run (运行) | An infinite actual execution. | `Run` |
| keeps moving, of a family (保持运动) | From every member some step leads to a member. Within finitely many configurations a run starts from every member and stays in the family, with no decision and no selection. | `KeepsMoving`, `KeepsMoving.run` |
| dependent choice (依赖选择) | A total relation has, from every starting point, a sequence along it. Asking of every process for a run from every member of every family that keeps moving is exactly this. | `DependentSelection`, `runs_iff_selection` |
| reach (可达) | A finite admissible path. | `Reach` |
| invariant (不变量) | A property kept at every step. | `Invariant` |
| absorbing region (吸收区域) | A region a law never leaves. For a process, a region absorbing under every move is an invariant. | `Law.Absorbing`, `Law.absorbing_iff_invariant`, `AbsorbingUnder`, `invariant_iff_every_move` |
| drawn into, basin (被吸入、吸引域) | A configuration outside an absorbing region whose orbit enters it is drawn into it; those drawn into an absorbing region form its basin. Every determined law draws in exactly the configurations that do not return. | `Law.DrawnInto`, `Law.Drawn`, `Law.drawn_iff_not_returns` |
| attractor (吸引子) | An absorbing region that configurations from outside enter. Within finitely many configurations the region of returning configurations draws in everything else. | `Law.returning_absorbing`, `Law.drawnInto_returning` |
| lock-in (锁定) | What is drawn in never comes back. After entering an invariant region, what lies outside is no longer reachable; leaving needs a move that does not keep the region. | `Law.DrawnInto.never_back`, `Invariant.locks_out`, `Run.locked_in`, `Run.leaving_needs` |
| novel realization (新实现) | A claim not realized before, under some reading. | `Novel` |
| scope (所见范围) | The configurations a policy's actual executions reach. | `Scope` |
| judgment process (判断过程) | Takes one admissible response at the configuration judged and then ends; its common response is the robust judgment. | `judgeProcess` |
| conditions (条件) | The observables of condition levels, activity, dependence and drawing. | `Conditions` |
| renewing / consuming (更新／消耗) | The relational classification of a formation with respect to the conditions it takes part in. | `RenewingIn`, `ConsumingIn` |
| web of dependence (依存网) | The unfolding in which dependences, required levels and drawing change step by step. | `Web` |
| kept / broken (维持／断裂) | Across a step, a satisfied dependence is still satisfied / no longer satisfied. | `Kept`, `Broken` |
| comes to be / ceases to be (开始成立／不再成立) | A specification goes, across a step, from not holding to holding / from holding to not holding. Arising, forming and coming to stand, and ceasing, dissolving and ceasing to stand, are this one change read on activity, dependence and level respectively. | `ComesToBe`, `CeasesToBe`, `Web.change_readings` |
| forms / dissolves, of a dependence (形成／消解) | A dependence appears / disappears across a step. | `Forms`, `Dissolves` |
| renewal at a step (步内更新) | The step of the event keeps every dependence on the conditions it takes part in; the stepwise classification of renewal. | `RenewingAt` |
| breaking, of an event (断裂) | Drawing on a condition at a step at which some dependence on it breaks. | `BreakingOn`, `BreakingAt` |
| arising (生起) | A formation goes from inactive to active across some step. | `Arises` |
| ceasing (止息) | A formation goes from active to inactive across some step. In a finite family, arising recurs exactly where ceasing recurs. | `Web.Ceases`, `Web.arising_recurs_iff` |
| keeping pace (同步) | In a finite family, over any stretch of steps the numbers of comings-to-be and ceasings-to-be differ by at most the number of members; what holds at the end together with what ceased in between is exactly what held at the start together with what came to be in between. | `keep_pace`, `held_over_stretch` |
| unceasing arising (不息) | Some formation arises at arbitrarily late steps; the same formation may arise again. Earlier English: "unceasing generation". This weak predicate is not Ren's 生生不息 (see the crosswalk below the table). | `Unceasing` |
| depletion, of a finite family (消耗) | At the event's steps at which no condition of the family rises, some condition in it falls; it says only that a condition in the family changes. In a balanced flow it holds where the family is dissipated at the event's step and some formation takes from it. | `Depletes`, `Balances.depletes` |
| dissipation (耗散) | On a web of dependence: an amount that never rises while every arising lowers it. On a flow: every formation taking part gives a closed family no more than it takes from it, and less when it takes. It constrains only steps at which a finite family is closed, and there giving must be conditional on taking. | `dissipation_generation_not_recurring`, `Dissipative`, `giving_needs_uptake` |
| one exchange (同一交换) | At a dissipated step, one member's rise with another member's fall. No particular fall is attributed to a particular rise, and a fall is not a ceasing. | `rise_with_fall`, `rise_exchange` |
| closed (封闭) | At a step no formation taking part exchanges with a condition outside the family. | `ClosedAt` |
| enclosed step (封闭的步) | Across the step some finite family without repetition is closed; where the conditions are finitely many in all, every step is. | `Enclosed` |
| balance at a step (一步的平衡) | A flow balances on a family across a step: each member's level after the step together with what was taken equals its level before together with what was given. Balance is balance at every step on every family. | `Flow.BalancesOn`, `Flow.balances_iff_on` |
| imbalance relatum (不平衡关系项) | Where a flow does not balance, the difference is counted as the exchange of one more relatum that is never active and requires nothing. Every flow then balances, a flow balances on a family across a step exactly where that relatum is idle there, and the two representations are equivalent step by step. Adopted on 14 October 2026, with Ren's agreement (`Becoming.Imbalance`, `Becoming.Reading`). | `Flow.withImbalance`, `Flow.balancesOn_iff_idle`, `Flow.withImbalance_acting_taking_on_iff` |
| rest of the whole (整体的其余) | The imbalance counted as the relatum `none`: it gives each condition the part by which its level rises beyond the flow's net exchange, takes the part by which it falls beyond it, and never requires anything. Counting it, every flow balances, and balance is exactly its being idle. Its exchange is read off the difference between the levels and the formations' exchanges; it is not observed. Adopted on 14 October 2026, with Ren's agreement (`Becoming.Reading`). | `Flow.withImbalance`, `World.withRest`, `World.withRest_closed_iff` |
| running down (下降) | Across a step a family contains, without repetition, every condition standing before or after the step, and the flow balances and dissipates on it (closure is not needed). Then a level changes exactly where the family's total level falls, and change recurs exactly where it recurs at steps that do not fall. | `Flow.RunsDownOn`, `Flow.RunsDown`, `Flow.change_recurs_iff` |
| laws of one step (一步的规律) | Laws holding for every world, with the conditions their proofs use written into the law. Where a premise holds at every step, the condition holds everywhere: the results of a selection are corollaries and derive no premise. | `Becoming.Locality` |
| enclosed around (围住) | Across the step some finite family without repetition containing the given family is closed. Where equality is decidable, every enclosed step encloses every finite family; without deciding equality, it is not the case that it fails to enclose one. | `Around`, `Enclosed.around`, `Enclosed.around_not_not` |
| drawing in (汲入) | The family cannot be inward from any step on: it is not the case that from some step on no formation gives to the family while taking from outside it. Under balance and dissipation, an enclosed finite family without repetition in which taking recurs, or whose embodied members keep arising, draws in in this sense. | `Inward`, `taking_draws_in`, `generation_draws_in` |
| conveying (输送) | Where a formation taking part takes from one condition and gives to another, something is conveyed from the first to the second. Read with one kind of relata, it is one reading of `related`, adopted as a formed relation ([D12](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/DELEGATED-CHOICES.md)). | `Conveys`, `conveyance` |
| fed from beyond (从其外得到供给) | Something is conveyed into some member from outside the family. With equality decidable, this is exactly the step not being inward. | `FedFromBeyond`, `inward_iff_not_fed` |
| joint excess (联合超出) | The participants together take more from a condition than the step gives, even though each is within what is given. | `WithinOwn`, `WithinJoint`, `excess_bounded` |
| cut (切口) | No formed relation leaves a region. Under conveying, the region of a continuing family is not a cut. | `Cut`, `region_not_a_cut` |
| fed by (供给延续) | At the same step, one event's formation gives to a condition that another event's formation needs. | `FedBy` |
| flow (流) | The amounts that the formations taking part take from and give to conditions at each step. Where it balances, the change of level is exactly the difference of the two. | `Flow`, `Balances` |
| uptake / replenishment (取用／补充) | The total that the formations taking part take from / give to the family at a step; the two balance with the change of level. | `uptake`, `replenishment` |
| takers / renewers (取用者／补充者) | The formations that take from / give to the family at a step. | `takers`, `renewers` |
| arising taking (取用而生起) | A formation takes from the family at its first active step. | `ArisesTaking` |
| census (普查) | The formations active at each step listed in a finite list without repetition: the listing of activity. Under a census, ceasing recurring needs arising recurring, not conversely; where the census returns within some number at arbitrarily late steps the two keep pace, and where the count grows without bound, however slowly, there can be arising with no ceasing (`slow`). Over any stretch, the list at the end together with what ceased in between is exactly the list at the start together with what came to be in between (the census balance). The same law read on levels (the census of what stands), on dependence (the census of dependence), and for any specification whose holders are listed at each step. | `Census`, `Listed`, `arising_of_ceasing`, `unceasing_iff_bounded`, `Listed.over_stretch`, `Listed.ceasing_iff_shortfall`, `World.CountedBy`, `World.slow_world` |
| covers (覆盖) | A list contains every member that holds at a step. Wherever there is a covering list at arbitrarily late steps, ceasing-to-be recurring needs coming-to-be recurring; a census is the case covering every step. | `Covers`, `coming_of_ceasing_covered` |
| decided (已定) | A proposition holds or does not hold; a decision procedure gives this as data. Decidability is not a commitment of the representation: a law that counts or names a step writes being decided where it counts as its own condition (`Becoming.Decidedness`). | `Decided`, `not_not_decided` |
| count (计数) | A number counts the members of a list at which a specification holds: it is the sum of their indicators. A count exists exactly where what is counted is decided, and it is unique; the marks of a decision procedure are the count. The census between counts needs no decidability (`Becoming.Decidedness`). | `Indicates`, `Counts`, `CountsAcross`, `counts_iff_decided`, `Counts.over_stretch` |
| tally (计数表) | The number listed at each step, with the numbers that cease and come to be across steps: across each step some members are kept, which with those that cease are the list before and with those that come to be are the list after. It is exactly balance together with no more ceasing across a step than were listed before it. Every listing gives a tally, and every tally is realized by a queue (joining at the back, leaving at the front), so the tally is the whole law of counting. Without balance or without embodiment the premises do not constrain the count; the two together with only active formations giving do. Under all eight premises the laws of counting are exactly the tally and no arising at a step at which no relatum is active, with some arising added where every premise binds. Under the 256 selections of premises there are only these two laws, and at most three more where every selected premise binds, in finite worlds and where enclosed from some step on as well. Read on what stands, the laws are the same where not enclosed, and where enclosed embodiment is not needed only where balance and dissipation bind. Read on dependence, the premises add no law. | `Tally`, `Listed.keeps`, `tally_iff`, `Queue.counts`, `counting_laws`, `tally_iff_counted`, `World.counting_free`, `World.counting_binds_jointly`, `World.premised_counting`, `World.counting_under`, `World.counting_binds_iff`, `World.binding_counting`, `World.binding_counting_under`, `World.finite_binding_counting_under`, `World.enclosed_binding_counting_under`, `World.standing_counting_under`, `World.dependence_counting` |
| change bound (变化界) | From some step on, the number listed at each step together with the steps since then at which something ceased or came to be is at most a number. Within a change bound change does not recur. Where enclosed at every step from some step on, the count of activity is within the total level of what stands when enclosed. Under the eight premises the laws of counting, where enclosed and in finite worlds, are exactly the tally, no arising at a step at which no relatum is active, and the change bound from step 0. Under every selection of premises the change bound joins exactly where balance, embodiment and dissipation are selected, both where enclosed and in finite worlds; the count of what stands joins the change bound exactly where balance and dissipation are selected, with no embodiment needed. | `ChangeBound`, `occurrences`, `World.enclosure_bounds_change`, `World.enclosed_counting`, `World.finite_counting`, `World.enclosed_counting_under`, `World.finite_change_bound_iff`, `World.enclosure_bounds_standing`, `World.enclosed_standing_bound_iff`, `World.finite_standing_bound_iff` |
| taking (取用即汲取) | Only active formations take, and only from conditions they draw on. | `Taking` |
| more without end (不止地更多) | For every number, from some step on at least that many formations take from the finite family of conditions. | `More` |
| re-identification / renaming (改认／改名) | A one-to-one correspondence between two namings at each step that leaves each step's relations unchanged; a re-identification that is the same at every step is a renaming. | `Reidentify`, `Reidentify.rename`, `Web.reidentify` |
| continuity (连续) | A formation that stays active, or stays dependent on some condition, across a step keeps its name. With the old naming as reference it is one-way; with the new naming as reference as well, it is two-way. A reading of identity, not a definition of it. | `KeepsActive`, `KeepsTenure`, `Reidentify.back` |
| succession (接续) | Joining a formation that ends at some step with one that begins at or after that step as one and the same (with steps between that are inactive and dependent on nothing, for example dormancy). One-way continuity does not exclude it, and two-way continuity does not exclude it across dormancy. Read through the formed relation of continuation (an AI reading, [D5](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/DELEGATED-CHOICES.md)). | `Lineage`, `Succession`, `Dormancy` (witness) |
| continuation (延续关系) | Which formation before a step a formation after it continues. A split is one formation continuing as several, a merge several continuing as one; a re-identification is a one-to-one continuation. Under continuation a formation ceases if no active formation after the step continues it. Without splits, ceasing recurring needs arising recurring; without merges and with a bounded count, conversely. | `Web.Continues`, `ArisesFrom`, `NoSplit`, `CeasesFrom`, `NoMerge`, `Census.balanceFrom` |
| whole / parts (整体／部分) | A whole is active exactly while some part is: a coincidence of activity. Where parts are brief, or parts end and the parts active at once are finitely many, the whole's lasting needs parts to keep arising. | `Whole`, `Covered`, `Brief`, `Ends`, `Listed` |
| roles (角色) | Formation and condition are two roles of one relatum. Readings of two kinds embed into a reading of one kind with no formation as a condition; which condition is which formation is given by a common index, a premise of the representation. | `Web.roles`, `Flow.roles`, `Web.relabel` |
| embodied (体现) | A relatum stands as a condition, at a positive level, exactly while it is active: a reading under the one correspondence. Its arising is its coming to stand, and so a replenishment. Embodiment itself enters neither valuation. | `Embodied`, `Embodied.comesToStand`, `Embodied.replenishes`, `Mutualism.apart_embodied` |
| coming to stand (开始作为条件而在) | A condition's level goes from zero to positive across a step: a rise from zero. In a balanced flow every rise is given, and coming to stand is given at least the new level, so it is a replenishment. A rise recurring under dissipation keeps a finite family from being closed from any step on, and an enclosed one draws in. | `Web.ComesToStand`, `ComesToStand.rises`, `Balances.rise_given`, `standing_given`, `rising_draws_in` |
| ceasing to stand (不再作为条件而在) | A condition's level goes from positive to zero across a step. In a finite family, coming to stand recurs exactly where this recurs, with no premise. | `Web.CeasesToStand`, `Web.standing_recurs_iff` |
| bearing on itself (承担自身) | A relatum, while active, depends with positive need on its own level. | `SelfBearing` |
| coextinction (共灭) | Under `Dependent`, a relatum inactive at some step ends, at that step, every formation that a chain of dependence (with positive need, through embodied relata) leads to it from. With a reserve, coextinction is postponed and not escaped; where the reserve decays it is bounded link by link along chains of dependence of any length, and where it does not decay, with excluded middle as hypothesis, it still ends link by link. | `coextinct`, `Carries.coextinct_lasts`, `Carries.chain_ends`, `Carries.chain_ends_of_em` |
| reserve (储备) | An amount that does not rise while the formation is unsupported and falls while it is active then. Zero reserve is exactly `Dependent`. It can be the level of a held condition, and is a given amount where held conditions are not represented. A formation unsupported from some step on is active at most as many times as its reserve at the start. | `Web.Carries`, `carries_zero`, `Flow.carries_of_held`, `Carries.lasts` |
| undermining (侵蚀) | Drawing on a condition at a step after which the condition is below both what some dependence now requires and what that dependence required at some step of its unbroken holding when it was satisfied. The stepwise reading of `living` is active and not undermining. | `MetAbove`, `UnderminingOn`, `UnderminingAt` |
| return (回程) | A dependence holding at some step is satisfied again at some later step (or within `p` steps). | `Returns`, `ReturnsWithin`, `RenewedWithin` |
| horizon (视界) | A finite prefix; two runs agree before it. | `Run.AgreeUpTo` |
| making a difference (差别制造) | With one side's part removed, the dependence is kept at every step with the same move (and that move is available). | `MakesDifference`, `Overdetermined` |
| interaction (交互) | One side draws on a condition on which the other depends at a positive level, at any step: `related` under the discipline of correspondence. | `Interacts`, `Web.interaction` |
| living on (以之为生) | In a reading of one kind, a formation at some step draws on a relatum and depends on it with positive need; under `Dependent` what it depends on stands as a condition at that step. A candidate reading of `related` besides interaction. | `Web.LivesOn`, `LivesOn.stands`, `Web.sustenance` |
| descent (世系) | In a reading of one kind, a relatum comes from a formation taking part at the step at which it comes to stand, if given by it. Relating every event of the two, it is another candidate reading of `related`. | `Flow.DescendsAt`, `Flow.Descends`, `Flow.descent` |
| transmission (传递) | Offspring inherit the parent's dependences with requirements at most δ more. Where it binds it is independent of the other premises; given the reading of taking and taking as dependence, it follows (`Becoming.Transmission`). In `price` it is each offspring's change relative to its parent. | `Flow.TransmitsWithin`, `Flow.transmitsWithin_iff`, `World.transmitting_of_drawn`, `raised`, `lowered` |
| selection (选择) | Where only the active give and activity needs support, only supported formations leave offspring. In `price` the total of the parents' amounts weighted by their numbers of offspring is not a genuine selection term: it is not zero even where every number of offspring is the same. Fitness is not defined. | `unsupported_childless`, `selected`, `selected_le`, `selected_uniform` |
| world (世界) | The relata of a reading of one kind, the flow between them and the web of dependence. The space is every world: laws hold of the whole, and premises mark out parts. | `World` |
| exchange (交换) | A world's move across a step: what the relata present take and give. The law of exchange says that each relatum's level after a step together with what it took equals its level before together with what it was given; a world balances exactly where it runs by that law. | `Exchange`, `exchange`, `World.move`, `World.balances_iff_exchange` |
| law shown (所显示的律) | The steps a history has taken. It is the least law of which the history is a run, determined exactly where the history is consistent. The correspondence is not an identity; it closes where history is kept. | `Shown`, `shown_le_iff`, `exists_law_iff`, `Run.keepHistory_shown` |
| alike / bisimulation (相同／互模拟) | Configurations related by a bisimulation realize the same claims and go by the same moves to configurations that are alike. An equivalence relation; configurations that are alike pass the same tests. | `Bisimulation`, `Alike`, `Alike.tests` |
| determined up to likeness (相同之下确定) | Any two outcomes of the same move are alike. It transfers along likeness; determinedness as equality depends on the naming. | `DeterminedAt`, `DeterminedAt.transfer`, `Twins.alike_one` |
| holds its splices / live steps (含有拼接／活的步) | A family of histories contains every sequence made of its own steps; a law's live steps are those some history of it takes. A family of histories is a law's family of histories exactly where it holds its splices, and two laws admit the same histories exactly where their live steps are the same. | `SpliceClosed`, `family_iff`, `live`, `same_histories_iff` |
| possibly / necessarily drawn (可能／必然被吸入) | Some path / every run from the configuration enters a region the process never leaves. Every process necessarily draws in exactly what does not return; what is possibly drawn in can return along another path. | `PossiblyDrawnInto`, `NecessarilyDrawnInto`, `necessarilyDrawn_iff_not_returns`, `possiblyDrawn_iff` |
| description that makes sense (有意义的描述) | A description that identifies exactly the configurations that are alike. It is unique in what it identifies, and carries a law where the frame is determined up to likeness. A description that separates nothing always carries a law, but makes sense only where everything is alike. | `IdentifiesAlike`, `identifiesAlike_iff`, `IdentifiesAlike.carries` |
| rectangular whole / separated description (矩形的整体／分离) | Whatever goes with one part goes with every item of the other part. A separated description says only what each part can be, and gives back the whole exactly where the whole is rectangular; where the whole is not rectangular, the parts are related. | `Rectangular`, `Separated`, `separation_faithful_iff` |
| one state, same ahead (同一状态) | Two moments of a history of values after which every value is the same. States are read off the history, not given as configurations; there are finitely many exactly where the history repeats from some moment on. | `SameAhead`, `alike_moments_iff`, `finitely_many_states_iff` |
| gap / completing unfolding (缺口／补全的展开) | For a family of readings indexed by what it reads, the reading obtained by moving each member's value at itself through a map, which no member says. A family that keeps what it said and says the gap next is incomplete at every step and never returns. | `Diagonal.gap`, `Diagonal.gap_unsaid`, `Diagonal.Unfolds` |
| claims in context / gluing (语境中的主张／粘合) | Claims are shown in contexts. What the contexts show glues where some context-free reading agrees with every context, exactly where the contexts a claim lies within show it alike. Context-free claims are a commitment of the representation. | `Contextual`, `Contextual.Glues`, `Contextual.glues_iff_agree`, `Contextual.fitsFree_iff` |
| parity system / contradiction (奇偶系统／矛盾) | Claims are listed, and each context is given the claims it counts and the parity it requires; an assignment meets a context where the values of the claims it counts have the required parity. A contradiction is a selection of contexts, repetition allowed, counting every claim an even number of times while the required parities add up to odd. With equality of claims decidable and the list without repetition, a context-free assignment meets every context exactly where no selection contradicts. | `ParitySystem`, `ParitySystem.Contradicts`, `ParitySystem.global_or_contradicts`, `ParitySystem.glues_iff`, `ParitySystem.mixture_le` |
| bound on states (状态数的界) | A history of values has at most N states: every moment is in the same state as one of the N moments before it; exactly where it is a lasso within N. | `StatesBelow`, `Identification.statesBelow_iff_lasso` |
| identification / extrapolation given the bound (辨识／给定界的外推) | A finite prefix fixes the whole course. The extrapolation given the bound N stays silent before 2N − 1, and from then on proposes by the lasso candidate within N that the prefix bears out; it misses nowhere. | `Identification.prefix_fixes`, `Identification.withBound` |
| runs part (运行分开) | Two runs take one value through the map before some step and different values at it. Where no two runs from a configuration part, they have one course through the map. | `Parting.PartAt`, `Parting.Unparted` |
| free branching (自由分支) | The process in which every configuration can be followed by every configuration; every course is the course of one of its runs. | `Parting.free`, `Parting.freeRun` |
| typical, by counting (典型) | A course whose squared deviation (q·a − p·b)² is less than d. The courses deviating by at least d weigh, times d, at most n·p·q·(p + q)ⁿ. | `Typicality.offBy`, `Typicality.typical` |
| extrapolation built from the process (依过程所建之外推) | Reads off the prefix the configurations the run may be at, takes the first from which it can go on without end, and proposes its value through the map. It is right at a step on every run exactly where no two runs part at that step. | `Parting.fromProcess`, `Parting.world_right_iff` |
| situation, of a step (情境) | One way of taking a step; with it as the move the law is determined. A step's natural-number weight is the number of situations that take it. | `Situations.finerLaw`, `Situations.finer`, `Situations.situations_of_course` |
| generative / degenerative (生成的／退化的) | Ren's terms. Ren wrote "generative和degenerate虽然都在一个becoming model上，但是他们不overlap吧" (that generative and degenerate, though both on one becoming model, do not overlap; the particle 吧 presupposes it). The AI reads them as naming a part's widening and narrowing of what can form, which at the level of formation are renewing and consuming formation and as relations do not overlap ([D2](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/DELEGATED-CHOICES.md)). On 10 October 2026 Ren wrote that the generative is what "its existence creates more possibilities of becoming - more future, not less", and confirmed reading possibility as what can form next. The AI formalized this as a part widening what can form next, the degenerative as narrowing it, with keeping the same and mixed besides (§2), with no axiom (§6.13). | `Web.RenewingIn`, `Web.ConsumingIn`, `Web.renewing_not_consuming`, `Web.Includes`, `Web.Widens`, `Restoring.Inflow.widens`, `Clearing.narrows` |
| widens / keeps the same / mixed (扩大／保持不变／混合) | One path of levels admits every formation the other admits and at some step admits one more / each includes the other / each exceeds the other. The three exclude one another pairwise; the generative space is what can form now, and where a part widens it is the region Ren's sentence of 11 October names (§6.13; D22, revised on 18 October 2026). | `Web.Widens`, `Web.Keeps`, `Web.Mixed` |
| arising recurs / ceasing recurs (生起复现／止息复现) | Some relatum arises / ceases at arbitrarily late steps. In a finite world with activity decided, the two are one law; asked of every finite world without decidability being given, that law is equivalent to excluded middle; with infinitely many relata the two come apart. They are readings of activity. The English names arising/ceasing were confirmed by Ren; the Chinese renderings are the AI's ([words](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-09-arising-ceasing-names.md)). They are not the "generative" and "degenerative" of Ren's proposal: those do not overlap, while these coincide in a finite world (an AI reading, [D2](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/DELEGATED-CHOICES.md)). | `World.ArisingRecurs`, `World.CeasingRecurs`, `World.arising_iff_ceasing`, `World.law_decides`, `World.law_iff_em` |
| coming to rest (归于静止) | From some step on, nothing arises or ceases (`World.RestsFrom`); finding that step needs an omniscience principle (`World.lpo_of_finite_ends`). The theorems prove two strengths, and their names follow. Where the name carries `unrefuted` (for example `World.rests_unrefuted`), what is proved is that it comes to rest cannot be refuted: ¬¬∃ N, rest from N. From that, neither arising nor ceasing recurs (`World.not_recurring_of_rests_unrefuted`). Where only non-recurrence is proved, the name carries `not_recurring`. When this chapter says a theorem "comes to rest", it means the strength the theorem proves. The Chinese term and the title 静止定理 (the rest theorem) are Ren's. The rest theorem: a world with censuses of activity and of what stands, whose arising relata are embodied, balanced, dissipative, and enclosed around what stands at every step from some step on, comes to rest in the unrefuted sense, with no decision procedure; the census is used only as a list covering the active (decidability localized). The world need not be finite, but what stands and what is active at each step must be finitely many; in a world that comes to rest so, what ever stood or was active is not unboundedly many, and neither census can be dropped. Finite worlds are a case. | `World.RestsFrom`, `World.comes_to_rest_unrefuted`, `World.arising_stops_unrefuted`, `World.rest_needs_standing`, `World.rests_unrefuted`, `World.not_recurring_of_rests_unrefuted`, `World.rest_few` |
| stopping unrefuted (停止不能被否证) | That something stops from some step on cannot be refuted; equivalently, it is not the case that after every step a later occurrence cannot be refuted (`stop_unrefuted_iff`). Finding the step needs an omniscience principle. The falls of an amount that does not rise are so, with no decision. Stronger than non-recurrence; the rest theorem goes through it with no decision procedure (decidability localized; formerly "settles"). | `stop_unrefuted_iff`, `falls_stop_unrefuted`, `amount_still_unrefuted`, `Web.generation_stops_unrefuted`, `Flow.change_stops_unrefuted`, `World.comes_to_rest_unrefuted_from` |
| openness (开放) | Not enclosed at every step from some step on: exchange goes beyond every finite family. So where a world with a census, all relata embodied, balanced and dissipative, has unceasing arising. In `exposed` the net amount is zero. The eight premises do not decide it, and it together with the eight premises does not give unceasing arising (`holding`). It names exchange outside every finite family; inflow is gain, stated separately (an AI reading, [D7](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/DELEGATED-CHOICES.md)). | `World.arising_opens`, `World.exposed_standing`, `World.openness_contingent`, `World.holding_world` |
| net giver / gain (净给予者／增益) | A relatum taking part across a step that gives the family more than it takes from it. Counting the rest, every rise of the family's stock has a net giver, a formation taking part or the rest. Under the first law, the gain of a family without repetition comes from the rest or the family is open there. Gain and openness are stated separately. | `Flow.NetGiver`, `Flow.rise_netGiver`, `Flow.rise_given`, `Flow.rise_from_rest_or_open` |
| law of transmission (传递律) | With no premise: the offspring's total plus what transmission lowered equals the parents' amounts weighted by their numbers of offspring plus what transmission raised. The additive counting form of the Price equation, an arithmetic identity, in which the weighted total is not a genuine selection term. | `price` |
| bearing (承担) | Depending at a positive level on a condition that can fail it. | `Bearing` |
| idle condition (惰性条件) | A condition no activity draws on, or on which no dependence can fail. | `Idle` |
| formation event (形成事件) | (formation, step): the claims of the whole-run and stepwise readings. | `F × Nat` |
| relation, at the level of readings (关系) | (formation, condition, step): the claims of the relation-level readings. | `Web.relations` |
| valuation (评价) | Living, relation, region, significance. | `Valuation` |
| own region (自身区域) | The region of formed relations; not an owner. | `own` |
| significance (意义) | Positive significance from a standpoint. Under the continuation condition it is read as the significance of continuation, not necessarily positive. Positive relevance is read from significance: it is the significance of realized living content (§6.12). | `sig` |
| seed (种子) | Some living claim has significance from some standpoint. | `Seed` |
| living significance | Realized living content is positively relevant; at the level of claims, every living claim has significance from every standpoint. Not an axiom: the theory states it as a condition and characterizes its content with no axiom, and the dynamics do not decide it; with the continuation condition as hypothesis it is a theorem (§6.15). | `G0`, `Valuation.Significant`, `Web.significance_undetermined` |
| self-significance | A standpoint's own living continuation has significance for it; read as wanting one's continuation (§6.7). | `Valuation.SelfGood` |
| relational neutrality / standpoint neutrality | Significance passes along every formed relation to living content / what has significance from one standpoint has it from every standpoint. The two boundary neutralities of living significance (§6.1). | `Valuation.transmits` / `Valuation.shares` |
| held (持有) | A living claim lies in some standpoint's own region. Where every living claim is held, living significance is exactly self-significance everywhere together with standpoint neutrality (an AI reading of "what wants its continuation" in Ren's proposal of 10 October 2026). | `Held`, `Unheld`, `significant_iff_held` |
| center (中心) | Whose continuation counts. Every point of wanting is a center (self-significance). The dynamics fix, along dependence, the extent that what wants its continuation must count; they do not fix the center (`center_not_derived`). The AI reads Ren's proposal as standpoint neutrality making what has significance from any center have significance from every standpoint, with no center special. On Ren's later answer, centers are formations in the dynamics, and the condition is read as unrestricted significance (§6.8). Without the condition, what a center actually depends on is its feeding closure (§6.9); a center's own feedback carries at most its influence closure, and its exchanges reach its reach closure (§6.10); a center's presupposition along a relation is drawn at its closure (§6.11). | `SelfGood` (self-significance), `shares` (standpoint neutrality) |
| admission (接纳) | The levels meet every requirement a formation has at that moment; support is admission by the standing levels. | `Web.Admits`, `Web.supported_iff_admits` |
| a part withheld (扣留一个部分) | The part (a formation or a system) neither takes nor gives, and the rest of the exchange goes on as before. The levels after withholding are unique where they exist, and the difference is exact. | `Flow.withhold`, `Flow.WithheldLevels`, `Flow.Balances.withheld` |
| margin (余量) | How far the levels after withholding exceed what is required. A formation is supported exactly where the part's accumulated net drawing exceeds the margin on none of the conditions it requires: the exact location of the phase boundary. | `Flow.Balances.supported_iff_within_margin` |
| includes / exceeds (包含／超出) | One path of levels admits at every step every formation the other admits / at some step admits a formation the other does not. Inclusion is a preorder. | `Web.Includes`, `Web.Exceeds` |
| widens / narrows / mixed (扩大／缩小／混合) | The levels with the part include and exceed those without it / those without include and exceed those with it / each exceeds the other. Inclusion does not order the mixed; ordering them needs a measure. | `Restoring.Inflow.widens`, `Clearing.narrows`, `Transfer.mixed` |
| within / beyond a region (区域之内／之外) | Within a region of formations, these levels admit every formation those levels admit / beyond the region some formation is admitted by those levels and not by these. The smaller the region, the more lies beyond it; nothing lies beyond the whole. | `Web.AdmitsWithin`, `Web.LosesBeyond`, `Web.not_losesBeyond_whole` |
| continuation condition; unrestricted significance (不受限的意义) | For anything, its continuation into the future has significance. §6.15 takes it as a condition, and the theory does not assert it (an AI reading of Ren's proposed words): from every standpoint, the continuation of anything at every step has significance; the significance of continuation is boundary-neutral. | `Unrestricted` |
| retains / adds (保留／增加) | From a standpoint, one path of levels admits every significant continuation the other admits / admits a significant continuation the other does not. Under the condition these are inclusion and exceeding. | `Web.Retains`, `Web.Adds` |
| preemption (先占) | Taking a condition away before any dependence on it forms: not undermining on the stepwise reading, yet closing off formations that could form later. Under the condition it loses significant continuations. | `Preemption.not_undermining_yet_loses` |
| feeding closure (供给闭包) | The least region containing a formation and closed under feeding (whatever feeds a member is a member). It follows with no premise; what lies outside the closure gives nothing to the conditions the closure requires. Read as "what it actually depends on" (an AI reading). For a closure that is fed, it lies within the influence closure. | `Flow.WithinFeeding`, `Flow.FedClosed`, `Flow.beyond_gives_nothing`, `Flow.withinFeeding_iff` |
| closure (闭包) | One formation lies within another's closure under a relation: every region containing the latter and closed under the relation contains it. | `ClosedUnder`, `WithinClosure` |
| influence closure (影响闭包) | The closure under being affected: what can affect a formation (to affect is to exchange with a condition it depends on). A formation's own feedback carries at most this. | `Flow.AffectedBy`, `Flow.WithinInfluence`, `Flow.Balances.uncoupled_beyond_influence` |
| reach closure (所及闭包) | The closure under affecting: what a formation's exchanges can affect link by link. It has a center and points forward. Offered for Ren's "the system that one is in" (an AI reading). | `Flow.Affects`, `Flow.WithinReach` |
| coupling closure (耦合闭包) | The closure under being affected through what couples in (another formation exchanges with a condition coupled into it, by threshold or by level). It contains the influence closure and is that closure where coupling is by threshold only. Where members respond only through their own couplings, what lies outside the closure does not show within it. | `Flow.WithinCoupling`, `Flow.alike_within_closed` |
| selection through the couplings (经耦合的选择) | A selection on histories of levels (keeping, rewarding, leaving offspring) in which histories alike through the selector's couplings are selected alike; scoring likewise. It reaches exactly what couples into the selector, and tells nothing apart outside the coupling closure. | `Web.SelectsThrough`, `Web.ScoresThrough`, `Web.selects_alike_iff`, `Flow.unselected_beyond_coupling` |
| selector (选择者) | The formation, or group of formations taken together, whose couplings fix a selection; taken as what actually couples into it. The more couples in by level, the more it can tell apart. | `Web.SelectsThrough`, `Web.SelectsThroughAll`, `Web.SelectsThrough.widen` |
| gap, of selection (缺口) | The candidate's reach closure less the selector's coupling closure. In `Clearing` the ending within it is not selected by a selection through the selector's couplings, and is selected through the dweller's couplings and through the couplings taken together. That the gap closes only through dependence, coupling by level or others' couplings taken together is an AI reading. | `Clearing.dweller_within_reach`, `Clearing.unselected`, `Clearing.selected_pooled`, `Diversion.favours` |
| exclusion (排除) | No history satisfying the claim is alike with the actual one through the formation's couplings up to some step. Relative to a model (a class of admissible histories), it excludes the model and the claim together. | `Web.Excludes`, `Web.ExcludesWithin`, `Web.excludesWithin_iff_conjunction` |
| course / prefix (经映射的历程／前段) | A sequence of values, for example a determined law's orbit read through a map; the prefix is the values before step t, latest first. | `prefixOf`, `back_prefix` |
| extrapolation by elimination (逐除外推) | Candidates are put in order, and the extrapolation moves to the next exactly where the one it holds misses. It never passes a candidate that accords, and every one it passes missed within the prefix. | `indexOn`, `byElimination`, `index_le_of_accords`, `missed_before` |
| lasso (套索) | A sequence that repeats periodically from some step on: exactly the course, through a map, of a world a list holds. On it the lasso extrapolation's misses are bounded, and that it comes to rest cannot be refuted. | `Lasso`, `lasso_of_held`, `lasso_realized`, `lasso_byElimination_bounded`, `rest_unrefuted` |
| diagonal course (对角序列) | A sequence that at every step answers the extrapolation's proposed value so that it misses. It is the course of a law that keeps the whole prefix, and so is open. | `diagonal`, `diagonal_misses`, `diagonal_open` |
| merged enumeration, at the meta level (合并枚举) | Classes of candidates merged along pairs into one enumeration. Learning over the classes is the same learning on it, with the bounds composed; every merge has its diagonal course. | `merged`, `merged_bound`, `merged_unrested` |
| subthreshold (阈下) | A condition coupled in by threshold only passes only whether the requirement is met. Where exchange follows what couples in, a subthreshold difference persists until the support of the two webs diverges. | `Web.alike_of_threshold`, `Flow.subthreshold_alike`, `Flow.difference_changes_after_divergence` |
| experiment (实验) | Varying one's own move where the configuration (as the experimenter reads it) returns. Relative to determinedness, the record fixes a move's outcome exactly where it was taken. | `Run.Fixes`, `Run.fixes_of_takes`, `Run.not_fixes_of_untaken`, `Run.fixes_on_return` |
| control (对照) | The model that what differs between two occasions is irrelevant to the change: the steps factor, through the reading, into a determined process. A move staggered against what was left out can exclude it; a synchronized move cannot. | `FactorsThrough`, `Run.generalizes`, `Confound` |
| description / carries a law (描述／承载规律) | A projection π of configurations is a description. It carries a law where some determined process on descriptions admits every step read through π, exactly where what is alike goes alike: configurations alike through π go by the same move to configurations alike through π. The image is the one candidate to check. Read as mechanism (an AI reading). | `GoAlike`, `Image`, `carries_law_iff`, `image_determined_iff` |
| 理 (modeling of laws) | Ren's reading (12 October 2026): the modeling of the laws of structure and change. Formally, modeling structure is a lumping and modeling change is a lumping through which the steps go alike, and the two are one concept (the AI's reading of the formal term). A model is tested only through what couples in. | `Lumped`, `goAlike_iff_lumped`, `carries_law_iff` |
| lumping / generalization (归并／推广) | A function π of inputs. Functions that go alike through π take one value at inputs alike through π. The data fix a value not yet tried exactly where it is lumped with some observed input; generalization is right exactly where the world goes alike through the lumping. | `Lumped`, `Determines`, `determines_lumped_iff`, `lumped_right_iff` |
| free, beyond the data (自由) | Every member's values on the data combine with every member's value at x. Where the class is free, the data fix only what its members share; the class of all functions is free at every untried point. | `Free`, `free_determines_iff`, `every_determines_nothing` |
| training (训练) | Selection through the data: functions agreeing on the data are selected alike. A fit is right beyond the data exactly where the class is fixed there by the data. Generalization comes from the class training can reach, not from the loss (the second half is the AI's reading). | `Training.Through`, `Training.loss_through`, `Training.fit_right_iff` |
| depends only on / re-absorbed (只依于／重新吸收) | The next configuration depends wholly on π; then differences of the starting point outside π vanish within one step. A rule holding the outcome is its instance with the rule as projection. | `DependsOnlyOn`, `DependsOnlyOn.reabsorbed`, `Law.reabsorbed`, `Law.perturbation_reabsorbed` |
| adaptive system (改变自身规则的系统) | The state goes on under the rule, and the rule itself goes on too: one determined process on the joint configuration. The state has a law of its own exactly where the rule makes no difference to it. | `adaptive`, `adaptive_state_law_iff`, `Run.steps_by_rule`, `Switching` |
| intervention / noise (干预／噪声) | In the standard model x′ = F(x, u, ε), an intervention is a move not taken, fixed by the model and not by the record; noise is a move not represented, and forgetting it the process stays determined exactly where the noise makes no difference. | `inputs`, `inputs_intervention_unfixed`, `inputs_model_fixes`, `forget_inputs_determined_iff` |
| enough (够) | A goal is met at a state the unfolding leaves unchanged. Under a determined law, seeking rests exactly where it reaches such a state. | `Law.rests_iff`, `Law.fixed_rests` |
| 无明 (not-seeing) | An AI reading: the not-seeing that locks seeking onto a goal that cannot be met at rest. Its boundary is enough. | `Becoming.Seeking` |
| hijacking (劫持) | An AI reading: a goal formed by a selection that rewards what cannot rest, and kept by the rules the winners write. Its share rises with offspring, and the outcome is held in the rule. | `share_rises`, `Law.perturbation_reabsorbed` |
| system (系统) | The closure under sharing a condition (both depend on it or exchange with it); a class. Where every formation takes part in one common condition, it is the whole. | `Flow.Shares`, `Flow.WithinSystem`, `Flow.system_whole_of_common` |
| what a part reaches (部分所及) | The part's members exchange with a condition a formation depends on; reaching before a step is exchanging, at an earlier step, with a condition it requires at that step. The comparison with and without the part is decided within what the part reaches. | `Flow.Reaches`, `Flow.ReachesBefore`, `Flow.Balances.comparison_within_reach` |
| responsive reach (回应所及) | The part's closure, between two flows, under being affected through what couples in: a member exchanges in one of the two flows with a condition coupled into another formation, which exchanges with one coupled into a third, and so on link by link. Dual to the coupling closure; where coupling is by threshold only and there is one flow, it is exactly the union of the members' reach closures. Where the rest responds through its couplings, the difference and the comparison are confined to it. | `Flow.WithinResponse`, `Flow.withinResponse_iff_coupling`, `Flow.withinResponse_thresholdOnly_iff`, `Flow.Balances.comparison_within_response` |
| responds through the couplings (经耦合而应) | Every formation outside the part through whose couplings the two flows are alike up to a step exchanges alike at that step in the two flows. A supplied premise, not derived from the dynamics; the rest held fixed is a case. | `Flow.RespondsThroughCoupling`, `Flow.respondsThroughCoupling_withhold` |
| law of exchange / reads what has stood (交换律／读已立者) | Reads off the history of levels how much each formation takes from and gives to each condition at each step. Reading what has stood: across step t it reads the history only up to step t. | `ExchangeLaw`, `ExchangeLaw.Nonanticipating`, `Flow.Follows` |
| sensitive (敏感) | A formation's exchange is sensitive to a condition: changing only that condition's level at some step changes what it exchanges. Where the law reads finitely many conditions under each history (every law reading what has stood does, where the conditions are finite), the law reads through what it is sensitive to: no coupling it reads through (together with what it depends on) leaves out what it is sensitive to, and sensitivity is the least coupling (in the strength of double negation: a coupling it reads through cannot fail to contain what it is sensitive to). With infinitely many conditions, whether this condition can be dropped turns on omniscience principles (given a decision of vanishing, the tail law is a counterexample), and with the conditions numbered every counterexample gives the weak limited principle of omniscience. | `ExchangeLaw.Sensitive`, `ExchangeLaw.ReadsFinitely.through_sensitive`, `ExchangeLaw.Nonanticipating.through_sensitive`, `ExchangeLaw.Through.couples_of_sensitive`, `tailLaw_no_least_coupling`, `wlpo_of_not_through_sensitive` |
| reads finitely many conditions (读有限多个条件) | At every step, for every formation and under every history, the law's exchange is fixed by the levels, up to that step, of some finite list of conditions, and the list may vary with the history (continuity). Such a law reads what has stood. With infinitely many conditions there may be no one list that suffices for every history. | `ExchangeLaw.ReadsWithin`, `ExchangeLaw.ReadsFinitely`, `ExchangeLaw.ReadsFinitely.nonanticipating`, `Pointer.no_list` |
| changed by no condition, a whole and no part (没有一个条件改变) | A number read off levels that is the same for levels differing in one condition only; exactly one that is the same for levels agreeing from some condition on, fixed by the tail alone. The law exchanging it is insensitive to every condition, and where the number differs there is no least coupling. | `ChangedByNoCondition`, `ChangedByNoCondition.eq_of_agree_from`, `wholeLaw`, `wholeLaw_no_least_coupling` |
| rule deciding false throughout (规则) | Decides, of every Boolean sequence, whether it is false throughout: the weak limited principle of omniscience as a rule, which gives `WLPO`. The whole with no part, and the counterexample to the least coupling, each exist exactly where it exists. A rule deciding whether there is a true term is exactly the decision of vanishing. | `changedByNoCondition_iff_rule`, `not_through_sensitive_iff_rule`, `WLPO.of_rule`, `vanishDecision_iff_lpoRule` |
| coupling strength (耦合强度) | A formation's exchange with a condition is coupled at a step to a condition's level with strength at most k: under two histories differing only in that level, the differences in what is taken and given add up to at most k times the difference of the level. A measure of sensitivity: for a law reading what has stood, strength zero is exactly insensitivity. Strengths add over levels changed one at a time and bound a part's share of a move. Not every law has a finite strength. | `ExchangeLaw.RespondsWithin`, `ExchangeLaw.insensitiveAt_iff_strength_zero`, `ExchangeLaw.exchangeGap_le_strengths`, `ExchangeLaw.exchangeGap_le_shares`, `Propagation.Square.not_respondsWithin` |
| carried amount (所携带的量) | The course the carrying law generates from the starting gap and the part's gap by the coupling strengths. Where the rest follows a law reading what has stood with those strengths, every gap is at most it, and it is attained: the most difference that can arrive. It is positive exactly along a route in time of positive strengths. | `ExchangeLaw.carrying`, `carried`, `gap_le_carried`, `most_difference_arrives`, `carried_pos_iff`, `Conveyed` |
| order, of the carried amount (阶) | What routes of at most j responses carry: what the strengths do to the next order. It rises with j to the carried amount and is exact up to step j. Where no loop lies upstream, a finite order is exact at every step. What a cut misses is bounded, and a self-loop attains the bound. | `carriedWithin`, `carriedFrom`, `carriedWithin_exact`, `carriedWithin_exact_of_no_loop`, `carried_le_carriedWithin_add` |
| loop, of couplings (回路) | A chain from a condition back to itself through couplings of positive strength; a loop upstream has a chain to the condition in question. What reaches a loop is multiplied, every time around, by at least one plus the product of its strengths. With the same formations and strengths at every step, only a loop upstream makes the carried difference exceed every polynomial. | `Couples`, `Coupled`, `OnLoop`, `Upstream`, `carried_runaway`, `polynomial_iff_no_loop` |
| what a cut loses (截断所失) | What of the carried amount is not carried when cut at order j: exactly what j + 1 responses in turn carry from the carried amount. It falls with the order, and is positive exactly where a gap is conveyed through j + 1 responses in time order. | `responses`, `carried_eq_carriedWithin_add`, `responses_antitone`, `carriedWithin_lt_iff` |
| chain of couplings in time order (按时间次序的耦合链) | A chain in which each coupling reads the level after the previous coupling has arrived. A finite order is exact from every starting gap exactly where such chains ending at the condition in question are bounded in length. | `CoupledIntoBy`, `exact_every_start_iff`, `finite_order_iff_bounded` |
| settled, of a schedule of couplings (安定) | Every coupling from some step on recurs. After settling, a finite order is exact exactly where no loop of the later couplings lies upstream. | `SettledFrom`, `strengthsFrom`, `CouplingRecurs`, `finite_order_iff_no_loop_from` |
| route back in time (沿时间往回的路线) | From a condition's level at some step back, one response per step to an earlier level, with the level held in between, or ending at a source. The transfer of its counting weights at horizon j + 1 is order j of the carried amount, and the carried amount is the sum of these routes. | `RoutePoint`, `routeWeight`, `routeSum`, `routeSum_eq_carriedWithin`, `carried_eq_courses` |
| closing, the formation of a loop (闭合) | The loops formed by a step are the loops of the couplings acting before it. A loop forms at a step exactly through a coupling acting at that step that closes a chain: it gives to a condition that already has a chain to the level it reads. | `strengthsBefore`, `onLoop_iff_formed`, `onLoop_succ_iff`, `forms_by_closing`, `responds_to_own_level` |
| what a law reads, a schedule (律所读) | For every formation outside the part, the law gives alike under histories agreeing on the marked conditions. An unread level is coupled with strength zero, and loops lie within what is read. | `ExchangeLaw.ReadsAt`, `restrictReads`, `ExchangeLaw.ReadsAt.respondsWithin_unread`, `couples_reads`, `onLoop_succ_reads` |
| break, of a stock read only below a threshold (断裂) | Where the stock stands at the threshold, the law reads only that it stands. Before the break the law is its standing law: it does not read the stock, no loop passes through it, and a raise of the stock is carried nowhere. The loop through the stock forms at the break. | `ExchangeLaw.ReadsBelow`, `ExchangeLaw.standing`, `break_carried_nowhere`, `ExchangeLaw.ReadsBelow.standing_no_loop`, `Thresholds.Draw` |
| reading into, each condition, a schedule (读入) | A formation's exchange with a condition is fixed only by the levels marked for that condition. Reading one schedule is the special case of reading alike for every condition; an unread level is coupled with strength zero. | `ExchangeLaw.ReadsInto`, `readCouplings`, `restrictInto`, `ExchangeLaw.ReadsAt.readsInto` |
| downstream, of a stock's difference (下游) | The conditions reachable from the stock by routes of reading formed by some step. Courses differing only in the stock are alike where they are not downstream; a stock coupled in by level is a route of length zero. | `Upstream`, `conveyed_upstream`, `apart_only_downstream`, `upstream_succ_iff`, `Observation.Measure` |
| return, around a loop (返回) | A chain of couplings in time order from a condition back to it: the first coupling reads the level from the starting step, and each later one reads the level after the previous one acted. With strengths natural numbers of at least one, every return at least doubles what arrives; with strengths below one the doubling does not follow. | `ChainInTime`, `Returns`, `carried_doubles_return`, `carried_returns`, `runaway_of_returns` |
| how often couplings act (耦合的频度) | The count a of steps at which some coupling acts. The most difference that can arrive is at most D·(1 + M)^a, attained by a self-loop; where 2^a has a polynomial bound, so does it, whatever the strengths. | `Frequency.count`, `Frequency.coupling`, `carried_le_count`, `carried_le_of_rare`, `Frequency.SelfLoop.polynomial_iff` |
| recurs or stops (复现或停止) | The dichotomy that, for every decided two-place family, either every row is true somewhere or some row is false throughout. It is exactly every schedule settling, and gives the limited principle of omniscience. | `RecursOrStops`, `StopsBy`, `settled_iff_recursOrStops`, `settled_every_iff`, `lpo_of_settled` |
| counterfactual, of a course (反事实) | The course the same law of exchange generates where the part acts otherwise. Fixed by the moves, it exists where feasible (never taking more than stands and is given). | `counterfactual_fixed`, `ExchangeLaw.course_balances_iff`, `counterfactual_is_course` |
| boundary of a self (自身的边界) | Along a relation, a region containing the formation and closed under the relation; the closure is the least, the whole the greatest. | `Boundary` |
| super-true / sub-true (超真／次真) | Holds however the boundary is drawn among the admissible ones / holds under some way of drawing it; taken from supervaluation (Fine 1975) and subvaluation (Hyde 1997). | `SuperTrue`, `SubTrue` |
| boundary-invariant / standpoint-invariant (对边界不变／对立场不变) | What holds under one way of drawing holds under every way, that is, it is determinate / what has significance from one standpoint has significance from every standpoint, the rule of standpoint neutrality. With the presupposition and covering, the two are equivalent. | `BoundaryInvariant`, `StandpointInvariant`, `boundaryInvariant_iff_standpointInvariant` |
| presupposition (预设) | From a standpoint, the continuation of everything within the boundary has significance. Super-true it is the continuation condition; sub-true along a relation it is significance on the closure. Read as the self's "belief" Ren spoke of (an AI reading). | `SignificantWithin`, `presupposition_scopes` |
| positive relevance read from significance (从意义读出的正面相关) | Realized living content whose continuation has significance from every standpoint. Under the condition it satisfies living significance and is the least relevance satisfying it, exactly the realized living content. Positive relevance is read from significance, so this is positive relevance. | `Frame.relevance`, `g0_of_unrestricted`, `relevance_least` |
| possibility reading (可能性读法) | Counts a formation where a continuation's levels admit it, active or not. Under the condition, extending by it is retaining and adding what has significance. It counts what can form, not what has formed; continuations are compared by it (an adopted reading, §6.12). | `Ranking.possible`, `Ranking.possible_extends_iff` |
| possibilities first (可能性优先) | Compare possibilities first, then the events a reading counts. A strict order satisfying C that ranks presence within renewal above absence, and also ranks rest that adds no possibility below activity; an added premise. | `Ranking.possibleFirst`, `Ranking.Rest.rest_ranked_below` |
| renewing over a region (在区域上更新) | Active, and the step keeps every dependence on every condition it takes part in within a region of conditions. Covering what it takes part in is renewal at that step; covering what it requires is keeping its own support. | `Web.RenewingOver`, `Web.RenewingOver.at`, `Web.RenewingOver.keeps_own` |
| boundary neutrality (边界中立) | The rule is limited by no formed boundary. | `Neutral` (contrast `Within`) |
| connected (连通) | Living claims are linked through formed relations. | `Connected` |
| contributory reason (支持理由) | A reason supporting a response; not an obligation or a ranking. | `Reason` |
| lifting rule (提升规则) | Lifts the comparison of outcomes to responses with several outcomes. | `LiftBetter` |
