### Retired Lean names (18 October 2026)

On 18 October 2026 the Lean names that carried "registration" and the learning vocabulary of a knower facing an external world were renamed by the vocabulary the [naming study](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/REGISTRATION-VOCABULARY-STUDY.md#8-applied-n5-18-october-2026) adopted (couples into, alike through, excludes, prefix, misses and accords, extrapolation by elimination). The statements and proofs are unchanged. The definitions are below; the theorem names follow them (for example `Web.RegistersAlike.trans` is `Web.AlikeThrough.trans`, `Learning.record_length` is `Learning.prefix_length`). The full table of 175 declarations is [RENAME-TABLE.json](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/development/n1-migration/RENAME-TABLE.json). Earlier studies, sources and receipts keep the old names as history. The prose words of this chapter were then changed by reading, not by this renaming (N6): registration closure is the coupling closure, selection through registration is selection through the couplings, responds through registration is responds through the couplings, contextual registration is claims in context, refutation (of a claim at a formation) is exclusion, course / record is course / prefix, learning by refutation is extrapolation by elimination, the learner given the bound or the world is the extrapolation given the bound or built from the process. 识 is kept for a formation's own course through the lumping its couplings make.

| Retired Lean name | Current Lean name |
|---|---|
| `Web.Registers` | `Web.CoupledInto` |
| `Web.RegistersAlike`, `…UpTo`, `…All` | `Web.AlikeThrough`, `…UpTo`, `…All` |
| `Web.Belief` | `Web.ClassThrough` |
| `Web.Refutes`, `…Within`, `…By` | `Web.Excludes`, `…Within`, `…By` |
| `Flow.RegisteredFrom`, `…Either`; `Flow.WithinRegistration` | `Flow.CoupledFrom`, `…Either`; `Flow.WithinCoupling` |
| `Flow.RespondsThroughRegistration`; `TimedResponse.RegistersAt` | `Flow.RespondsThroughCoupling`; `TimedResponse.CoupledIntoAt` |
| `supportOnly` | `thresholdOnly` |
| `Registers` (Likeness) | `RespectsClaims` |
| `Law.registered` | `Law.frameBy` |
| `Learning.record`; `recordThen` | `Learning.prefixOf`; `prefixThen` |
| `Learning.Refuted`, `Learning.RefutedAt`; `Learning.Holds` | `Learning.Misses`, `Learning.MissesAt`; `Learning.Accords` |
| `Learning.learner` | `Learning.byElimination` |
| `Learnable.withWorld`, `…At`; `Parting.worldLearner` | `Learnable.fromLaw`, `…At`; `Parting.fromProcess` |
| `Viability.Answering.registers`; `Viability.Matching.registered`, `…blind` | `Viability.Answering.keeps`; `Viability.Matching.keepsBit`, `…keepsNothing` |

### Crosswalk to the corpus glossary

The root [GLOSSARY.md](../../../GLOSSARY.md) is the vocabulary of the whole corpus, with provenance marks and dated first uses; it has no entries for the spine's formal terms. The terms below appear in both. Each row gives the corpus sense in brief and how it stands to the terms above. The corpus entry remains the source for its sense and provenance, and the relations stated here are the AI's reading, not a ruling by Ren. Where the two senses differ, they are not to be merged.

| Term (Chinese name) | Corpus sense, and its relation to this chapter | Lean anchor |
|---|---|---|
| 生生不息 (shēngshēng bùxī) | Ren's reading of the inherited phrase: the source of further generation is unexhausted and the way of transformation unsealed; not the persistence of any form, nor rising complexity or growth. The corpus breaks it into 生 (a possible structure entering actuality), 生生 (that entry also preserving, restoring or creating the next round's conditions and generators) and 不息 (the pattern keeps being realized again through metabolism, death and transmission). This chapter's 生生 is the object of the ethical direction. The chapter reads 不息 as arising that recurs through renewal and 生生不息 as 生生 going on at the whole with more still able to form, so return into copies of existing forms falls short (D5, taken by Ren on 18 October 2026). The formal `Unceasing` ("unceasing arising") is much weaker: some formation arises at arbitrarily late steps. | none; compare `Unceasing` |
| 成生 / 绝生 (generative / degenerative) | Ren's act-level test: does an act preserve, restore or extend the affected party's capacity to keep perceiving, acting, relating, correcting and generating, or close it. Ren corrected "destructive" to "degenerative" on 28 April 2026; the correction is to be kept. This chapter's "generative / degenerative" row formalizes the part-level widening and narrowing of what can form (D2). | `Web.Widens`, `Clearing.narrows` |
| 开 (open) | Ren's process-state with four conditions holding together (未, 可, 应, 转: an unexhausted domain remains, becoming is still reachable, it can still respond, it can still revise and transform); explicitly not a quantity. This chapter's "openness" is narrower and different: exchange beyond every finite family. The two must not be identified. | none; compare `World.arising_opens` |
| 闭 / 成闭 (closure / achieved closure) | Ren's typed closure, in which closure as such is not evil; 成闭 is closing that does not reopen, which the corpus names bad. The nearest formal terms here are lock-in and being drawn in (what is drawn in never comes back). They state the dynamics only and carry no valuation. | none; compare `Law.DrawnInto.never_back`, `Run.locked_in` |
| possibility space (可能性空间) | Ren's: not the number of options but whether genuinely different futures remain materially reachable. Here the possibility reading counts what can form next, and widening compares paths of levels by inclusion. Ren confirmed reading possibility as what can form next on 10 October 2026 (row "generative / degenerative"). | `Ranking.possible`, `Web.Widens` |
| 理 (lǐ) | In the corpus's 化/理 dual, the invariant of a trajectory, both pattern and type. Ren's reading of 12 October 2026, used here, is the modeling of the laws of structure and change (row 理 above). The two senses differ; the corpus also holds 道含理，理不含道 (the Way contains pattern, pattern does not contain the Way). | `Lumped`, `goAlike_iff_lumped` |
| 道 (dào) | Inherited, used in the corpus in three registers that are not to be merged: compass, formal predicate, and algebra. This chapter defines no formal 道. | — |
| 善 / 恶 (the good / evil) | Ren's: the good does not lie in maximizing anything but in what one does not close; evil is the knowing conversion of a shared condition into capture, closure that does not reopen. This chapter states no formal predicate for either; its ethical direction is living significance under the condition of §6.15 (§6, §7). | — |
| 智 (zhì) | Ren's: the capacity to stay in contact with reality; capability and optimization power are not intelligence in this sense. This chapter's open target "the core as the root of wisdom" (§8.1) concerns wisdom, which is related but not the same. | — |
| point of enough | Ren's ruling that growth is bounded by need: "the problem is the mind not the approach". The nearest formal term is enough: a goal met at a state the unfolding leaves unchanged, at which seeking rests. | `Law.rests_iff` |
| meta-attractor (元吸引子) | The corpus's self-reinforcing pattern that gains power over the rule by which states are selected. Its valence varies across texts, as the corpus entry records. The nearest formal terms here are hijacking (a goal kept by the rules the winners write), re-absorption under a rule, and runaway. | `share_rises`, `Law.perturbation_reabsorbed` |
| relational self (关系之我) | Ren's: a self is what a knot of relations is doing at a place, an action center formed by relations gathering. Here a center is a formation in the dynamics, and the boundary of a self is drawn along a relation, from its closure to the whole. | `Boundary`, `own` |
| 位 / 际 (locus / relation) | The structural dual of the 間 project: a locus is exhausted by its relations. Here formation and condition are two roles of one relatum, and relations are read at the level of readings. | `Web.roles`, `Web.relations` |
| Yi (一) | Ren's one primitive, of which subject, object, container and agent are roles read off context facts. The nearest term here is roles: formation and condition as two roles of one relatum. | `Web.roles` |
| lineage (相续) | In the context runtime, the parent-child path through the moment graph in which identity lives. This chapter's succession and lineage (接续) join a formation that ends with one that begins; the senses are related but not the same. | `Lineage`, `Succession` |
| membrane (膜) | Ren's self-managed boundary that admits, exchanges and can be corrected through, unlike a wall. The nearest formal terms are a cut (no formed relation leaves a region) and being fed from beyond. | `Cut`, `FedFromBeyond` |

The corpus glossary records that three closure vocabularies (成闭/护闭/元闭/伪开, 成生之限/绝生之限, and English "close / keep open") circulate without a crosswalk. This table does not resolve that.

## 11. Where the earlier chapters stand

The chapters are records of argument, evidence and criticism. Each is kept at its original scope and is not rewritten because of this chapter, which replaces only their role as entry points. The [reading catalogue](../results/README.md) lists every chapter with its argument, significance, scope and next question. The statuses below follow restructure batch H4 (17 October 2026; receipt `_process/architecture/moves/H4.json`): 35 files stayed in `chapters/`, and the 104 other chapters and the 5 October release bundle `verification/` moved unchanged to [`../history/`](../history/). The [chapter index](README.md) lists every chapter and where each moved chapter now is.

| Kind | Chapter | Status |
|---|---|---|
| Canonical | [THE-THEORY-OF-BECOMING](THE-THEORY-OF-BECOMING.md) | This chapter. |
| Method | [NONDUAL-METHOD](NONDUAL-METHOD.md) | Read before all foundation work. |
| Register | [CLAIMS](CLAIMS.md) | Generated register stub; the [result register](../results/RESULT-REGISTER.md) and its records govern. |
